---

> Cikakken bayani na kowane environment variable da OmniRoute ke ganewa.
> Don samfurin farawa cikin sauri, duba [`.env.example`](../../.env.example).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Dole ne kowane variable da aka rubuta a nan ya bayyana a cikin `.env.example`, kuma
> kowane variable da ke cikin `.env.example` dole ne ya bayyana a nan. `npm run check:env-doc-sync`
> yana tabbatar da wannan yayin commit da kuma a cikin CI. Don tsallake wani variable da gangan, ƙara shi zuwa
> allowlist da ke cikin `scripts/check/check-env-doc-sync.mjs`.

## 1. Sirrukan da Ake Buƙata

Sirrin da ba a saita ba kuma aka yi wa alamar **Eh** ana samar da su ta atomatik a farkon gudanarwa (`JWT_SECRET`, `API_KEY_SECRET`) sannan a adana su a `server.env` cikin kundin bayanai — saita su kai tsaye idan wannan kundin na wucin gadi ne. Banda wannan shi ne `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET`: ba a adana shi a `server.env` — wuraren shigar sabar suna ƙirƙirar ƙima ta wucin gadi ga kowace farawa wadda gada da ke cikin tsarin kaɗai za ta iya amfani da ita, saboda haka abokan hulɗar gada na waje suna buƙatar a saita shi kai tsaye.

| Sauyawa                      | Ana Buƙata               | Tsoho                           | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------ | ------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `JWT_SECRET`                 | **Eh**                   | _(babu)_                        | `src/lib/auth`                                            | Yana sanya hannu/tabbatar da duk kukis na zaman dashboard (JWT). Samar da shi da `openssl rand -base64 48`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `API_KEY_SECRET`             | **Eh**                   | _(ana ƙirƙira ta atomatik)_     | `src/shared/utils/apiKey.ts`                              | Sirri da ake amfani da shi wajen ƙirƙira da tabbatar da checksum na maɓallan API da aka ɗaure da na'ura. Idan ba a saita shi ba, tsarin farawa na yau da kullum yana samar da shi kuma yana adana shi a `server.env` idan ana iya rubuta wa fayil ɗin. Saita shi kai tsaye idan kundin bayanai na wucin gadi ne. Ƙirƙire shi da `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `INITIAL_PASSWORD`           | A'a                      | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `src/lib/auth/managementPassword.ts`                      | Yana saita kalmar sirrin dashboard yayin farawa muddin ba a riga an adana wata kalmar sirri ba; za a yi watsi da shi da zarar an adana kalmar sirri. Shigarwar npm da ta tushe suna kwafe `.env.example` zuwa `.env`, saboda haka suna farawa da ƙimar wucin gadi `CHANGEME` da kowa ya sani: maye gurbinta kafin farawa na farko, ko saita `INITIAL_PASSWORD=` (fanko) a cikin wannan `.env` don ƙirƙirar kalmar sirri a cikin jagoran fara amfani a ziyarar farko, kamar yadda ƙimar da ba a saita ba (tsohuwar ƙimar hoton Docker) take yi. Canja kalmar sirri da aka adana a Dashboard → Settings → Security, ko gudanar da `omniroute reset-password` (`node bin/reset-password.mjs` a cikin kwafin tushe) tare da saita `DATA_DIR` zuwa kundin bayanan sabar. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET` | **Eh** (yanayin samarwa) | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `src/app/api/internal/codex-responses-ws/route.ts`        | Sirrin da aka raba don gadar WebSocket ta Codex Responses ta ciki. Yana tantance buƙatun gada tsakanin mai isar da WS na Electron/mazuruftan yanar gizo da OmniRoute. ⚠️ **ANA BUƘATARSA a yanayin samarwa — idan ba a saita shi ba, za a ƙi duk buƙatun gadar WS.** Samar da shi da `openssl rand -base64 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SW_BUILD_ID`      | A'a                      | _(git SHA)_                     | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Bayyanannen id na karya ma'ajiyar cache na service-worker don murfin PWA (#11779); shi ne na farko a jerin tantancewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SOURCE_VERSION`             | A'a                      | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Na biyu a jerin — masu gina PaaS (misali, irin na Heroku) ne ke saita shi a matsayin commit ɗin da aka tura.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SW_BUILD_ID`    | A'a                      | _(an samo)_                     | `src/shared/components/PwaRegister.tsx`                   | Ƙimar jama'a ta lokacin ginawa da abokin ciniki ke amfani da ita wajen rajistar `/sw.js?v=…`; ana samo ta daga biyun da ke sama, sannan git SHA.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_STAMP_TOKEN` | A'a (ta atomatik)        | _(ta atomatik a kowace farawa)_ | `src/server/authz/policies/management.ts`                 | Sirrin kowane tsari da ke tabbatar da cewa amintacciyar alamar peer-IP ta fito ne daga sabar HTTP ta OmniRoute (`scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`). Middleware na authz yana amincewa da kasancewar buƙata a gida (tacewar loopback/LAN ga hanyoyin LOCAL_ONLY) ne kawai idan alamar tana ɗauke da wannan token. Ana samar da shi ta atomatik a kowace farawa — bar shi ba a saita ba; saita shi tabbatacce kawai ga tsarin matakai da yawa waɗanda dole ne su raba alamar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUSTED_PROXIES`  | A'a                      | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`                              | Jerin IP ko zangon CIDR na reverse proxies da aka raba da waƙafi, waɗanda jerin yarda/ƙin IP zai iya amincewa da `X-Forwarded-For` / `X-Real-IP` nasu, ban da adiresoshin loopback, na cibiyar sadarwa mai zaman kanta da iyakokin Cloudflare, waɗanda koyaushe ake amincewa da su. Ana buƙatar wannan ne kawai ga proxy da ke kan wani adireshin jama'a dabam; in ba haka ba adireshin proxy ɗin kansa ne matatar za ta tantance. Takaita saurin kowane abokin ciniki na relay (`/api/v1/relay/chat/completions`) yana amfani da wannan adireshin abokin ciniki da aka tantance, ba kan headers na forwarding kaɗai ba.                                                                                                                                            |

### Umarnin Samarwa

```bash
# Samar da dukkan sirruka huɗu a lokaci guda:
echo "JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)"
echo "API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)"
echo "INITIAL_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 16)"
echo "OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
```

> [!CAUTION]
> Kada a taɓa commit na fayilolin `.env` masu ɗauke da sirruka na ainihi zuwa tsarin sarrafa sigogi. `.gitignore` ya riga ya ware `.env`, amma tabbatar kafin turawa.

---

## 2. Ma’ajiya & Rumbun Bayanai

OmniRoute yana amfani da **SQLite** (ta hanyar `better-sqlite3`) domin duk wani adana bayanai na dindindin. Waɗannan canje-canje suna sarrafa wurin bayanai, ɓoyewa, da zagayowar rayuwarsu.

| Canji                                      | Tsoho                         | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                       | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `DATA_DIR`                                 | `~/.omniroute/`               | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Tushen kundin adireshi na SQLite DB, kwafin ajiya, da fayilolin bayanai. Sauya shi domin kundin Docker ko hanyoyi na musamman.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_DEFAULT_DATA_DIR`         | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/dataPaths.ts`                                | Hanyar kaucewa kariyar DATA_DIR ta gwaji/eval (#10428). Gwaje-gwaje da binciken eval/print na Node (`-e`/`--eval`/`-p`/`--print`, har da siffofin `--eval=`/`--print=`) waɗanda ba su da `DATA_DIR` ana karkatar da su zuwa kundin wucin gadi da za a yar domin kada su buɗe ainihin rumbun bayanan mai gudanarwa; saita zuwa `1` domin sake amincewa da amfani da ainihin kundin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_SHA`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/monitoring/buildSha.ts`                      | Git SHA na artifact da ke gudana. Ana hatimce shi ta `npm run build:release`; ana iya saka shi cikin kwantena da ake turawa ba tare da alamar `dist/BUILD_SHA` ba. Ana nuna shi a matsayin `system.buildSha` a `/api/monitoring/health`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELEASE_REF`                    | `origin/main`                 | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Ref ɗin da ƙofar tabbatar da asalin pack-artifact ke kwatanta build SHA da shi (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CANARY_BUILD`             | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Saita zuwa `1` domin ba da damar shirya build wanda SHA ɗinsa ba ya kan layin fitarwa, tare da rubuta shi a matsayin canary da aka yi da gangan maimakon ƙin amincewa a ƙofar (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_API_KEY`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `scripts/ops/deploy-canary.mjs`                       | Maɓallin API na binciken smoke na tura canary, wanda ake aikawa a matsayin `Authorization: Bearer` zuwa `/v1/chat/completions`. Rubutun tura tsarin kaɗai ke amfani da shi (#10429), uwar garken ba ta taɓa amfani da shi. Ba shi da alaƙa da canje-canjen `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_*` na tsarin smoke na CLI da ake kunna ta hanyar zaɓi (`RUN_CLI_SMOKE=1`, `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_BASE_URL/MODEL/API_KEY_ENV/TARGETS/TIMEOUT_MS` a cikin `tests/integration/upstream-cli-smoke.int.test.ts`) — duba [Haɗin CLI → Binciken smoke na ainihi](../guides/CLI-INTEGRATIONS.md).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILDING`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/buildPhase.ts`                               | Alamar matakin build (#10060): `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs` ne ke saita ta zuwa `1`, kuma duk wani build worker da aka ƙaddamar yana gadonta domin matakin DB ya dawo da stub mara aiki maimakon loda native addon na better-sqlite3 (wanda ke katse worker lokacin fita). Kada a taɓa saita ta ga uwar garken da ke gudana.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_NATIVE_DEP_CHECK`          | `0`                           | `scripts/check/check-native-deps.mjs`                 | Saita zuwa `1` domin tsallake binciken prebuild na native dependency na zaɓi ga bishiyoyin vendored na musamman. Wannan ba ya sa dependency da ya ɓace ya zama abin build; yi amfani da shi ne kawai idan ana samar da native dependencies ta wata hanya ta daban.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `open-sse/executors/promptql/threadSticky.ts`         | **Laƙabin fallback** na `DATA_DIR`, wanda ake dubawa kawai idan ba a saita `DATA_DIR` ba. Ana amfani da shi wajen gano ma’ajiyar zaman thread-sticky ta PromptQL executor da ke kan faifai (`<dir>/promptql-thread-sessions.json`); idan babu ɗaya daga cikin canje-canjen da aka saita, ma’ajiyar za ta kasance cikin ƙwaƙwalwa kawai (ba za a adana ta bayan sake farawa ba).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR`                    | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/plugins/scanner.ts`                          | Kundin da **runtime plugin scanner** ke karantawa — kuma tushen da plugin manager ke girkawa a ciki — yana maye gurbin tsohon wurin da ake samo daga home (#11827). Nuna shi zuwa bishiyar plugin da aka bind-mount a Docker/K8s maimakon matsar da HOME kawai domin sauya wurin hanyar dubawa (HOME yana sarrafa duk sauran halayen da suka danganci home ma). Rashin saiti = `~/.omniroute/plugins`, ko `/tmp/.omniroute/plugins` idan tsarin bai fitar da wani home kwata-kwata ba — wannan canjin yana kawar da rashin gano plugin ba tare da sanarwa ba. Ana rubuta kundin da aka warware sau ɗaya lokacin farawa a matsayin `scanner.dir_resolved`, tare da shigarwar da ta yi nasara. Na gefen uwar garken kawai: plugin na umarnin CLI suna ci gaba da amfani da nasu `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH` (sashe na 9). |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`                   | _(babu komai = a kashe)_      | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | Maɓallin AES don cikakken ɓoyewar bayanan SQLite yayin da suke a ma'aji. Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY_VERSION`           | `v1`                          | `scripts/build/bootstrap-env.mjs`, `electron/main.js` | Alamar sigar maɓallin ɓoyewa. Ƙara ta yayin sauya maɓalli domin tallafa wa warware ɓoyewar tsofaffin kwafin ajiya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`               | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/backup.ts`                                | Idan `true`, yana tsallake kwafin ajiyar fayil ɗin SQLite na yau da kullum/kafin rubutu (adana/share farashin models.dev, rubuce-rubucen saituna). Kwafin ajiya na hannu da na kafin maidowa har yanzu suna aiki. **Ba ya** kashe tilas ɗin hoton kariya mai ɗorewa na mai gudanar da ƙaura ko kariyar ƙaura mai yawa ga DB mai ɗorewa da ke akwai. Ana taƙaita kwafin ajiyar da ba na hannu ba zuwa mafi yawa sau ɗaya cikin mintuna 60. **Settings → Storage** na Dashboard na iya kashe kwafin ajiya na yau da kullum da kansa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_SLOW_MS`                 | _(ba a saita ba/a kashe)_     | `src/lib/db/adapters/slowDiagnostics.ts`              | Millisekan; ƙimomin da suke ƙasa da 1 ko waɗanda ba lambobi ba suna ci gaba da kasancewa a kashe; yana yi gargaɗi mafi yawa sau ɗaya cikin 10s; layin log yana ɗauke da driver, operation, da elapsedMs kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CRYPT_KEY`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Tsohon suna na madadin** don `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`. Ana karɓarsa a matsayin mafita ta baya idan babban variable ɗin ba ya nan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_BASE64`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Tsohon suna na madadin** (nau'in da aka yi wa encoding na Base64) wanda ake karɓa a matsayin mafita ta baya. Ana yi masa decoding ta atomatik kafin amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_DB_HEALTHCHECK_INTERVAL_MS`     | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Sauya tazarar binciken lafiyar SQLite na lokaci-lokaci (ms). Idan ba a saita ba, ana samo tsoffin ƙimomi daga `NODE_ENV`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`       | _(an cire)_                   | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | **An cire.** `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` mai gudana lokaci-lokaci zai iya lalata haɗin taswirar wal-index da ake rabawa kuma ya rushe tsarin da SIGBUS (#13973), saboda haka babu scheduler ɗin kuma. Variable ɗin ba ya aiki: ƙima mai kyau tana rubuta gargaɗin daina amfani sau ɗaya, yayin da `0` ko rashin saitawa ba ya fitar da komai. Ana kula da WAL ta hanyar PASSIVE checkpoints (a ƙasa), kuma shutdown checkpoint ne ke rage shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_PASSIVE_INTERVAL_MS`        | `300000` (5m)                 | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Sauya tazarar `wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE)` mai yawan gudana (ms). Yana rage pending WAL frames domin checkpoints su kasance masu sauri kuma fayil ɗin WAL ya ci gaba da kasancewa cikin iyaka tsakanin ragewar lokacin shutdown. `0` yana kashe shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_GUARD_MAX_MB`               | `256`                         | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Lokacin da PASSIVE tick ya gano cewa fayil ɗin WAL ya wuce wannan girman, gudanar da `wal_checkpoint(RESTART)` domin WAL ya fara daga farko ba tare da sake rubuta wal-index da aka yi mapping ba. An cire live truncate-mode checkpoints (duba layin `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Saita zuwa `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` domin fita daga process bayan matsanancin matsin albarkatu ya ci gaba har tsawon `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS`, wanda zai bai wa supervisor (systemd `Restart=always`, manufofin sake farawa na Docker) damar dawo da process mai tsabta maimakon ci gaba da bayar da 503s ba tare da iyaka ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS` | `120000` (2m)                 | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Tsawon lokacin da matsanancin matsin lamba dole ne ya ci gaba kafin fitowar sake farawa da kai ta fara aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_PSI_DISABLED`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressurePolicy.ts`            | Saita zuwa `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` domin yin watsi da `/proc/pressure/memory` (`psi_some` / `psi_full`) na duk host lokacin da maƙwabci mai yawan hayaniya ya jawo 503s alhali wannan process yana lafiya (#13124). Kariyoyin cgroup da V8 heap suna ci gaba da aiki. PSI kuma zai daina ƙidayuwa a matsayin shaidar kernel throttle ga `cgroup_high`: jimillar cgroup da ta haura `memory.high` daga nan za ta zama critical ne kawai idan `high`/`max` na `memory.events` ya ƙaru. Idan babu `memory.events`, ana riƙe raw ratio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLJS_WASM_PATH`                | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_        | `src/lib/db/adapters/sqljsAdapter.ts`                 | Takamaiman path (cikakke ko dangane da cwd) zuwa `sql-wasm.wasm` yayin amfani da WASM fallback adapter na `sql.js`. Ana gano shi ta atomatik ta hanyar package dependencies da candidate layouts idan ba a saita ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`           | `30`                          | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Yawan kwanakin da ake ajiye wuraren dubawa, fayilolin shigarwa/fitarwa/kuskure da aka ambata, da kuma layin aikin Batch API da ya kai ƙarshensa (an kammala/an gaza/an soke/ya ƙare) kafin aikin sharewa na atomatik ya goge su. Yana fara aiki ne kawai bayan an kunna `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`; ya yi daidai da wa'adin riƙe fitarwar Batch API na OpenAI. Ba ya shafar hanyar `DELETE /api/v1/batches/delete-completed` da mai gudanarwa ke kunnawa, wadda bisa tsari take ci gaba da aiki ba tare da sharadin shekaru ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`      | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Idan `true`, a bar aikin sharewa na atomatik ya goge ayyukan Batch API da suka kai ƙarshensu (da wuraren dubawarsu) bayan `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`, sannan ya share abun cikin BLOB na fayilolin da aka ɗora bayan nasu `expires_at`. A kashe yake a tsoho: duk wata shigarwa da ke akwai za ta ci gaba da riƙe wannan bayanin daidai kamar da har sai mai gudanarwa ya zaɓi shiga. Haka kuma tuta ce ta fasali da za a iya gyarawa daga dashboard — duba `docs/reference/FEATURE_FLAGS.md` → Lokacin aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`            | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`, `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`     | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake duba lafiyar DB gaba ɗaya yayin farawa. Yana da amfani ga ayyuka masu gajeren rai da gwaje-gwajen haɗawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORCE_DB_HEALTHCHECK`           | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Saita zuwa `1` don tilasta kunna madaukin duba lafiyar DB, ko da a yanayin da galibi za a tsallake shi (misali, ayyuka masu gajeren rai).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_POSTINSTALL`               | `0`                           | `scripts/postinstall.mjs`                             | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake dumama yanayin aiki na asali yayin `npm install`. Yana da amfani a shigarwar CI/marasa fuskar mai amfani inda an riga an gina sqlite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_MIGRATIONS_DIR`                 | _(gano ta atomatik)_          | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Sauya kundin adireshin da mai gudanar da ƙaura ke bincikawa. Yana da amfani lokacin jigilar ƙaurorin da aka haɗa cikin ginshiƙai na musamman.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_EXTRA_MIGRATIONS_DIRS`          | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner/extraDirs.ts`             | Ƙarin kundin adireshin ƙaura a matsayin shigarwar `namespace=dir` waɗanda alamar raba hanyar dandali ta raba (misali `ee=/opt/app/enterprise/db/migrations`). Ana rubuta fayilolin da aka samu a wurin a matsayin `<namespace>-<number>`, don haka rarrabawar da ke jigilar nata ƙaurorin ba za ta taɓa cin karo da guraben lambobi na tushen sama ba. Shigarwa mara inganci, namespace mara inganci, ko kundin adireshi da babu shi zai jawo kuskure yayin farawa maimakon a tsallake tsarin bayanai cikin shiru.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_PENDING_MIGRATIONS`         | `50`                          | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Iyakar kariya ga tarin ƙaurorin da ke jira (#3416). Farawa zai tsaya idan fiye da wannan adadin ƙaurori ne ke jira a DB da ke akwai (don kariya daga teburin sa ido da aka goge). Ƙara shi don dawo da tsohon madadin bayanai; saita zuwa `0` don kashe dubawar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTALL_UPGRADE_WORKDIR`        | _(`<repo>/.install-upgrade`)_ | `scripts/check/check-install-upgrade.mjs`             | Kundin aikin ƙofar sakin `check:install-upgrade`. Yana buƙatar kusan 12 GB (bishiyoyin shigarwa biyu masu kusan ~3 GB kowanne tare da tarball), don haka kada ya yi aiki a kan ƙaramin tmpfs — a kan runner mai ɗaukar nauyin kansa `/tmp` tmpfs ne na 12 GB mai goyon bayan RAM, kuma ƙofar ta cika shi har ta yanke kunshin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS`        | _(tsoho a cikin lamba)_       | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Tazarar zubarwa (ms) ga mai rubuta kashe kuɗi/farashi a rukuni. Ƙananan ƙimomi suna rage haɗa rubuce-rubuce; manyan ƙimomi suna rage takaddamar DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_MAX_BUFFER_SIZE`          | _(tsoho a cikin lamba)_       | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Matsakaicin adadin shigarwar kashe kuɗi da za a riƙe a ma'aji kafin zubarwa ta tilas. Ƙara shi a turawa masu QPS mai yawa; rage shi idan iyakantaccen ƙwaƙwalwa ya fi muhimmanci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_FETCH_DEBUG`              | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                        | Saita zuwa `"true"` don fitar da rajistan gyara matsala na `[ProxyFetch]` a hanyar relay ta Vercel. A kashe yake a tsoho don guje wa fallasa alamun tsara hanya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_BINARY_PATH`         | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/sync.ts`        | Cikakkiyar hanyar binary na proxy core na cikin gida (fayil mai suna `sing-box`) wanda ke duba saitin core da aka samar kafin ya maye gurbin fayil ɗin da aka karɓa. Idan ba a saita ba, yana rubuta `<path>.generated` ne kawai kusa da fayil ɗin da aka karɓa. Ga muhallin host kawai: ba a taɓa adana shi a cikin ma'ajin bayanai ko karɓarsa ta API na gudanarwa ba, saboda ana aiwatar da binary ɗin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_RELOAD_COMMAND`      | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/reload.ts`      | Yadda proxy core na cikin gida ke sake lodawa bayan maye gurbin saiti da aka tabbatar: jerin argument na JSON da ake gudanarwa ba tare da shell ba, ko kuma daidai `external` lokacin da mai sa ido na waje ke amfani da fayil ɗin. Ba a taɓa adana shi a cikin ma'ajin bayanai ko fallasa shi ta API ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                    | `false`                       | `src/lib/proxyLogger.ts`                              | Saita zuwa `"true"` ko `"1"` don haɗa IP na abokin ciniki/mafitar sadarwa da gajeren farkon asusu a cikin cikakken layin rajistar tsari na `[ProxyEgress]`. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa don kada rajistar tsarin ta fallasa IP ko gajeren farkon asusun.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `bin/cli/commands/quota.mjs`                          | Saita zuwa `1` don buga bayanan binciken lokacin kowace buƙata (`[omniroute] GET <path> completed in Nms`) daga umarnin ƙididdigar CLI zuwa stderr.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_PATH`               | _(ta atomatik)_               | `scripts/dev/healthcheck.mjs`                         | Takamaiman hanya da gwajin lafiyar kwantena ke bincikawa. Idan ba a saita ba, gwajin zai samo ta daga `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`; saita ta zai sake kunna amfani da mashigar sa-ido mai zurfi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG_COMPLETION`               | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `bin/cli/commands/completion.mjs`                     | Saita zuwa kowace ƙima marar fanko don fitar da bayanan binciken `[omniroute completion]` daga hanyoyin ma'ajiyar kammala-umarni ta shell na CLI (karantawa/sabuntawa/rubutawa). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — waɗannan ma'ajiyoyi suna gaza ba tare da sanarwa ba domin ma'aji da ya ɓace ko ya lalace kada ya taɓa hana kammalawa da maɓallin tab aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `BATCH_RETRY_DURATION_MS`                  | `86400000` (awa 24)           | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Matsakaicin tsawon lokacin sake gwadawa ga kowane abu na rukuni (ms). Abubuwan da suka wuce wannan lokaci za a yi musu alamar gazawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_BASE_MS`                    | `5000`                        | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Jinkirin asali (ms) don ƙarin jinkiri na exponential a sake gwada abubuwan rukuni.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_MAX_MS`                     | `3600000` (awa 1)             | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Iyakar (ms) ƙarin jinkiri na exponential tsakanin sake gwada abubuwan rukuni.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `BATCH_MAX_CONCURRENT`                     | `1`                           | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Matsakaicin adadin rukunan da ake sarrafawa a lokaci guda. Ƙara shi don haɓaka yawan aiki; bar shi ƙasa don kauce wa guguwar wuce iyakar buƙatu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Kafin a sauya rumbun bayanai mai ɗorewa da yake akwai, mai gudanar da ƙaura yana wallafa cikakken,
> kwafin ajiya mai adireshi bisa abun ciki a ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR/db_backups/`. Wallafawa na buƙatar tsarin fayil
> da ke goyon bayan hard links masu kasancewa a tsarin fayil ɗaya waɗanda ba sa sake rubutawa, tare da daidaitawar fayil mai ɗorewa. Masu masaukin POSIX kuma
> suna buƙatar daidaitawar kundin adireshi; a Windows, Node na iya ƙin karɓar hannayen kundin adireshi, saboda haka OmniRoute yana kammala rubuta
> fayil ɗin da aka wallafa sannan ya ɗauki daidaitawar shigarwar kundin adireshi a matsayin ƙoƙarin iyawa.
> Idan `DATA_DIR` da aka ɗora ba zai iya samar da waɗannan tabbacin ba, farawa zai gaza cikin yanayin kariya kafin aiwatar da
> ƙaura. Matsar da `DATA_DIR` zuwa volume mai waɗannan fasalolin; kada a yi amfani da
> `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP` don kauce wa tsaron ƙaura.

### Yanayin amfani

| Yanayin amfani                  | Saitin                                                                           |
| ------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Haɓakawa a cikin na'ura**     | Bar duk tsoffin saituna. DB yana a `~/.omniroute/omniroute.db`.                  |
| **Docker**                      | `DATA_DIR=/data` + ɗora volume a `/data`.                                        |
| **An ɓoye bayanai yayin ajiya** | Saita `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY` + adana kwafin maɓallin! Rasa shi = rasa bayanai. |
| **CI/Gwaji**                    | `DATA_DIR=/tmp/omniroute-test` — na wucin gadi ne, ba a buƙatar ɓoyewa.          |

---

## 3. Hanyar Sadarwa & Tashoshin Haɗi

| Maɓalli                                     | Tsoho                                                    | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PORT`                                      | `20128`                                                  | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Babban tashar haɗi don UI na Dashboard da maƙararan API (yanayin tashar haɗi guda ɗaya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`                       | _(babu komai = tushe)_                                   | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/docker/ensure-docker-base-path.mjs`          | Ƙaramin hanyar URL don samar da OmniRoute a bayan reverse proxy (yana saita `basePath` na Next.js; tura masu amfani na tantancewa suna la'akari da basePath). Misali `/omniroute`. A Docker ana haɗa ƙimar yayin `docker build` (`ARG OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`); hotunan tushe da aka riga aka gina za su iya amfani da wata ƙimar lokacin aiki sau ɗaya a farkon container kafin Next.js ya fara. Saita `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` zuwa asalin jama'a wanda ya haɗa da wannan ƙaramar hanyar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`           | _(babu komai = tushe)_                                   | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`                                  | Madubin `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` da browser ke iya gani, wanda ake saka shi kai tsaye a lokacin gini domin nunin maƙarar dashboard ya nuna `https://host/omniroute/v1` maimakon `https://host/v1`. Idan ba a saita shi ba, zai koma amfani da `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`. Sake ginawa bayan canjawa (`basePath` na Next yana samuwa ne a lokacin gini).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED`                     | _(ba a saita ba = ba za a taɓa iya saka shi a frame ba)_ | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/dashboardEmbed.mjs`                    | Zaɓin yarda da saka shafukan HTML a cikin iframe. Idan ba a saita ba, kowace hanya tana zuwa da `frame-ancestors 'none'` + `X-Frame-Options: DENY`. Saita shi zuwa `vscode` don samar da shafukan (dashboard, shiga, takardu, shafin sauka) tare da `frame-ancestors 'self' vscode-webview:` kuma ba tare da `X-Frame-Options` ba, domin VS Code Simple Browser ya iya nuna su (yanayin `dashboardOpen: "editor"` na OmniCopilot). Fagen API (`/api`, `/v1`, `/v1beta`, `/a2a`, `/healthz`, sunayen madadi na matakin tushe) yana riƙe da tsauraran headers a kowane hali. `vscode` kawai ake ganewa — `1`/`true` ba sa kunna shi. Na lokacin gini: sake ginawa bayan canjawa (`docker build --build-arg DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED=vscode` don images; saita shi a kan shigarwar da aka riga aka gina ba shi da tasiri).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `API_PORT`                                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Idan an saita, yana samar da proxy API na `/v1/*` a wannan tashar haɗi ta daban.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `API_HOST`                                  | `0.0.0.0`                                                | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Adireshin ɗaurewa don tashar haɗin API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `DASHBOARD_PORT`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Idan an saita, yana samar da UI na Dashboard a wannan tashar haɗi ta daban.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNI_MAX_CONCURRENT_CONNECTIONS`           | `0` _(a kashe)_                                          | `src/sse/utils/backpressure.ts`                                          | Yana iyakance haɗin tattaunawa masu gudana a lokaci guda; buƙatun da suka wuce iyakar suna samun `503` tare da `Retry-After`. Cikakkiyar lamba mai kyau tana kunna kariyar; rashin saitawa/`0` yana kashe ta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTANCE_ID`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Tsayayyen ID na musamman don wannan gateway yayin haɗa instances na OmniRoute a jere. Yana ba da damar binciken madauwari na peer mai shigowa. Haruffan da aka yarda da su: haruffa, lambobi, `.`, `_`, `:`, da `-`; iyaka haruffa 64.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_URLS`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`     | Jerin tushen URLs na OmniRoute da waƙafi ya raba waɗanda za su iya karɓar `X-OmniRoute-Peer-Trace`. URLs na upstream da aka sanya a jerin izini a sarari ne kaɗai suke karɓar metadata na peer; duk sauran masu samarwa ba a taɓa su ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_MAX_HOPS`                   | `4`                                                      | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Matsakaicin adadin instans na OmniRoute da aka ziyarta a baya waɗanda ake karɓa a buƙata mai sarƙaƙƙiya (`1`-`32`). Maimaita instans ko ƙarewar kasafi yana mayar da HTTP `508 Loop Detected`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT`                       | `20130`                                                  | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Port ɗin da aka wallafa a ɓangaren host don Dashboard a yanayin samarwa na Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROD_API_PORT`                             | `20131`                                                  | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Port ɗin da aka wallafa a ɓangaren host don API a yanayin samarwa na Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PORT`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Yana da fifiko a kan `PORT` yayin aiki a cikin Electron ko wasu wrappers.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `LIVE_WS_PORT`                              | `20129`                                                  | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Port don sabar sa ido kai-tsaye ta WebSocket.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `LIVE_WS_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                                              | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Adireshin ɗaurewa don sabar WebSocket kai-tsaye. Saita zuwa `0.0.0.0` don bayyana ta a LAN (sannan kuma saita `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`).
