Haɗe-haɗe suna tantance kansu ta amfani da cookie na zaman dashboard ko maɓallin Bearer API mai izinin gudanarwa (`manage`/`admin`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Ƙarin origins da waƙafi ya raba waɗanda aka yarda su buɗe WebSocket kai-tsaye. An riga an yarda da origins na dashboard na loopback ta tsohuwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_HOSTS`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | `src/server/ws/liveServerAllowList.ts`                                   | Ƙarin sunayen hosts da waƙafi ya raba waɗanda aka yarda da su don origins na WebSocket kai-tsaye. Ba kamar `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` (cikakkun URLs na origin) ba, wannan yana daidaita ɓangaren host kawai — mai amfani ga saitunan LAN/Tailscale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_WS_PUBLIC_URL`            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | `src/hooks/useLiveDashboard.ts`                                          | URL na jama'a don WebSocket na dashboard kai-tsaye (a ɓangaren browser). Saita shi yayin sanya reverse proxy ko Cloudflare Tunnel a gaban sabar WS (misali `wss://ws.my-ai.com/live-ws`); browser zai haɗu a can maimakon `ws://hostname:20132`. Ana kuma amfani da ɓangaren pathname a matsayin hanyar upgrade ta WebSocket (tsoho: `/live-ws`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_LIVE_WS`                  | `true`                                                   | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts` da `scripts/start-ws-server.mjs`           | Saita zuwa `0` ko `false` don kashe sabar WebSocket kai-tsaye (ana kunna ta ta tsohuwa, kuma tana ɗaure da loopback). Maɓallin CI/harness ne da ke kashe standalone helper script na WebSocket kai-tsaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE`                       | `30`                                                     | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/route.ts`                         | Iyakar ƙimar relay ga kowane (token, IP), buƙatu a minti. A cikin memory, ga kowane instans. `0` ko ƙasa da sifili yana kashe shingen ma'aunin IP (iyakar DB ta kowane token har yanzu tana aiki).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `NODE_ENV`                                  | `production`                                             | Next.js core                                                             | Yana sarrafa yawan bayanan log, caching, nuna cikakkun bayanan kuskure, da ingantawar Next.js.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK`                   | `1` (Turbopack — ƙimar tsoho ta lamba)                   | `package.json` / Next.js 16                                              | Turbopack shi ne bundler na tsoho don `npm run dev` da `npm run build` (ginawa masu saurin ninki 2-3, an gwada su). Saita zuwa `0` don komawa webpack a Windows, lokacin da aka ci karo da matsalolin rashin jituwa na native binding / bundler-compat, **ko a kan na'urori masu ƙarancin RAM** — an san cewa ginawar Turbopack na production a wannan jerin sigar Next.js (16.2.x) kan kai matakin amfani da ƙwaƙwalwa mai nisa fiye da webpack a manyan jadawalin modules (gyaran memory-eviction na Turbopack a Next 16.3 bai zama barga ba tukuna); komawa webpack yana kaiwa mataki mafi ƙasa sosai. Duba #6409. **Ginawar hoton Docker tana amfani da `0` (webpack) a matsayin tsoho**: `Dockerfile` yana ayyana `ARG OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=0` domin `docker build .` kai tsaye (Railway da sauran masu masaukin dannawa-sau-ɗaya, da masu masaukin kansu na wucin-gadi) kada a kashe shi saboda OOM a kan builder mai iyakantacciyar ƙwaƙwalwa; aika `--build-arg OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=1` don gina hoton da Turbopack. Duba `docs/guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md` → Albarkatun lokacin ginawa. |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | `src/lib/db/core.ts` / `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`                       | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake binciken lafiyar amincin SQLite lokacin farawa. Yana da amfani don saurin farawa a manyan rumbunan bayanai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `NOTIFY_SOCKET`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | systemd (ƙa'idar sd_notify)                                              | systemd ne ke saita shi lokacin da tsarin aiki ke gudana ƙarƙashin service unit mai haɗin sd_notify; OmniRoute yana karanta shi (duba `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`) don aika sanarwar READY/WATCHDOG. Bai kamata mai amfani ya taɓa saita shi ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`               | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | `scripts/dev/systemd-notify.mjs`                                         | Saita zuwa `1` don kashe systemd sd_notify (Type=notify / WatchdogSec=) ko da ana gudana ƙarƙashin systemd unit. Mai sanarwar ba ya yin komai a wajen systemd ko ta yaya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK_INTERVAL`          | `3600000`                                                | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts` | Tazarar lokaci (ms) ta mai tsara binciken lafiyar bayanan shaida da ke gudana a bango. Mafi ƙanƙanta: 10000 (10s).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL`               | `300000`                                                 | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/cache.ts`     | TTL (ms) na matsayin lafiyar bayanan shaida da aka adana a cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK` | `false`                                                  | `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts`                                  | Saita zuwa `1` ko `true` don kashe gwajin lokaci-lokaci na haɗin masu samarwa da ke gudana a bango. Masu samar da bincike (SEARCH_VALIDATOR_CONFIGS a cikin `src/lib/providers/validation/searchProviders.ts`, misali `tavily-search`) koyaushe ba a haɗa su cikin zagayen binciken — “tabbatarwar” su ainihin tambaya ce ga upstream wadda ake cajin kuɗi, saboda haka ba a taɓa bincikar lafiyarsu bisa jadawalin lokaci (#9970).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `DEEP_HEALTH_CHECK_ENABLED`                 | `0`                                                      | `src/app/api/monitoring/health/route.ts`                                 | Saita zuwa `1` don ba mai kira da aka tantance izinin neman `/api/monitoring/health?deep=1`, wanda ke ɗaukar samfurin completions surface sau ɗaya a kowane TTL. Masu kira marasa tantancewa ba sa taɓa tayar da probe ɗin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVENT_LOOP_STALL_THRESHOLD_MS`   | `5000`                                                   | `src/lib/healthzLag.ts`                                                  | Iyakar tsayawa (ms) ga mai rikodin event-loop na farawa: daskarewa da ta fi wannan tsawo tana rubuta layin `event loop stalled` guda ɗaya lokacin ci gaba tare da ƙididdigar ƙwaƙwalwa da ayyukan da ke gudana, sannan tana ƙara counter/maximum na `eventLoopStall` a `/api/monitoring/health`. Saita zuwa `0` don kashewa; ƙimar da ba za a iya karantawa ba ko mara kyau tana komawa `5000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `HOST`                                      | `0.0.0.0`                                                | `scripts/dev/run-next.mjs`                                               | Adireshin bind na sabar dev/start ta Next.js. Yana maye gurbin tsohuwar ƙimar `0.0.0.0` idan an saita shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `HOSTNAME`                                  | `127.0.0.1`                                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`                                    | Adireshin ɗaurewa da mai gudanar da Playwright ke amfani da shi lokacin ƙaddamar da Next.js. Tsohon ƙimarsa ita ce `127.0.0.1` don gwaje-gwaje masu zaman kansu. **Kada a yi amfani da shi don `omniroute serve`** — a maimakon haka, yi amfani da `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST` (shell na POSIX suna saita `HOSTNAME` kai tsaye zuwa sunan na’urar; `.env` ba zai iya maye gurbinsa ba).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST`                     | `0.0.0.0`                                                | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                             | Adireshin ɗaurewa na `omniroute serve`. Yana kauce wa karo da canjin shell na POSIX mai suna `HOSTNAME` (bash/zsh koyaushe suna saita shi zuwa sunan na’urar). Idan ba a saita shi ba, zai koma `0.0.0.0`. (#6194)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

### Yanayin Tashoshin Sadarwa

```
┌─────────────────────────── Tasha Guda (tsoho) ─────────────────────────────┐
│  PORT=20128                                                                 │
│  → Allon sarrafawa: http://localhost:20128                                  │
│  → API:             http://localhost:20128/v1/chat/completions              │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Rarrababbun Tashoshi ────────────────────────────┐
│  DASHBOARD_PORT=20128                                                       │
│  API_PORT=20129                                                             │
│  API_HOST=0.0.0.0                                                           │
│  → Allon sarrafawa: http://localhost:20128                                  │
│  → API:             http://0.0.0.0:20129/v1/chat/completions               │
│  Amfani: Bayyana API ga LAN tare da taƙaita Allon sarrafawa zuwa localhost. │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Docker na Samarwa ──────────────────────────────┐
│  PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT=443   PROD_API_PORT=8443                              │
│  → Yana haɗa tashoshin container da tashoshin host a docker-compose.prod.yml.│
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 4. Tsaro & Tantancewa

| Maɓalli                                     | Tsoho                                                                                                    | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MACHINE_ID_SALT`                           | `endpoint-proxy-salt`                                                                                    | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Gishirin da ake haɗawa da masu gano kayan masarufi don ƙirƙirar sawun na'ura. Canza shi ga kowane turawa domin keɓewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SALT`                        | _(ba a saita ba = gishiri bazuwar na kowace shigarwa da ake adanawa a `<DATA_DIR>/cli-token-salt.json`)_ | `src/lib/machineToken.ts`                                | Gishirin HMAC don samar da alamar tantancewar CLI ta gida. Saita wannan ƙimar yana sauya duk alamomin CLI da ke kan na'urar kuma koyaushe yana da fifiko kan gishirin da aka adana. Duba `docs/security/CLI_TOKEN.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE`                        | `false`                                                                                                  | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Yana saita tutar `Secure` a kan kukis na zama. **Dole ne ya kasance `true`** yayin aiki a bayan HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `REQUIRE_API_KEY`                           | `false`                                                                                                  | Matsakaiciyar manhajar API                               | Idan `true`, duk buƙatun wakili na `/v1/*` dole ne su ƙunshi ingantaccen mabuɗin API. **Wannan tutar ba ta taƙaita `GET /v1/models` ba**, wanda maimakon haka yake bin tsarin shiga na dashboard (`requireAuthForModels`) — saboda haka samun `401` daga `/v1/models` BA ya nufin cewa an kare inference. Duba `docs/security/INFERENCE_AUTH_POSTURE.md` (#13695).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL`                      | `false`                                                                                                  | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`         | Yana ba da damar bayyana cikakkun ƙimomin mabuɗan API a cikin UI na Dashboard. Ana iya daidaita shi daga Feature Flags na Dashboard; haɗarin tsaro ne a kan tsarin da ake amfani da shi tare.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `NO_LOG_API_KEY_IDS`                        | _(fanko)_                                                                                                | `src/lib/compliance/index.ts`                            | ID na mabuɗan API da waƙafi ya raba waɗanda ba a saka buƙatunsu cikin rajista (bin ƙa'idar GDPR).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `DEFAULT_RATE_LIMIT_PER_DAY`                | _(ba a saita ba = babu iyaka)_                                                                           | `src/shared/utils/apiKeyPolicy.ts`                       | Kasafin buƙatu na kowace rana da ake amfani da shi a matsayin madadin ga mabuɗan API waɗanda ginshiƙin `rate_limits` ɗinsu yake null. Idan ba a saita ba ko fanko ne: babu iyakar da aka ɗora a fakaice (#2289, #11017). `0` ma iri ɗaya ne (babu iyaka). Cikakkiyar lamba tabbatacciya N tana kunna N/rana, 5N/mako, 20N/wata. Ƙimomin da ba fanko ba amma suke da gurɓataccen tsari suna komawa ga tsoffin tagogin 1000/rana, 5000/mako, 20000/wata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES`                       | `10485760` (10 MB)                                                                                       | `src/shared/middleware/bodySizeGuard.ts`                 | Matsakaicin girman jikin buƙata da aka yarda. Yana ƙin payload da ya wuce wannan iyaka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`           | `262144` (256 KB)                                                                                        | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ainihin jikin buƙata da ya kai ko ya wuce wannan ma'auni yana karɓar izinin shigar da nauyi mai yawa na atomik na cikin tsarin aiki kafin fassara JSON (hanyar BYTE, har da `POST /v1/responses`). Yana da hanyar tserewa ta sararin lafiya iri ɗaya da [#10437](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/pull/10437) kamar mai nauyin tsari; har yanzu `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / [#10110](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/10110) suna iyakance shi domin kada [#7849](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/7849) ya sake dawowa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_BODY_BYTES`        | `52428800` (50 MB)                                                                                       | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Matsakaicin iyaka mai tsauri na hanyar chat da ake aiwatarwa bisa bytes da aka karanta yayin shigarwa mai iyaka, ciki har da buƙatun da ba su da `Content-Length`, ko suke da wanda ba shi da inganci, ko na yaudara; abin da ya wuce iyaka yana karɓar `413`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT`        | _(ba a saita ba — babu iyakar adadin buƙatu)_                                                            | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | **#503-fanout:** wannan tsohuwar iyakar ADADIN buƙatu yanzu tana aiki ne kawai idan an saita ta kai tsaye. Idan aka bar ta ba a saita ba (tsohon saitin), karɓar buƙatun chat masu nauyi a maimakon haka yana ƙarƙashin `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` — kasafin BYTES da ake samo ta atomatik bisa ainihin iyakar ƙwaƙwalwar tsarin a cikin **process guda ɗaya** (heap na V8 guda ɗaya). Buƙatun `/v1/responses` guda biyu masu cin karo da juna, kowannensu kusan token 750k, suna durƙusar da heap mai ~12 GiB (#7849) — wannan gargaɗin **kasafin ƙwaƙwalwa** ne, ba matsakaicin iyakar samfur mai tsauri na 2 ba. Process mai lafiya (wanda heap ɗinsa yake ƙasa da rabon zubarwa) ZAI IYA karɓar ƙarin dogayen `/v1/responses` na lokaci guda ta `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM`. Dubban goma na abokan SSE masu dogon haɗi (40–50) batun heap + `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / #10110 ne, ba “matsakaici 2” ba. Ƙara wannan kawai don “amfani da host” ba tare da nazari ba yana sake jawo #7849. Ninka **heaps** tare da **N na `DATA_DIR` masu zaman kansu** (#11024); kada a taɓa amfani da `replicas>1` a kan fayil ɗin SQLite guda ɗaya. |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES`         | _(ana samo shi ta atomatik)_                                                                             | `src/shared/middleware/admissionBudget.ts`               | **#503-fanout:** saitin maye gurbin kasafin bytes na shigarwa da ake samo ta atomatik (25% na mafi ƙanƙantar iyakar ƙwaƙwalwar V8/cgroup, a raba da ninkawar wucin gadi ta 8x). Ana taƙaita ƙimomin da aka samo da waɗanda aka saita kai tsaye zuwa 8 MiB–2 GiB. Body da ya fi ingantaccen kasafin girma yana gaza nan take da `413 body_exceeds_budget`; cunkoso tsakanin bodies da kowannensu za a iya sarrafawa ya kasance mai yiwuwa a sake gwadawa da `503`. Abokan SSE masu dogon haɗi 40–50 na lokaci guda sun dogara ne da wannan kasafi + heap, ba matsakaicin iyaka mai tsauri na “2” ba. Karanta `chatAdmission.maxInflightBytes` / `budgetSource` / `pressureSeverity` a `/api/monitoring/health` kafin daidaita saituna.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEAP_SHED_RATIO`  | `0.75`                                                                                                   | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Rabon zubarwa saboda matsin heap (`heapUsed / heap_size_limit`) don karɓar buƙatun BYTE da STRUCTURE masu nauyi (#10183, #10268, #10437). Ana zubar da buƙatar nauyi ta lokaci guda da ta wuce `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` tare da `503` mai yiwuwa a sake gwadawa ne kawai idan heap SHIMA ya kai ko ya wuce wannan rabo; idan heap yana lafiya, ana karɓar ta ta healthy-headroom maimakon haka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM` | `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` (tsohon saiti `1`)                                                  | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙarin ƙarfin da aka iyakance don hanyar gaggawa ta heap mai lafiya (#10437) a kan **duka** STRUCTURE da BYTE (`admitChatRequest`, ciki har da bodies ≥ `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`). Ba tare da wannan iyaka ba, kowace buƙata mai aiki amma heap ɗinta yana lafiya tana tsallake karɓar buƙatu ba tare da wani iyaka ba. Da zarar wannan adadin leases na lokaci guda ya fara aiki ta hanyar heap mai lafiya, ƙarin buƙatu masu aiki za su koma ga hanyar jira mai iyaka/zubarwa ƊAYA da ake amfani da ita ƙarƙashin ainihin matsin heap. `0` yana kashe tsallakewar gaba ɗaya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_MESSAGE_COUNT`        | `200`                                                                                                    | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Adadin saƙonnin da ke ware buƙatar chat a matsayin mai nauyi ko da body ɗinta yana ƙasa da iyakar bytes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_TOOL_COUNT`           | `64`                                                                                                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Adadin tools da ke ware buƙatar chat a matsayin mai nauyi ko da body ɗinta yana ƙasa da iyakar bytes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_ESTIMATED_TOKENS`     | `32000`                                                                                                  | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙiyasin token mai taka-tsantsan bisa girman string wanda ke ware buƙata a matsayin mai nauyi; wannan wakilin kuɗin karɓar buƙata ne, ba tokenization na lissafin kuɗin provider ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_MESSAGES`          | `0` (an kashe)                                                                                           | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Iyakar tarihin chat ta zaɓi. An kashe ta a tsohon saiti: adadin saƙonni manufofin deployment ne, ba siffa ta gama-gari ta buƙata ba, kuma sanya iyaka a nan yana ƙin tattaunawa da `413` na ƙarshe kafin pipeline na matsewa ya iya sa a iya sarrafa su. Ana iyakance ƙaruwar heap ta `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` da zubarwa saboda matsin heap. Saita ƙima mai kyau a deployments masu ƙarancin ƙwaƙwalwa waɗanda ke buƙatar iyaka mai tsauri; abin da ya wuce iyaka zai karɓi `413` mai tsari da ke buƙatar taƙaitawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_NONSTREAMING_RESPONSE_BYTES` | `67108864` (64 MB)                                                                                       | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/nonStreamingResponseBody.ts` | Matsakaicin iyaka mai tsauri ga amsar upstream marar streaming da aka adana gaba ɗaya a ƙwaƙwalwa. Bayan wannan, ana soke upstream reader kuma buƙatar tana gaza da sauri maimakon ci gaba da faɗaɗa string marar iyaka har heap ya ƙare.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORWARDING_HEADER_BUDGET_BYTES`  | `768`                                                                                                    | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/responseHeaders.ts`          | Matsakaicin adadin wire bytes da ake tura wa daga headers na martanin upstream. Idan an wuce kasafin, ana cire headers masu ƙarancin fifiko (misali, `x-codex-*`, `x-oai-request-id`) domin a ci gaba da kasancewa cikin iyakokin header na reverse-proxy da aka saba amfani da su. Saita ƙima mafi girma domin tura ƙarin metadata na upstream, amma hakan zai ƙara girman header na martani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CORS_ORIGIN`                               | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                                                        | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Tsohuwar jerin izinin CORS mai origin guda ɗaya. Fi son `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` don sabbin turawa. CORS na abokan hulɗar API na burauza masu amfani da cross-origin ne kawai; rubuce-rubucen dashboard masu buƙatar tantancewa suna amfani da buƙatun same-origin tare da kariyar CSRF da aka ɗaure da zaman maimakon haka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                                                        | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Jerin origins da CORS ya yarda da su, waɗanda waƙafi ya raba. Ba a aika wildcard sai idan an saita `CORS_ALLOW_ALL=true` a bayyane.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CORS_ALLOW_ALL`                            | `false`                                                                                                  | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Hanyar kaucewa ta lokacin haɓakawa kawai, wadda ke maimaita kowane `Origin` na burauza. Kada a kunna ta a turawa na gama-gari ko na samarwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED`               | `true`                                                                                                   | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Tsohon alias. Ana karanta togglen dashboard ɗinsa (override na DB) kafin wannan ƙimar muhalli; `false`, `0`, `no`, ko `off` a ɗayansu yana kashe binciken host kamar yadda `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` ke yi, ko da an adana togglen dashboard na wannan flag a kashe; sauran ƙimomi suna barin aikin guard ga flags biyu da ke ƙasa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`     | `false`                                                                                                  | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Yana kashe binciken hosts na outbound URL guard, ciki har da toshe cloud-metadata (duk na 169.254.0.0/16, metadata.google.internal), a hanyoyin provider da ke bin yanayin guard: tabbatar da URL, gano model, base URLs na provider-node (ciki har da remote rerank nodes), da gwajin proxy-fallback, tare da sauran hanyoyin outbound da ke da alaƙa da provider kamar ɗauko hoto/media daga nesa. Haka kuma yana ba da izinin private webhook targets, ban da cloud-metadata/link-local endpoints, waɗanda za su ci gaba da kasancewa a toshe ko da an kunna wannan flag. Buƙatun chat da ake aikawa ta `BaseExecutor.execute()` suna ci gaba da binciken cloud-metadata nasu. Local providers suna aiki da saitunan tsohuwa: `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` (tsoho `true`) ya riga ya ba da izinin local da LAN URLs a hanyoyin tabbatarwa, ganowa, da provider-node; gwajin proxy-fallback da private webhook targets suna duba wannan flag kawai kuma suna toshe local/LAN hosts yayin da yake kashe. Yana rinjayar `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS=false`. Ƙimar da aka adana daga togglen dashboard tana da fifiko a kan wannan variable.                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS`       | `true`                                                                                                   | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Ba da izinin URLs na provider a local/private addresses (127.0.0.1, localhost, LAN, private ranges) don tabbatar da provider, gano model, chat, da sauran hanyoyin outbound masu alaƙa da provider waɗanda yanayin guard ke gudanarwa. **Tsoho `true`** (local-first): daga nan guard yana toshe cloud-metadata endpoints — duk na 169.254.0.0/16 tare da sanannun metadata hostnames (metadata.google.internal, metadata.goog, Alibaba/Azure/Oracle IMDS, fd00:ec2::254). Saita `false` don yanayin public-only, wanda yake toshe private da loopback hosts ma. Guard yana bincika hostname ko IP literal yadda aka rubuta shi, kafin kowane DNS lookup; a yanayin public-only, safe-fetch wrapper kuma yana warware hostname sannan ya ƙi duk wata private answer. `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` yana kashe waɗannan bincike a hanyoyin da layinsa ya lissafa. Built-in local providers, kamar LM Studio, Ollama, vLLM, Llamafile, da Triton, suna tsallake duk binciken host don tabbatar da key da chat. Ƙimar da aka adana daga togglen dashboard tana da fifiko a kan wannan variable. (#5066)                                                           |
| `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`               | `false`                                                                                                  | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/audioProviderNodes.ts`           | Ba wa hanyoyin `/v1/audio/*` (transcriptions, speech, translations) damar amfani da provider node mai dacewa da OpenAI wanda aka host a wajen localhost. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — tura audio zuwa remote host yana sauya egress identity kuma dole ne ya zama shawarar operator a bayyane. Loopback/private nodes (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) ana ba su izini koyaushe kuma wannan ba ya shafarsu. (#3963)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`              | `false`                                                                                                  | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/rerankProviderNodes.ts`          | Ba wa `POST /v1/rerank` (da loopback rerank step na memory engine) damar amfani da provider node mai dacewa da OpenAI wanda aka host a wajen localhost — akwatin LAN ko Tailscale peer da ke gudanar da TEI, Infinity, vLLM, da sauransu. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — tura aiki zuwa remote host yana sauya egress identity kuma dole ne ya zama shawarar operator a bayyane. Loopback nodes (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) ana ba su izini koyaushe kuma wannan ba ya shafarsu. Dole ne base URL na remote node ya kuma wuce provider outbound URL policy (`OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` / `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_PROVIDER_NODE_HOSTS`       | _(babu komai)_                                                                                           | `src/shared/network/localNodeHosts.ts`                   | Sunayen host (waɗanda aka raba da waƙafi ko farin sarari) waɗanda tsarin tura buƙata na provider-node ke ɗauka a matsayin na gida — galibi sunan sabis na Docker/Compose na reranker, TEI ko audio sidecar a kan hanyar sadarwar Docker ta OmniRoute (`http://reranker:8080/v1`), don kada node ɗin ya buƙaci IP na container da aka saka kai tsaye. Host ɗin da aka jera sun cancanci `/v1/rerank`, `/v1/audio/*` da binciken lafiyar gida ba tare da kunna `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` / `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` ga kowane node na nesa ba, kuma ana kiransu kai tsaye — ba a taɓa bi ta `HTTP(S)_PROXY` ko proxy da aka ɗaure wa haɗi ba. Har yanzu suna aika maɓallin API na haɗin. Sunaye masu lakabi guda ɗaya da sunayen `.internal`/`.local` kawai ake karɓa; ana yin watsi da FQDN na jama'a, rubutattun IP da sunayen cloud-metadata tare da gargaɗi. (#14635)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`     | `false`                                                                                                  | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Lokacin da aka kunna OIDC, a kashe shiga da kalmar sirri domin masu amfani su iya tantance kansu ta hanyar OIDC Single Sign-On kawai. Ana kuma karɓar taƙaitaccen sunan `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`; Dashboard Feature Flag mai maɓalli iri ɗaya yana da fifiko. (#10889)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`               | `false`                                                                                                  | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Taƙaitaccen suna na `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN` (#10889).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

### Jerin Dubawa na Ƙarfafa Tsaro

```bash
# Mafi ƙarancin tsaron samarwa:
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true        # Yana buƙatar HTTPS
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true           # Tantance duk kiran proxy
ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL=false     # Kada a taɓa bayyana maɓallai a UI
CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://your.domain.com
MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES=5242880    # Iyakacin 5 MB
```

---

## 5. Tsabtace Bayanai Masu Shigowa & Kare PII

OmniRoute yana samar da kariya mai matakai biyu: binciken yunƙurin kutse a ɓangaren buƙata da cire PII a ɓangaren martani.

> **⚠️ Iyakoki:** Waɗannan matakan kariya bincike ne na _hasashe gwargwadon ƙoƙari_, ba cikakken bangon kariya daga kutse ta hanyar prompt ko tsarin PII DLP ba. Za su iya haifar da sakamako na ƙarya (kamar yi wa prompts na persona/RPG marasa lahani alama) da kuma kasa gano barazana (kamar leetspeak, tazara, da tsarukan da ba na Turanci ba). Su kaɗai ba su isa wajen bin ƙa'idodi ba. Daidaita modes ɗin kuma gwada su da zirga-zirgarku kafin ku dogara da su.

### Ɓangaren Buƙata: Kariyar Kutse ta Prompt

| Maɓalli                           | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| --------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED`         | `true`            | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Kunna binciken saƙonni masu shigowa domin gano tsarukan kutse ta prompt.                                                                                                                           |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE`            | `warn`            | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Manufofin kutse: `warn` = rubuta log kawai, `block` = ƙin buƙatar tare da 400. Tsohon `redact` **ba ya** cire rubutun kutse; yi amfani da `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED` domin sake rubuta PII na buƙata. |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_MODE`            | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Tsohon madadin suna na `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE` — halayya iri ɗaya.                                                                                                                                  |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | `high`            | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Mafi ƙarancin matakin tsanani da `MODE=block` zai ƙi: `high` (tsoho), `medium`, ko `low`. Ana sa ido kawai kan tsarukan matsakaicin tsanani sai an rage matakin.                                   |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Tsohon madadin suna na `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` — halayya iri ɗaya.                                                                                                                       |
| `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`           | `false`           | `src/lib/guardrails/piiMasker.ts`        | Idan `true`, ɓoye PII a cikin buƙatu masu shigowa (ba tare da dogaro da yanayin kutse ba).                                                                                                         |
| `CREDENTIAL_REDACTION_ENABLED`    | `false`           | `src/lib/guardrails/credentialMasker.ts` | Ɓoye sanannun tsarukan API-key / secret-token daga bayanan buƙata/martani. Sai an zaɓa a kunna; yana kwaikwayon `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`.                                                           |

### Ɓangaren Martani: Mai Tsabtace PII

| Maɓalli                          | Tsoho    | Fayil ɗin Tushe           | Bayani                                                                                 |
| -------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION`      | `false`  | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | Bincika martanin LLM domin gano PII da ya fallasa kafin mayar da shi ga abokin ciniki. |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION_MODE` | `redact` | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | `redact` = ɓoye PII, `warn` = rubuta log kawai, `block` = watsar da dukkan martanin.   |

### Mai Tsabtace Context na Tokenized-Route na VS Code

| Maɓalli                             | Tsoho | Fayil ɗin Tushe                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------------- | ----- | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VSCODE_SANITIZE_CONTEXT` | `1`   | `src/app/api/v1/vscode/contextSanitizer.ts` | Yana cire context na active-editor da aka haɗa kai tsaye (`editorContext`, `activeEditor`, `currentFile`, `selection`, `openTabs`…) daga buƙatun `/v1/vscode/[token]/*` kuma yana ɓoye abubuwan da ke cikin fayiloli masu muhimmanci da aka haɗa a bayyane. Amintacce ta tsoho; saita zuwa `0` domin kashewa. |

### Yanayin Amfani

| Yanayi                    | Saituna                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Bin ƙa'idodin kamfani** | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=block`, `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED=true`, `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION=true` (toshe kutse + ɓoye PII na buƙata/martani; modes ɗin ba su dogara da juna ba) |
| **Sa ido kawai**          | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=warn` — yana rubuta logs amma ba ya taɓa toshewa                                                                                                     |
| **Amfani na kashin kai**  | Bar duk a kashe — babu ƙarin nauyi                                                                                                                                                                         |

---

## 6. Manufofin Kayan Aiki da Zaɓin Hanya

| Sauyi                                                       | Tsoho                            | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                 | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `TOOL_POLICY_MODE`                                          | `disabled`                       | `src/lib/toolPolicy.ts`                         | Yana sarrafa damar LLM ta amfani da kayan aiki/kiran ayyuka. `allowlist` = kayan aikin da aka jera kawai, `denylist` = duk sai waɗanda aka jera, `disabled` = babu ƙuntatawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_PATH`                              | `./config/payloadRules.json`     | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`             | Hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin JSON na ƙa'idojin sarrafa payload (gyare-gyaren upstream na kowane samfuri/protocol).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_RELOAD_MS`                         | `5000`                           | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`             | Tazarar sake lodawa (ms) don sake loda fayil ɗin ƙa'idojin payload kai tsaye.
