Mafi ƙanƙanta `1000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PREFER_CLAUDE_CODE_FOR_UNPREFIXED_CLAUDE_MODELS` | `false`                          | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                    | Zaɓin shiga: aika ID na samfuran `claude-*` marasa prefix daga abokan cinikin Claude Code ta asusun OAuth na Claude Code maimakon buƙatar prefix na mai samarwa. Prefix na mai samarwa da aka bayyana kai tsaye har yanzu yana da fifiko. Hakanan ana iya saita shi ta maɓallin kunnawa a dashboard a shafin mai samar da Claude.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDACT_THINKING`                                 | `true`                           | `open-sse/executors/base/redactThinkingBeta.ts` | Saita `false` don hana OmniRoute ƙara `redact-thinking-2026-02-12` Anthropic beta da kansa (tubalan tunani marasa komai masu sa hannu kawai). Buƙatun da ke da `thinking.display: "summarized"` suna tsallake shi ko da yaushe; beta na redact da abokin ciniki ya sasanta ko zaɓin shiga na `requestDefaults.redactThinking` mai dacewa da CC yana ci gaba da riƙe shi. Buƙatun OAuth na Claude suna riƙe beta ɗin (yana cikin sawun OAuth na Claude Code) sai dai idan an kunna alamar fasalin `CLAUDE_OAUTH_SUMMARIZED_THINKING_UNREDACTED` wadda a tsohuwa take a kashe.                                                                              |
| `BARE_MODEL_DEFAULT_OVERRIDE_LEGACY`                        | `false`                          | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                    | Halayyar tsohuwar `resolveBareModelToConnectionDefault` (#4825): maye gurbin kowane sunan samfuri mara prefix da `defaultModel` na haɗin da aka zaɓa. A kashe take a tsohuwa; `auto` kawai ko samfuri mara komai ake maye gurbinsa; tana rubuta gargaɗi guda ɗaya ga kowane biyu idan tana kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `COMBO_CONCURRENCY_PER_MODEL`                               | `3`                              | `open-sse/services/comboConfig.ts`              | Iyakar ayyukan lokaci guda ga kowane samfuri don haɗaɗɗun round-robin (#9100). An ƙayyade semaphore na haɗin round-robin zuwa buƙatu 3 masu gudana lokaci guda ga kowane samfuri ba tare da damar canjawa ba, wanda ke jera zirga-zirgar da ke da yawan ayyukan lokaci guda a bayan wannan iyaka. Ana tabbatar da shi zuwa `>= 1`, sannan a taƙaita shi zuwa `<= 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `DISABLE_CONTEXT_WINDOW_CHECKS`                             | `false`                          | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`                 | Zaɓin shiga mai haɗari wanda ke tsallake binciken taga mahalli / iyakar token na shigarwa na OmniRoute ga buƙatun kai tsaye na samfuri guda. Masu samar da upstream har yanzu suna tilasta ainihin iyakokinsu; matse prompt da iyakar token na fitarwa ta samfurin kansa suna ci gaba da aiki. Jerin fifikon da ke aiki shi ne maye gurbin DB na Feature Flags > sauyin muhalli > tsoho; ba a buƙatar sake kunnawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`                           | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`          | Takardar YAML ta `providers:` da aka saka kai tsaye (RIC-738, D4). Idan an saita ta (tare da ko ba tare da tubalin `strategy:` ba), `/v1/chat/completions` yana karkata zuwa hanyar shiga guda ɗaya mai dacewa da OpenAI da ake gudanarwa da kai maimakon tsarin cloud. Idan ba a saita ba (tsoho): hanyar tana wucewa kai tsaye zuwa tsarin cloud da ake da shi. Duba `docs/routing/SELF_HOSTED_OPENAI_ENTRY.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS_FILE`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`          | Hanya zuwa fayil ɗin YAML da ke ɗauke da takardar `providers:` iri ɗaya da `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, don turawa da suka fi son fayil maimakon sauyin muhalli da aka saka kai tsaye. Kowanne daga cikinsu yana kunna hanyar shiga da ake gudanarwa da kai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_API_KEY`                             | _(ba a saita ba — hanya a buɗe)_ | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`          | Maɓallin API na haɗin gwiwa na zaɓi don hanyar shiga guda ɗaya da ake gudanarwa da kai (tsarin farko na D5, wanda aka tanada don tsarin ƙayyadadden kason kowane maɓalli). Idan an saita shi, dole ne buƙatu su ƙunshi `Authorization: Bearer <key>`. Idan ba a saita ba: hanyar a buɗe take, daidai da tsarin mai samarwa na gida da ake gudanarwa da kai wanda ake da shi (turawa ta loopback/cibiyar sadarwa amintacciya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`        | Takardar YAML `strategy:` ta cikin layi don injin zaɓin hanya mai tabbataccen sakamako (M2/RIC-740, D3) — blacklist/whitelist, mai katsewa na cooldown, fifikon farashi, la'akari da latency, da jerin fallback. Tana maye gurbin tubalin `strategy:` na cikin layi da aka saka a cikin `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, bisa kowane maɓalli. Duba `docs/routing/DETERMINISTIC_ROUTING.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY_FILE`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`        | Hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin YAML mai ɗauke da takardar `strategy:` iri ɗaya da ta `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`, don turawar tsarin da ta fi son fayil maimakon env var na cikin layi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONVERSATION_TRACKING`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `open-sse/services/conversationTracker.ts`      | Saita `1` don dakatar da tattara tarihin tattaunawa. `resolveConversationId()` yana dawo da sakamakon da ba a bibiyarsa kafin ya karanta SQLite ko ya rarrabe tarihin saƙonni, kuma hakan ya shafi ID na zaman da abokin ciniki ya bayar ma. Gudanar da zaman zaɓin hanya bai canza ba, kuma ba a share bayanan da ke akwai. Don turawar tsarin da ba ta amfani da kallon tattaunawa na dashboard kuma take son hana jadawalin zagaye ci gaba da girma.                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_API_KEY`                                   | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`  | Maɓallin API don dabarar na'ura mai zaɓin hanya ta auto-combo `nadir` wadda ake shiga da zaɓi, ana amfani da shi idan ba a saita `config.nadir.apiKey` na combo ɗin kansa ba. Na zaɓi ne: kira marasa maɓalli suna shiga matakin Nadir na ɓoyayyen mai amfani, wanda ke da iyakancewar ƙimar amfani bisa kowane IP, don haka ba za a iya amfani da shi a bayan gateway da ake rabawa ba. Mai muhimmancin sirri ne; fi son filin kowane combo idan combos suna amfani da asusu daban-daban.                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_BASE_URL`                                  | `https://api.getnadir.com`       | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`  | Tushen asalin API na yanke shawarar Nadir, ana amfani da shi idan ba a saita `config.nadir.baseUrl` na combo ɗin kansa ba. Ana buƙatarsa ne kawai don Nadir mai masaukin-kai, wanda ke riƙe farkon prompts a cikin hanyar sadarwarka. Ana amincewa da `/v1` a ƙarshe kuma ana cire shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW`                           | `provider`                       | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`            | Tagar mako-mako don iyakokin amfani da USD na kowane maɓallin API (tilasta buƙata, `GET /api/keys/{id}/usage-limits`, `@@om-usage`). `provider`: makon yana ƙarewa a lokacin sake saita mako-mako mafi kusa a tsakanin haɗin upstream da maɓallin zai iya kaiwa gare su, ko kuma ya zama kwanaki 7 masu birgima idan babu wanda aka sani. `calendar`: makon gida daga Litinin 00:00 zuwa Litinin mai zuwa 00:00, iri ɗaya ga kowane maɓalli. Ana ba da shawarar `calendar` idan asusun upstream guda ɗaya da ake rabawa yana amfani ga maɓallai masu yawa waɗanda kowannensu ke da kasafin kuɗinsa. Ƙimomin da ba su dace ba suna komawa zuwa `provider`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW_TIMEZONE`                  | _(yankin lokaci na process)_     | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`            | Yankin lokaci na IANA (misali `Asia/Shanghai`) wanda ke ƙayyade Litinin 00:00 don tagar mako-mako ta `calendar`. Idan ba a saita ba ko bai inganta ba: yankin lokaci na process ɗin OmniRoute (`TZ` na host ko container, wanda sau da yawa ba a saita shi ba, wato Coordinated Universal Time, a cikin hotunan container). Ana yin watsi da shi a yanayin `provider`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_TOOLS_LIMIT`                                 | `128`                            | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                  | Matsakaicin adadin tools na asali da aka yarda da su a cikin buƙata (tsohon ƙimar OpenAI). Cikakkun lambobi masu kyau kawai; ƙimomin da ba a saita ba ko marasa inganci suna barin `128`. Provider mai iyaka sananniya/da aka riga aka tanada (`grok-cli` 200, `nvidia` 1536) har yanzu yana amfani da iyakarsa — wannan yana sauya rufin tsohon ƙima ne kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROMPT_EMULATE_TOOLS`                            | _(a kunne)_                      | `open-sse/executors/syntxChat.ts`               | Kiran tool ta hanyar kwaikwayon prompt na SYNTX.ai: idan ba a saita ba ko kuma `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes`, ana saka kundin tools na gida cikin prompt; duk wata ƙima dabam (misali `0`) tana tsallake wannan sakawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

Mai matuƙar sirri; a fi son filin Dashboard na kowane haɗi idan an saita asusu da yawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

Mai matuƙar sirri; a fi son filin Dashboard na kowane haɗi idan an saita asusu da yawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEWHISPERER_BASE_URL`       | `https://codewhisperer.us-east-1.amazonaws.com`           | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Mahadar iyakokin amfani na CodeWhisperer (AWS Kiro). Sauya shi don relays / test fixtures.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Lokacin turawa a bayan reverse proxy (nginx, Caddy), saita `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` zuwa tsayayyen URL na jama'a (misali, `https://omniroute.example.com`) idan dole ne kiran-baya na OAuth ko hanyoyin haɗin jama'a da aka samar su yi amfani da wannan hostname. Idan babu wannan, kiran-baya na OAuth na iya gaza saboda `redirect_uri` ba zai dace ba, kuma hanyoyin haɗin jama'a da aka samar na iya nuna asalin container na ciki.
>
> Bar `BASE_URL` a matsayin URL na loopback/container na ciki don ayyukan server-to-server. Kada a yi amfani da `Origin` na browser ko hostname na jama'a don internal self-fetches masu ɗauke da bayanan shaida.
>
> Rubuce-rubucen dashboard da aka tabbatar da izini ba sa buƙatar tsayayyen base URL na jama'a: dashboard yana aika unsafe requests na same-origin tare da CSRF token mai alaƙa da session. Har yanzu OmniRoute yana tattara tabbatar da public-origin a wuri guda don haɗe-haɗen browser da ba na dashboard ba: ana fara amincewa da env vars na public URL da aka ayyana a sarari; ana watsi da ɗanyen headers na `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` sai dai idan an kunna `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` kuma an yi wa immediate proxy peer alamar token a matsayin amintacce. Kada a yi amfani da saitunan CORS don gyara requests na dashboard na same-origin; CORS na clients na browser masu cross-origin ne kawai.

---

## 8. Wakilin Fita

Tura kiran masu samar da LLM na upstream ta hanyar wakilin HTTP ko SOCKS5 don sarrafa zirga-zirgar fita, zaɓar hanya bisa yanki, ko ɓoye adireshin IP.

`OMNIROUTE_UPSTREAM_HTTP2_ENABLED` yana amfani da `true` a matsayin ƙimar asali. Saita shi zuwa `false`, `0`, `no`, ko
`off` don tilasta amfani da HTTP/1.1 a kan masu aikawa na Undici da ke cikin `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`
da `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`: fita kai tsaye, wakilan HTTP(S)/SOCKS, relays, da
sake gwada haɗinsu ta sabon socket. Wannan yana kiyaye tabbatar da takardar shaidar TLS, tantancewar
wakili, iyakokin haɗi, da saitunan keep-alive. Sake kunna OmniRoute bayan
canza shi, domin ana adana masu aikawa a ma'ajiyar wucin gadi kuma ana ƙirƙirar relay agents lokacin farawa.
Wannan sauyin ba ya sarrafa kwaikwayon sawun TLS na wreq ko hanyoyin sufuri na musamman ga masu samarwa.

| Maɓalli                                  | Na asali          | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`            | `open-sse/executors`                           | Kunna wakilin proxy na SOCKS5 don kiraye-kirayen zuwa sabis na sama. A kashe ta hanyar saita `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`            | Bangaren abokin ciniki                         | Sanin samuwar SOCKS5 a bangaren abokin ciniki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `true`            | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Tutar fasali da aka kunna ta tsohuwa (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); saiti daga DB na dashboard yana da fifiko). Tarin proxy da juyawa na kowane asusu za su daina sake ba da wani mamba da ya gaza a yanzu (binciken TCP da aka ƙi, ko kuskuren 429 ta cikinsa) na wani lokaci da ke ninkawa sau biyu duk lokacin da ya sake faruwa, har zuwa iyaka. `false` yana dawo da zaɓi na yau da kullum.                                                                       |
| `SELECTOR_CONTROL_ALLOWLIST`             | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/proxySubscription/selectorGuard.ts`   | Jerin izini don wuraren da local-core selector-control ke nufi bayan loopback (sunayen host ko IP da waƙafi/sarari ya raba; `*` yana buɗe LAN/na sirri amma ba metadata/link-local ba). Ana yarda da loopback ta http/https ta tsohuwa. Ƙimar DB ta dashboard tana da fifiko kan wannan maɓalli idan an saita ta kuma ba fanko ba ce.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_BASE_MS`                | `300000`          | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Lokacin tushe (ms) na warewar 429, wanda ke ninkawa sau biyu a kowane maimaituwa. Ana karanta shi sau ɗaya yayin farawa. An iyakance shi zuwa `1000..3600000`; ƙimar da ta fita daga iyaka ko wadda ba za a iya karantawa ba za ta koma ga ƙimar asali tare da gargaɗi.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_MAX_MS`                 | `900000`          | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Iyakar (ms) warewar 429. Ana karanta ta sau ɗaya yayin farawa. An iyakance ta zuwa `1000..3600000`; ƙimar da ta fita daga iyaka ko wadda ba za a iya karantawa ba za ta koma ga ƙimar asali tare da gargaɗi. Idan iyakar ta yi ƙasa da tushen, za ta koma ga ƙimar asali. Rage iyakar kuma yana taƙaita lokacin manta jerin maimaituwa: ana goge shigarwa bayan `2 x max` daga ƙarewar lokaci.                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_POOL_SHARED_EGRESS_ORDER`         | `false`           | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Tutar fasali ta zaɓin shiga (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); saiti daga DB na dashboard yana da fifiko). Ga masu samarwa waɗanda ake rarraba adadin amfaninsu bisa adireshin fita, a jera memban tarin da ke amfani da adireshin fita iri ɗaya da aka gani na wani memba da aka ƙi kwanan nan a ƙasan membobi masu lafiya kai tsaye. Jeri kawai ake yi, ba a taɓa warewa ba. Yana buƙatar `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`, wanda ke samar da siginar ƙin da yake karantawa. |
| `PROXY_WEBHOOK_REBOUND_MS`               | `300000`          | `src/lib/proxyEvents/proxyTransitionBridge.ts` | Tagar rebound (ms) da ke iyakance maimaita faɗakarwar webhook ta `proxy.set_aside` ga kowane memban tarin, domin cunkoson ƙin buƙata ya aika da aukuwa guda ɗaya. Ana karanta ta a kowane fitarwa. An iyakance ta zuwa `1000..3600000`; ƙimar da ta fita daga iyaka ko wadda ba za a iya karantawa ba za ta koma ga ƙimar asali ba tare da gargaɗi ba.                                                                                                                                |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Ma'aunin Node.js                               | Proxy na HTTP don kiraye-kirayen zuwa sabis na sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Ma'aunin Node.js                               | Proxy na HTTPS don kiraye-kirayen zuwa sabis na sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Ma'aunin Node.js                               | Proxy na bai ɗaya (yana goyon bayan `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                   | Yana ƙayyade maƙasudin echo-IP da gwaje-gwajen fitar proxy ke amfani da shi zuwa URL guda ɗaya. Idan ba a saita ba, gwajin zai fara da `api64.ipify.org` sannan `api4.ipify.org`, domin kada a ba da rahoton cewa tunnels masu IPv4-kawai ba sa aiki (#9694).                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Ma'aunin Node.js                               | Sunayen host/IP da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda za a tsallake proxy dominsu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`              | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`            | Matsakaicin sockets masu aiki lokaci guda ga kowane HTTP/SOCKS proxy dispatcher da aka adana a cache. Rafukan SSE masu daɗewa kamar Codex `/v1/responses` suna buƙatar haɗi fiye da ɗaya idan buƙatu da yawa suna amfani da proxy ɗaya na matakin asusu. Ana iyakance ƙimomin da suka wuce `256`.                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`           | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts`   | Lokacin ƙarewar SOCKS5 handshake (connect) a cikin ms. Ƙara shi idan ana amfani da host guda na residential gateway da cunkoso mai yawa (misali, buƙatu 100 a lokaci guda) — ainihin handshake na iya wuce 10s idan pool ya cika duk da cewa ana iya isa ga proxy, wanda in ba haka ba zai bayyana a matsayin kuskuren ƙarya na `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Proxy unreachable`. An iyakance shi zuwa `120000`.                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`           | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`              | Idan `false` ne (tsoho), buƙatar da proxy ɗin da aka ware mata ya kasa resolve za a **ƙi karɓa (fail-closed)** maimakon komawa ga haɗin kai tsaye — wannan yana hana fallasa ainihin IP. Saita `true` don dawo da tsohon yanayin komawa ga DIRECT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`           | `open-sse/executors`                           | Kwaikwayi fingerprint na TLS ta amfani da wreq-js (yana kwaikwayon Chrome 124). Yana magance toshewar JA3/JA4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                 | Jerin providers da aka yarda da su, waɗanda aka raba da waƙafi, don sabon tsarin tura TLS ta proxy (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`). Direct TLS yana ci gaba da tsohon halinsa idan ba a saita ba; waɗannan providers kaɗai ne ake turawa ta gadar fingerprint ta Chrome-124. Ana cire Groq (`api.groq.com`) ko da ba a saita jerin izinin ba, saboda Chrome JA3 yana jawo Cloudflare 1010, yayin da native undici ke isa ga API.                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`           | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts`   | Ba mahallin browser na Claude Turnstile Playwright damar yin watsi da kurakuran takardar shaidar HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

### Yanayin Amfani

| Yanayi                                                         | Saitin                                                                                                                               |
| -------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| **SOCKS5 ta cikin SSH tunnel**                                 | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                                      |
| **HTTP proxy na kamfani**                                      | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com`            |
| **Kariya daga fingerprint**                                    | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` — yana buƙatar `wreq-js` (an haɗa shi)                                                                 |
| **Fitar da ake sarrafawa / babu shiga kai tsaye**              | Bar `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` (tsoho). Buƙatu za su gaza gaba ɗaya idan babu proxy maimakon fallasa bayanai ta hanyar haɗin kai tsaye. |
| **Tsohon tsari / dev — ba da damar komawa ga haɗin kai tsaye** | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Yana dawo da halin kafin ƙarfafa tsaro: ana amfani da haɗin kai tsaye idan proxy resolution ya gaza.         |

> **Lura (tsallake NVIDIA validation — #3226):** Endpoint na NVIDIA don tantance API-key
> yana makale idan aka tura shi ta global proxy/TLS-patched fetch (undici dispatcher → 504).
> `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` da gangan yana tsallake
> proxy patch don wannan kiran validation guda ɗaya ta amfani da `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`.
> Wannan keɓantaccen yanayi ne da aka rubuta kuma aka iyakance — **ba ya** shafar fitar chat/usage.
> An ɗaure iyakar tsallakewar ta `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts`.

---

## 9. Haɗa Kayan Aikin CLI

Yana sarrafa yadda OmniRoute ke ganowa da kuma ƙaddamar da shirye-shiryen CLI na gefe (Claude Code, Codex, da sauransu).

| Maɓalli                           | Tsoho                            | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                     | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| --------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_MODE`                        | `auto`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | `auto` = bincika PATH na tsarin; `manual` = yi amfani da takamaiman hanyoyi kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CLI_EXTRA_PATHS`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ƙarin shigarwar PATH don gano fayilolin binary na CLI (an raba su da alamar colon).                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CLI_CONFIG_HOME`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Sauya kundin adireshin gida don karanta saitunan CLI (`~/.claude`, `~/.codex`). Dole ne ya zama cikakkiyar hanya kuma ya kasance cikin gidan aikin — **ko kuma**, a cikin container, hanya ce da aka bind-mount (haka `/host-home` yake aiki). Duk wani abu dabam zai koma kundin adireshin gida. |
| `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`         | `true`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ba OmniRoute damar rubuta fayilolin saitunan CLI (sabunta token, bayanan zaman aiki). Saita zuwa `false` don sa duk wani yunƙurin rubuta saitin CLI ya gaza tare da kuskuren "an kashe rubutawa" a sarari.                                                                                        |
| `CLI_CLAUDE_BIN`                  | `claude`                         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Hanya ta musamman zuwa fayil ɗin binary na Claude CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CLI_CODEX_BIN`                   | `codex`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Hanya ta musamman zuwa fayil ɗin binary na Codex CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CLI_DROID_BIN`                   | `droid`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Hanya ta musamman zuwa fayil ɗin binary na Droid CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CLI_OPENCLAW_BIN`                | `openclaw`                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Hanya ta musamman zuwa fayil ɗin binary na OpenClaw CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CLI_CURSOR_BIN`                  | `agent`, sannan `cursor`         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Hanya ta musamman zuwa fayil ɗin binary na wakilin Cursor. Idan babu ita, ganowa zai fara gwada `agent`, sannan ya koma ga `cursor`.                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CLI_CLINE_BIN`                   | `cline`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Hanya ta musamman zuwa fayil ɗin binary na Cline CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CLI_5DIVE_BIN`                   | `5dive`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Hanya ta musamman zuwa fayil ɗin binary na 5dive CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CLI_5DIVE_STATE_DIR`             | `/var/lib/5dive`                 | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Kundin yanayin tsarin 5dive (bayanan tabbatarwa mallakar root); yana kwaikwayon tsohuwar ƙimar STATE_DIR ta 5dive.                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CLI_CONTINUE_BIN`                | `cn`                             | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Hanya ta musamman zuwa fayil ɗin binary na Continue CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

| `CLI_AIDER_BIN`                   | `aider`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Hanya ta musamman zuwa fayil ɗin binary na Aider CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

| `ZCODE_SERVER_RUNTIME_ROOT`       | `~/.zcode/server`                | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Tushen runtime na app-server na ZCode (inda `node` da `zcode-server.cjs` da aka haɗa suke).                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ZCODE_SERVER_NODE`               | `<runtimeRoot>/node`             | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Fayil ɗin Node mai aiwatarwa da ake amfani da shi wajen ɗaukar nauyin app-server na ZCode.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ZCODE_SERVER_ENTRY`              | `<runtimeRoot>/zcode-server.cjs` | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Rubutun shigarwa na app-server da ake amfani da shi wajen ɗaukar nauyin sabar ZCode.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ZCODE_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_MS`        | `10000`                          | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Wa'adin ƙarewar farawa (ms) kafin a ɗauki cewa ƙaddamar da app-server na ZCode ya gaza.                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ZCODE_RPC_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `30000`                          | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Wa'adin ƙarewar RPC na kowace buƙata (ms) don kiran app-server na ZCode.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ZCODE_TURN_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `120000`                         | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Matsakaicin tsawon lokaci (ms) na zagayen ZCode guda kafin mai sa ido ya ƙare lokacinsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ZCODE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`          | `250`                            | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Tazarar bincike (ms) don kammala zagayen ZCode.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `HERMES_HOME`                     | `~/.hermes`                      | `src/lib/cli-helper/config-generator/hermesHome.ts` | Kundin gida na Hermes Agent inda OmniRoute ke karantawa/rubuta daidaitawar Hermes CLI. Ya yi daidai da env var da mai sakawa na Hermes PowerShell ke saitawa a Windows (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\hermes`).                                                                                                 |

### Daidaita Bayanan Martabar CLI Kai-tsaye

Waɗannan tutocin fasali na zaɓi ne kuma a kashe suke ta tsohuwa. Haka kuma, ana iya kunna ko kashe su daga
dashboard na CLI Code.

| Sauyawa                               | Tsoho   | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                  | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Bayan daidaita samfurin mai samarwa, zai sake rubuta fayilolin bayanin martaba na `~/.codex/*.config.toml` kai-tsaye daga kundin bayanai mai aiki. Yana buƙatar `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ba ya taɓa canza daidaitawar Codex mai aiki/ta tsohuwa, tantancewa, saitunan Codex-lb, ko zaɓin mai samarwa.               |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Bayan daidaita samfurin mai samarwa, zai sake rubuta fayilolin bayanin martaba na Claude Code na `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` kai-tsaye daga kundin bayanai mai aiki. Yana buƙatar `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ba ya taɓa canza daidaitawar Claude mai aiki/ta tsohuwa, tantancewa, ko zaɓin mai samarwa. |

### Misalin Docker

```bash
# Haɗa binary na na'urar mai masaukin baki cikin kwantena sannan a gaya wa OmniRoute inda suke:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` yana aiki ne kawai idan an bind-mount ainihin path ɗin daga
host — haɗa shi da mounts kamar `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (duba profile na
`host` a cikin `docker-compose.yml`). Ana yin watsi da path da ba ya cikin home
na mai amfani da container kuma ba bind mount ba ne, domin duk abin da aka rubuta
a wurin zai ɓace idan aka sake ƙirƙirar container.

Image ɗin yana gudana a matsayin `USER node`, don haka `/root` da ba a mount ba
**ba** ingantaccen override ba ne.

| Variable                                 | Default       | Source File                         | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ---------------------------------------- | ------------- | ----------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(auto)_      | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Tilasta kunna gano container (`1`/`true`) ko kashe shi (`0`/`false`). Ana buƙatar wannan ne kawai a runtimes da auto-detection bai gano ba.                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Ba da damar rubuta config na kayan aikin CLI zuwa path na container da ba a mount ba duk da haka. Daidaitaccen zaɓin CLI shi ne `--allow-container-write`.                                                                                                                               |
| `DOCKER_UPDATE_TRIGGER_FILE`             | ba a saita ba | `src/lib/system/autoUpdate.ts`      | Image na Docker kawai: **Update Now** na dashboard yana rubuta request na JSON zuwa wannan file (dole ya kasance cikin `DATA_DIR`) domin watcher na ɓangaren host ya pull kuma ya sake ƙirƙirar container. Duba [Jagorar Docker](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#dashboard-updates-in-docker). |

### Mataimakan CLI Binary (`omniroute`)

Waɗannan variables suna daidaita halayen CLI binary na `omniroute` kansa (ba
gano sidecar da ke sama ba).

| Variable                              | Default                           | Source File                             | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`                      | _(system)_                        | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Tilasta harshen output na CLI. Locale na BCP-47 (misali `en`, `pt-BR`). Yana rinjayar env vars na locale na system (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Saita zuwa `1` don tura stdout/stderr na server zuwa terminal a supervised mode. Daidai yake da flag ɗin `--log` a `omniroute serve`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Token na machine-auth da ake saka a matsayin header na `x-omniroute-cli-token`. Ana samar da shi kai tsaye a task 8.12.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `GEMINI_CLI_HOME`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/commands/setup-gemini.mjs`     | Tushen home na Gemini mai ɗorewa; run na sau ɗaya yana amfani da home na wucin gadi da aka keɓe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHILD_SIGNAL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `5000`                            | `bin/cli/commands/run.mjs`              | Lokacin jira cikin milliseconds kafin a tilasta kashe child na CLI da aka aika wa signal.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SKIP_DEFAULT_DATA_ENV` | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Saita zuwa 1 tare da `DATA_DIR` da aka bayyana kai tsaye domin tsallake environment file na data-directory na default user.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYNC_TOKEN`                | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/commands/sync.mjs`             | Token na sync don `omniroute sync bundle` (`GET /api/sync/bundle` yana karɓar token na sync kawai, ba API key ba). Daidai yake da `--token`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000`                           | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Timeout na HTTP ga kowane attempt (ms) don requests daga CLI → server.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                           | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Matsakaicin lokacin (ms) da CLI ke jira endpoint na health na server kafin ya buga gargaɗin timeout. Yana da amfani ga cold starts masu jinkiri (misali Windows). Haka kuma ana iya saita shi ta `--ready-timeout`.                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`                   | `0`                               | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Saita zuwa `1` don buga bayanan diagnostics na retry/backoff zuwa stderr yayin umarnin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`               | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Custom directory don gano plugins na CLI (packages na `omniroute-cmd-*`). Yana amfani da `~/.omniroute/plugins/` ta default idan ba a saita ba. Na CLI kawai — ba ya taɓa isa scanner na plugins na ɓangaren server, wanda `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` ke nuna masa hanya (sashe na 2).                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_NO_UPDATE_NOTIFIER`        | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Hana sanarwar update ta CLI lokacin fita daga process idan an saita shi zuwa kowace ƙima marar fanko. Sanarwar tana ci gaba da kasancewa a kashe dabam a CI, quiet mode, da modes na output marasa rubutu.                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCTOR_LIVENESS_URL`       | _(an samo)_                       | `bin/cli/commands/doctor.mjs`           | Liveness URL da `omniroute doctor` ke gwadawa. Zaɓin `livenessUrl` na programmatic yana da fifiko; in ba haka ba ana amfani da wannan ƙimar kai tsaye. Idan ba a saita ba, CLI yana samo endpoint na `/api/health/degradation` na dashboard na gida.                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_CATALOG_PATH`     | `src/shared/constants/providers/` | `bin/cli/provider-catalog.mjs`          | Path na catalog na provider da umarnin provider na CLI ke amfani da shi. Zaɓin `catalogPath` na programmatic yana da fifiko. Ana amfani da absolute paths yadda suke; relative paths suna warwarewa daga tushen project. Idan ba a saita ba, CLI yana amfani da directory na constants na provider, tare da tsohon file na `providers.ts` a matsayin fallback. |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_TEST_MODEL`       | _(default na provider)_           | `bin/cli/provider-test.mjs`             | Model na fallback na gama-gari don gwaje-gwajen haɗin provider na CLI. Tsarin fifiko shi ne configured connection model, override na model na takamaiman provider, wannan ƙimar, sannan built-in test model na provider.                                                                                                                                       |
