## 10. Haɗaɗɗun Wakilin Ciki & MCP

| Sauyi                                           | Tsoho                                               | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`                            | ganowa ta atomatik                                  | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | URL da aka ayyana kai tsaye domin kayan aikin MCP/A2A su isa OmniRoute. Yana soke ganowa ta atomatik ta localhost.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | Manhajojin MCP/A2A                                          | Maɓallin API don kiran kayan aikin MCP na ciki da ƙwarewar A2A.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | ID na maɓalli don danganta rajistar binciken MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ROUTER_API_KEY`                                | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | Na gado                                                     | Laƙabin gado na `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS`          | `30`                                                | `src/lib/a2a/taskManager.ts`                                | Adadin kwanakin da ake adana tarihin ayyukan A2A a ma'ajiyar bayanai ta gida kafin sharewar yau da kullum ta share layi. Idan ba a saita ba, ba lamba ba ne, ko `<= 0`, za a koma kan `30`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_MEMORY_HITS`                     | `1`                                                 | `src/lib/a2a/taskExecution.ts`                              | Maɓallin kashe fasalin sa-ido na bugun ƙwaƙwalwar A2A. Saita zuwa `0` don tsallake binciken dawo da ƙwaƙwalwa gaba ɗaya ga wani aiki; kowace ƙima dabam (har da rashin saitawa) tana barin sa a kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                             | `src/app/api/issue-agent/runs/route.ts`                     | Yana kunna wurin ƙarshen tantance matsalolin da aka rikoda na Issue Agent a yanayin offline/na gida. Bar shi a kashe sai idan ana gudanar da tsarin aikin tantance rikodi na gida kai tsaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_TIMEOUT_MS`              | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `src/lib/issueAgent/execution.ts`                           | Iyakar lokacin jira (ms) don gudu guda na tantance rikodi na Issue Agent. Ana iyakance shi zuwa matsakaicin ƙima ta ciki; yana komawa kan tsohuwar ƙima da aka gina a ciki idan ba a saita shi ba ko ba ya inganci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT`                             | _(mahallin da ke aiki)_                             | `bin/cli/program.mjs`, `bin/cli/api.mjs`                    | Mahalli/furofayil na yanayin nesa na CLI don umarnin `omniroute`; yana soke mahallin da ke aiki a ma'ajiyar mahallai ta gida. Daidai yake da `--context <name>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT_KEYCHAIN_DISABLED`           | `0`                                                 | `bin/cli/contexts.mjs`                                      | Kashe backend na keychain na OS na `keytar` na zaɓi don bayanan shaidar mahallin CLI. Idan an kunna, bayanan shaidar za su ci gaba da kasancewa a yanayin `config.json` `0600`, kuma CLI zai fitar da gargaɗin komawa madadin sau ɗaya; an tanade shi don amfani da aka yi niyya a yanayin headless/container.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_ENFORCE_SCOPES`                  | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Tilasta ikon samun dama bisa scopes a kan kiran kayan aikin MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_SCOPES`                          | _(dukansu)_                                         | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Scopes da waƙafi ya raba: `admin`, `combos`, `health`, `models`, `routing`, `budget`, `metrics`, `pricing`, `memory`, `skills`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_COMPRESS_DESCRIPTIONS`           | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Matse bayanan kayan aikin MCP kafin jera manifest ɗin. Ƙimomin kunnawa: `1`, `true`, `on`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_DESCRIPTION_COMPRESSION`         | `rtk`                                               | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Algoritim/furofayil na matsewa. Ƙimomin kashewa: `0`, `false`, `off`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                | `10000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Kasafin lokacin katsewa (ms) don karatun gudanarwa na cikin MCP-server (lafiya, juriya, combos, ƙayyadadden amfani, amfani).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Kasafin lokacin yankewa (ms) don tsallaken MCP da ke jiran mai samarwa (`route_request`, `web_search`, `web_fetch`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE`                   | `5`                                                 | `src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`                         | Matsakaicin adadin instans na fihirisar local-corpus da ake adanawa a ƙwaƙwalwa (LRU, ɗaya ga kowace tushen kundin adireshi da aka yi wa fihirisa). Ana tilasta mafi ƙarancin ƙima ta zama `1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `MODEL_SYNC_INTERVAL_HOURS`                     | `6`                                                 | `src/shared/services/modelSyncScheduler.ts`                 | Tazarar aiki tare da kundin samfura a cikin sa'o'i.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES`         | `70`                                                | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Tazarar binciken iyakar ƙimar mai samarwa da ƙayyadadden kaso.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_SPACING_MS`               | `1500`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Tazara (ms) tsakanin ɗebo ƙayyadadden kaso na OAuth a jere yayin aiki tare na rukuni; ana ɗebo haɗin OAuth ɗaya bayan ɗaya don guje wa aika buƙatu da yawa lokaci guda zuwa uwar garken sama. `0` yana soke wannan (a lokaci guda).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`         | `250`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Mafi ƙarancin tazara (ms) tsakanin ɗebo ƙayyadadden kaso daga uwar garken sama a jere a kan hanyar binciken farko/sa ido ta kowace buƙata; yana rarraba kiran hanyar sadarwa da ke gudana lokaci guda don kada asusu da yawa a kan IP ɗaya su aika buƙatu da yawa lokaci guda zuwa uwar garken sama. An haɗa shi da masu ɗebo ƙayyadadden kaso na Codex (`/wham/usage`), DeepSeek, Bailian (duk wuraren ɗebo biyu), OpenCode, da Crof (#6009, #6911). Har yanzu ba a rufe hanyar turawa ta gama-gari `usage.ts::getUsageForProvider` (github/glm/minimax/nanogpt/xai/etc.) ba — ana bibiyarta daban. Ba ya shafar samun bayanai daga cache. `0` yana kashe shi; an takaita shi zuwa `0..60000`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_JITTER_MS`               | `120`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Ƙarin tazarar bazuwar kowace buƙata (ms), wadda ake samfuranta a kowane ɗebo ƙayyadadden kaso da aka keɓe daga uwar garken sama. Tsohuwar ƙima ita ce 120; an takaita zuwa `0..60000`; `0` yana cire jitter kaɗai. Saita `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS=0` don kashe dukkan tsarin takaita gudu. Jitter ba ya ba da tabbacin hana takunkumi daga uwar garken sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_POST_USAGE_REFRESH_DELAY_MS`   | `5000`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Jinkiri (ms) kafin sabunta iyakokin mai samarwa bayan ainihin aukuwar amfani, domin bai wa API na ƙayyadadden kaso na uwar garken sama lokaci ya yi rajistar amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | ganowa ta atomatik                                  | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`             | Cikakkiyar hanya zuwa executable na Chrome ko Edge na tsarin da ake amfani da shi don shiga Adobe Firefly ta mu'amala da sabuntawa a bayan allo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`                 | a kunne                                             | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Yana sabunta yanayin IMS da haɗarin burauza ta amfani da zaman Chrome CDP da aka keɓe ga asusu. Saita zuwa `0` don kashe sabuntawa ta burauza.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`                    | a kunne                                             | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Yana adana zaman Adobe da aka gyara a ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR` yayin sake fara aikin tsari. Saita zuwa `0` don riƙe zaman a ƙwaƙwalwa kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`               | `12000`                                             | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Mafi ƙarancin tazara a cikin milliseconds tsakanin aikawa don ƙirƙirawa na Adobe Firefly; `0` yana kashe tazarar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`              | `15000`                                             | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Ƙarin lokacin shiru a cikin milliseconds bayan kowane aikawa na Adobe na uku da ya yi nasara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS`            | `8000`                                              | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`                   | Tushen jinkirin sake-gwadawa a cikin milliseconds bayan martanin Adobe 408 na wucin gadi; ana haɗa shi da tazarar aikawa a cikin yunƙuri da ba su wuce biyar ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES`         | `false`                                             | `src/instrumentation-node.ts`                               | Kashe duk ayyukan bayan-fage (aiki tare, farashi, sabunta samfura). Yana da amfani ga CI/gwaji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEADLESS`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `src/lib/system/headless.ts`                                | Farawa da injin proxy kawai: yana tsallake ƙananan tsarin zaɓi, `/dashboard` → 404. Daidai yake da `omniroute serve --headless`. Duba [HEADLESS.md](../guides/HEADLESS.md).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_RUNTIME_BACKGROUND_TASKS`     | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `src/lib/config/runtimeSettings.ts`                         | Tilasta kunna ayyukan baya lokacin gano gwaji ta atomatik. Saita `1` don kauce wa hasashen gwajin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUDGET_RESET_JOB_INTERVAL_MS`        | `600000`                                            | `src/lib/jobs/budgetResetJob.ts`                            | Tazarar duba sake saitin kasafi (ms). Mafi ƙaranci `10000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

Saita `false` don ajiye tubalan a cikin ƙwaƙwalwa kawai. Yana sarrafa bayyanar setup wizard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

Ƙimar da ba sifili ba tana kunna tsallakewar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

### Gadar OAuth CLI (Na Ciki)

| Maɓalli             | Tsoho              | Fayil ɗin Tushe                 | Bayani                                                 |
| ------------------- | ------------------ | ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | ganowa ta atomatik | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | URL na uwar garke don gadar tantancewar CLI↔OmniRoute. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(ba a saita ba)_  | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Token na tantancewa don gadar CLI.                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`              | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | ID na mai amfani don zaman gadar CLI.                  |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(ba a saita ba)_  | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Tsohon suna na `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`.                     |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(ba a saita ba)_  | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Tsohon suna na `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.                      |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(ba a saita ba)_  | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Tsohon suna na `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`.                    |

## 11. Bayanan Shaidar Masu Bayar da OAuth

Bayanan shaida da aka gina a ciki don **haɓakawa a localhost**. Don turawa zuwa wuraren nesa, yi rajistar naka a na’urar sarrafa masu haɓakawa ta kowane mai bayarwa.

| Sauyi                                       | Mai bayarwa             | Bayanan kula                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLAUDE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Claude Code (Anthropic) | Abokin ciniki na jama’a — ba a buƙatar sirri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REDIRECT_URI`                  | Claude Code             | Sauya URI na turawa. Tsoho: `https://platform.claude.com/oauth/code/callback`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CODEX_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Codex / OpenAI          | Abokin ciniki na jama’a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Gemini (Google)         | Yana buƙatar `_SECRET` mai dacewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | Gemini (Google)         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `KIMI_CODING_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Kimi Coding (Moonshot)  | Abokin ciniki na jama’a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MUSE_CODE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                 | Muse Code (Meta)        | Zaɓin sauyawa don id na abokin cinikin tsarin na’ura na Muse CLI na jama’a. Bar shi ba a saita ba don amfani da abokin cinikin jama’a da aka gina a ciki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Antigravity (Google)    | Yana buƙatar `_SECRET` mai dacewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`           | Antigravity (Google)    | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot          | Abokin ciniki na jama’a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `GHE_COPILOT_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | GHE Copilot             | Zaɓin sauyawa don id na abokin cinikin OAuth na GitHub Enterprise Copilot. Idan ba a saita shi ba, zai koma ga tsohon abokin ciniki na jama’a na `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `COPILOT_INTEGRATION_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot          | Zaɓin sauyawa don ID na haɗin abokin cinikin GitHub Copilot da ake aikawa a cikin kanun `Copilot-Integration-Id` da `Editor-Plugin-Version`. Tsohonsa shi ne `copilot-developer-cli`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `WINDSURF_API_KEY`                          | Windsurf / Devin (v3.8) | Madadin maɓallin API da `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` ke amfani da shi idan babu bayanan shaidar kowace haɗi. Na zaɓi ne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                             | Devin CLI (v3.8)        | Keɓantacciyar hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin binary na Devin CLI (`devin`). Ana tantance ta ta `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | ID na abokin cinikin OAuth don GitLab Duo. Yi rajistar manhaja a `https://gitlab.com/-/profile/applications` tare da URI na turawa `<NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL>/callback` da izini `api, read_user, openid, profile, email`. Zai koma ga `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`            | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Sirrin abokin cinikin OAuth don GitLab Duo. Na zaɓi ne — tsarin PKCE ba ya buƙatar sirri. Zai koma ga `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`                       | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Sauya tushen URL na GitLab (GitLab da ake masaukinsa da kai). Tsohonsa shi ne `https://gitlab.com`. Zai koma ga `GITLAB_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GITLAB_BASE_URL`                           | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Tsohon madadin `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`. Ana amfani da shi idan ba a saita nau'in `_DUO_` ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Tsohon madadin `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` wanda `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Tsohon madadin `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` wanda `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                 | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `QODER_OAUTH_AUTHORIZE_URL`                 | Qoder                   | Saita shi don kunna Qoder OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `QODER_OAUTH_TOKEN_URL`                     | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `QODER_OAUTH_USERINFO_URL`                  | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `QODER_PERSONAL_ACCESS_TOKEN`               | Qoder                   | Madadin maɓallin API kai tsaye (yana ƙetare OAuth).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`                       | Qoder                   | ID na filin aiki don Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_QODER_WORKSPACE`                 | Qoder                   | Wani suna na `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `QODER_CLI_CONFIG_DIR`                      | Qoder                   | Sauya kundin saitunan Qoder CLI (zaman PAT mai keɓantuwa, yana hana lalata shiga ta burauza).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `QODER_CLI_MAX_CONCURRENCY`                 | Qoder                   | Matsakaicin yawan ayyukan qodercli da za su gudana lokaci guda (ƙaddamarwar print ko buƙatun ACP); ƙarin buƙatu suna shiga jerin jiran FIFO. Idan ba a saita ba (tsoho) = babu iyaka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `QODER_CLI_QUEUE_TIMEOUT_MS`                | Qoder                   | Tsawon lokacin da buƙatar Qoder za ta jira gurbin qodercli (kawai idan an saita iyaka) kafin ta gaza da 429 `qodercli_busy` + `Retry-After`. Tsoho `120000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `QODER_CLI_TRANSPORT`                       | Qoder                   | `print` (tsoho) yana ƙaddamar da `qodercli --print` ga kowace buƙata; `acp` yana riƙe da tsari guda na `qodercli --acp` mai gudana na dindindin ga kowane PAT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | Qoder                   | Jigilar ACP: kashe tsarin qodercli mai gudana na dindindin bayan ya kasance ba ya aiki na wannan tsawon lokaci. Tsoho `600000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_MS`          | Qoder                   | Jigilar ACP: wa'adin da qodercli zai amsa ACP `initialize`. Tsoho `60000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_MAX_SESSIONS_PER_PROCESS`    | Qoder                   | Jigilar ACP: sake ƙaddamar da tsarin qodercli mai gudana na dindindin bayan wannan adadin zaman. Tsoho `500`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `BLACKBOX_WEB_VALIDATED_TOKEN`              | Blackbox Web            | Token na `tk` na frontend da za a aika a matsayin `validated` zuwa `/api/chat`. Ana buƙatarsa idan Blackbox yana tilasta daidaituwar token; idan ba haka ba, OmniRoute yana komawa ga UUID na bazuwar. Duba matsala #2252.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_BASE_URL`                    | Kariyar Vision Bridge   | OpenAI-compatible base URL don kiraye-kirayen vision-bridge da ba na Anthropic ba. Tsohon OpenAI URL env ko api.openai.com ne tsohon ƙimarsa. Nuna shi zuwa self-loop na OmniRoute na `/v1` ko duk wani OpenAI-compat endpoint (Gemini OpenAI-compat, OpenRouter). Batu #2232. Lokacin da URL ɗin yake `/v1` na OmniRoute kansa, ƙaramin buƙatar describe yana aika `x-omniroute-admission-bypass: internal` kuma yana tabbatar da izini da resolved self-loop credential (`sk_omniroute` sentinel a local mode, ko `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY` / `ROUTER_API_KEY` — #1350) domin turawar `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` ta yi aiki. |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_API_KEY`                     | Kariyar Vision Bridge   | API key don URL da ke sama. Yana maye gurbin OpenAI / Google env vars na kowane provider don kiraye-kirayen vision-bridge da ba na Anthropic ba. Anthropic models suna ci gaba da amfani da keɓaɓɓen hanyar Anthropic key ɗinsu. Batu #2232.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_VISION_BRIDGE_NEGATIVE_CACHE_MS` | Kariyar Vision Bridge   | Tsawon lokacin da ake adana sakamakon routing na "babu candidate mai amfani" a cache, cikin ms (tsohon ƙima `30000`). Ƙimomi marasa inganci ko mara kyau suna komawa ga tsohon ƙima; `0` yana kashe negative cache. Tushe: `src/lib/guardrails/visionBridgeRouter.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

> [!WARNING]
>
> 1. Je zuwa [Google Cloud Console → Credentials](https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials)
> 2. Ƙirƙiri OAuth 2.0 Client ID (nau'i: "Web application")
> 3. Ƙara URL na server ɗinka a matsayin Authorized redirect URI
> 4. Sauya ƙimomin credential a cikin `.env`.

---

## 12. Sauya Ƙimar User-Agent ta Mai Bayarwa

Sauya header na `User-Agent` da ake aikawa zuwa kowane mai bayarwa na upstream. Executor base class ne ke warware wannan a lokacin gudanarwa:

```
process.env[`${PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`]
```

> **Tushe:** `open-sse/executors/base.ts` → `buildHeaders()`

| Variable | Ƙimar Tsoho | Lokacin Sabuntawa |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` | `claude-cli/2.1.280 (external, cli)` | Lokacin da Anthropic ya fitar da sabon sigar CLI |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_TOOL_NAME_CLOAK` | `false` | `executors/base.ts` + `executors/cliproxyapi.ts` | Saita zuwa `1`/`true` don tura sunayen kayan aikin harness na ɓangare na uku kai tsaye zuwa Anthropic a duk hanyoyin da ke zuwa Anthropic (OAuth na asali da CLIProxyAPI). Ta tsohuwa, executor yana sanya wa sunayen kayan aikin da ba na Claude Code ba laƙabi ta ƙayyadaddiyar hanya (yana amfani da daidaitaccen taswirar Claude Code idan akwai, in ba haka ba PascalCase), sannan ya mayar da su yadda suke a cikin amsa ta hanyar `_toolNameMap`, don kada a ƙi harnesses masu kayan aikin snake_case a matsayin clients na ɓangare na uku da aka gane ta fingerprint. Don debugging kawai. |
| `CODEX_USER_AGENT` | `codex-cli/0.155.0 (Windows 10.0.26200; x64)` | Lokacin da OpenAI ya sabunta Codex CLI |
| `CODEX_CLIENT_VERSION` | `0.155.0` | Sauya sigar Codex client dabam da cikakken rubutun UA |
| `GROK_CLI_CLIENT_VERSION` | `1.0.41` | Sauya `x-grok-client-version` don inference, model sync, da device-code OAuth, tare da tsohon Grok CLI User-Agent don inference da model sync. Yana karɓar SemVer tare da metadata na prerelease/build na zaɓi, har zuwa haruffa 128 bayan cire whitespace da ke kewaye. Ƙimomin da ba a saita ba, marasa komai, marasa inganci, ko masu ɗauke da line break suna amfani da pin ɗin da aka zo da shi. Sauyin cikakken inference User-Agent da yake akwai yana ci gaba da samun fifiko. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` | `2.1.280` | Sauya sigar Claude Code da ake sanarwa dabam da `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT`. Anthropic yana ƙuntata wasu models bisa wannan ƙimar (#12417). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_BUILD_REVISION` | `1e2` | Sauya suffix mai haruffa 3 da OmniRoute ke liƙawa ga `cc_version=` a cikin Claude billing block. Ƙara shi tare da `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` — pinning na version kaɗai yana sanar da haɗin `version.revision` wanda babu wani binary na ainihi da ke fitarwa. |
| `GITHUB_COPILOT_CLI_VERSION` | `1.0.91` | Sauya sigar Copilot CLI da ake sanarwa dabam da `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` |
| `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` | `GitHubCopilotChat/0.54.0` | Lokacin da GitHub Copilot Chat ya sabunta |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_USER_AGENT` | `antigravity/2.0.1 darwin/arm64` | Lokacin da Antigravity IDE ya sabunta |
| `KIRO_USER_AGENT` | `AWS-SDK-JS/3.0.0 kiro-ide/1.0.0` | Lokacin da Kiro IDE ya sabunta |
| `KIRO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | `kiro-cli` | Sauya `clientId` (public id) na Kiro social device-code |
| `KIRO_VERIFY_FULL_CRC` | `false` | Shiga bisa zaɓi: cikakken tabbatar da CRC na kowane frame a Kiro event stream (don debugging na streams da suka lalace) |
| `KIRO_CREDIT_PRICE_USD` | `0.02` | USD ga kowane Kiro credit; yana sauya credits na `meteringEvent` zuwa ainihin kuɗin kowace request |
| `KIRO_REQUIRE_CREDITS` | `false` | Sa Kiro response ya gaza idan bai bayar da rahoton credit metering ba maimakon yin rikodinsa a matsayin wanda ba a caji ba |
| `QODER_USER_AGENT` | `Qoder-Cli` | Lokacin da Qoder CLI ya sabunta |
| `CURSOR_USER_AGENT` | `Cursor/3.3` | Lokacin da Cursor ya sabunta |

> [!TIP]
> Za ka iya ƙara sauya ƙimar User-Agent ga **kowane** mai bayarwa ta amfani da tsarin `{PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`. Executor yana ƙirƙirar sunan env var a lokacin gudanarwa.
