## 13. Daidaituwar Sawun Yatsa na CLI

Idan aka kunna, OmniRoute yana sake tsara taken HTTP da filayen jikin JSON domin su dace da ainihin sa hannun kayan aikin CLI na hukuma. Wannan yana rage haɗarin yi wa asusu alama, tare da kiyaye IP na proxy ɗinka.

**Tushe:** `open-sse/config/cliFingerprints.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`

### Na Kowane Mai Bayarwa

| Sauyi                    | Kunnawa | Tasiri                                           |
| ------------------------ | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CODEX`       | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Codex CLI      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLAUDE`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Claude Code    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_GITHUB`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar GitHub Copilot |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ANTIGRAVITY` | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Antigravity    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CURSOR`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Cursor         |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIMI_CODING` | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Kimi Coding    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KILOCODE`    | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Kilo Code      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLINE`       | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Cline          |

### Na Gabaɗaya

| Sauyi            | Kunnawa | Tasiri                                                             |
| ---------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ALL` | `=1`    | Kunna daidaituwar sawun yatsa ga **duk** masu bayarwa lokaci guda. |

### Sauya bayanan asalin Kimi Coding CLI

| Sauyi                   | Tsoho                   | Fayil ɗin Tushe                          | Bayani                                                                                |
| ----------------------- | ----------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KIMI_CLI_VERSION`      | `1.36.0`                | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Sauya sigar Kimi CLI da ake aikawa yayin kiran OAuth/API.                             |
| `KIMI_CODING_DEVICE_ID` | _(tsohon da aka ɗauka)_ | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Sauya ID na na'urar Kimi da aka ɗauka, wanda ake amfani da shi a taken abokin ciniki. |

> [!NOTE]
> Wannan fasalin yana aiki tare da sauye-sauyen User-Agent (§12). Tsarin sawun yatsa yana kula da jerin taken da jerin filayen jiki, yayin da sauye-sauyen User-Agent ke kula da takamaiman zaren UA. Ana iya kunna kowannensu da kansa.

---

## 14. Masu Bayar da Maɓallin API

Maɓallan API na masu bayarwa waɗanda ke amfani da tantancewa kai tsaye. **Saitin da aka fi so:** Dashboard → Providers → Add API Key.

Saita su ta hanyar sauye-sauyen muhalli wata hanya ce ta daban don girkawar Docker ko tsarin da ba shi da dubawar allo.

Tsarin da ake ganewa: `{PROVIDER_ID}_API_KEY`

| Sauyi              | Mai Bayarwa                                         |
| ------------------ | --------------------------------------------------- |
| `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` | DeepSeek                                            |
| `NVIDIA_API_KEY`   | NVIDIA NIM                                          |
| `JINA_AI_API_KEY`  | Jina AI (Foundation API + madadin Reader)           |
| `JINA_API_KEY`     | Jina AI (laƙabi na `JINA_AI_API_KEY`)               |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY`   | Gemini (Google AI Studio) embeddings + madadin taɗi |
| `GOOGLE_API_KEY`   | Gemini (laƙabi na `GEMINI_API_KEY`)                 |

> [!NOTE]
> An cire shigarwar `${PROVIDER}_API_KEY` na dindindin don Groq, xAI, Mistral, Perplexity, Together AI, Fireworks, Cerebras, Cohere, Nebius, da Qianfan a v3.8.0 saboda runtime ba ya ƙara karanta su — waɗannan masu bayarwa suna dogara ne kawai da Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / DB mai ɓoyewa. Duba sashen _Bincike: Sauye-sauyen da Aka Cire / Marasa Amfani_ a ƙasan wannan takarda don hanyar ƙaura.

> [!TIP]
> Maɓallan da aka saita ta Dashboard ana adana su da ɓoyewa a SQLite kuma suna da fifiko kan sauye-sauyen muhalli.
>
> **Jina:** embeddings na `jina-ai/…`, rerank, classify, segment, da `jina-search` ba sa cajin maɓallin muhallin cluster idan akwai haɗin dashboard na `jina-ai` (ko `jina-reader` na bai ɗaya) — `getProviderCredentials` yana cike na farko. Ana amfani da `JINA_AI_API_KEY` / `JINA_API_KEY` ne kawai idan babu maɓallin dashboard mai amfani. Rajistan kira yana danganta madadin muhalli da `connection_id=env:JINA_AI_API_KEY`. Katin Reader (`jina-reader`, `r.jina.ai`) ba ya taɓa ba da `/v1/embeddings` ko `/v1/rerank`.
>
> **Gemini:** `gemini/gemini-embedding-2` (laƙabi `google/gemini-embedding-2`) yana fara amfani da haɗin `gemini` na dashboard. Ana amfani da `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GOOGLE_API_KEY` ne kawai idan babu maɓallin dashboard mai amfani. Rajistan kira yana danganta madadin muhalli da `connection_id=env:GEMINI_API_KEY`. Zirga-zirgar multimodal ta asali tana amfani da `x-goog-api-key` zuwa `:embedContent` / `:batchEmbedContents` — abubuwan OpenAI `input` guda N suna zama vectors guda N.

---

## 15. Saitunan Lokacin Jira

Dukkan ƙimomi suna cikin **milisakanni**. Ana warware su daga wuri guda a `src/shared/utils/runtimeTimeouts.ts`.

### Tsarin Fifikon Lokacin Jira

```
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (sauyin maye gurbi na gaba ɗaya)
├─→ FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (kiran masu samarwa na waje, tsoho: 600000)
│   ├─→ FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   │   └── TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 10000)
│   ├── RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 15000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 0 = a kashe, shawara: 30000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 2)
│   ├── FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 30000)
│   └── FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 4000)
├─→ STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, tsoho: 600000)
├─→ STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 1260000; 0 yana kashe shi)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, tsoho: 80000)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS (yana iyakance ƙarin lokacin shirye-shirye mai daidaitawa, tsoho: 180000)
└─→ API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, tsoho: 30000)
    ├─→ API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (wanda aka samo, tsoho: 300000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (tsoho: 60000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (tsoho: 5000)
    └── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS (tsoho: 0 = a kashe)
```

| Sauyawa                                         | Tsoho                                                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Gajeriyar hanya ta gama-gari — tana maye gurbin tsoffin ƙimomin `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` da `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                              | `600000`                                                          | Jimillar iyakar lokacin buƙatar HTTP don kiran masu samarwa na sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | `600000`                                                          | Matsakaicin lokacin shiru tsakanin ɗanyen bayanan byte na sama kafin a katse. Samfuran tunani mai zurfi ba safai suke dakatawa fiye da sakan 90 ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `1260000`                                                         | Matsakaicin jimillar tsawon rayuwar rafin SSE mai aiki; ba ya sake farawa saboda bayanan byte na sama kuma ba ya dogara da `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. An samo shi daga mafi girman `timeoutMs` na kowane samfuri a rajista (1200000, Codex) tare da ƙarin tazara ta 60000, don haka ba za a taɓa kashe samfurin da aka ba shi damar amfani da cikakken kasafinsa a tsakiyar amsa ba. Saita zuwa `0` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SSE_COMMENTS`                        | _(a kashe)_                                                       | Ko OmniRoute na iya fitar da layukan sharhin SSE na `:` (misali bugun-zuciyar `: keepalive` da ƙarin bayanan metadata na `x-omniroute-*`). An kashe shi ta tsohuwa (#10524), domin tsauraran abokan ciniki masu dacewa da OpenAI suna yin JSON.parse ga kowane layin SSE kuma su lalace a kan sharhin `:`; bugun-zuciyar `data:` ba ya shafuwa. Saita `on`/`true`/`1`/`yes` don sake shiga. `open-sse/utils/sseHeartbeat.ts` ne ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SSE_COALESCE_MS`                               | `0` (a kashe)                                                     | Zaɓin shiga: haɗa abubuwan Claude `content_block_delta` masu jere (masu fihirisar tubali da nau'in delta iri ɗaya) da ake aika wa abokan cinikin Claude, tare da tura abin da aka tara aƙalla duk bayan ms N. Ba a rasa komai; ba a taɓa haɗa `signature_delta` da abubuwan iyaka ba. Ya shafi kowane nau'in delta sai dai idan an saita sauyawa na takamaiman nau'i da ke ƙasa. `open-sse/utils/sseDeltaCoalesce.ts` ne ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SSE_COALESCE_THINKING_MS`                      | `0` (a kashe)                                                     | Maye gurbin `SSE_COALESCE_MS` na takamaiman nau'i don `thinking_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SSE_COALESCE_TEXT_MS`                          | `0` (a kashe)                                                     | Maye gurbin `SSE_COALESCE_MS` na takamaiman nau'i don `text_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SSE_COALESCE_INPUT_JSON_MS`                    | `0` (a kashe)                                                     | Maye gurbin `SSE_COALESCE_MS` na takamaiman nau'i don `input_json_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `SSE_FRAME_COALESCE_MS`                         | `0` (a kashe)                                                     | Zaɓin shiga: tara firam-firam na SSE da aka tura cikin ms N zuwa rubutu guda ɗaya ga abokin ciniki. Ba a taɓa jinkirta firam na farko da firam na ƙarshe (`[DONE]`, `message_stop`, `error`, `response.completed`/`failed`/`incomplete`) ba; idan an kunna tarawa, ITL yana auna tazarar tsakanin tarin. `open-sse/utils/sseOutputSink.ts` ne ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SSE_STALL_WATCHDOG`                            | `timer`                                                           | Aiwatar da mai sa ido kan tsayawar byte don amsoshin da ake watsawa. `timer` yana sake saita agogo guda ɗaya ga kowane ɓangaren bayanan sama (yana kunna daidai lokacin da kasafin tsayawa ya cika); `interval` yana riƙe dubawa guda ɗaya ga kowane rafi mai zangon `clamp(budget/4, 1s, 5s)`, don haka tsayawa zai kunna cikin kasafi + zango. `open-sse/utils/streamStallWatchdog.ts` ne ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `80000`                                                           | Lokacin karɓar abin SSE na farko wanda ba ping ba. Yana gaji `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` idan an saita shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `180000`                                                          | Matsakaicin taga mai daidaituwa na jiran shirye-shiryen abin farko don manyan buƙatun watsawa, masu yawan amfani da kayan aiki, ko masu zurfin tunani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_POLICY_ENABLED`           | `true`                                                            | Maɓallin kashewa na heuristic ɗin `/goal`. Saita `false`/`0`/`off` don kashe ganowa gaba ɗaya — ba za a taɓa ƙara iyakokin lokacin shirye-shirye da farfaɗo da rafi saboda jikin buƙata/kanun buƙata ba, wanda ke rage haɗarin abokin ciniki ya tsawaita iyakar lokaci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS` | `600000`                                                          | Matsakaicin taga na jiran shirye-shiryen abin farko don gudanarwar wakilin `/goal` da aka gano ko buƙatun da aka tilasta da `x-omniroute-agent-goal`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_STREAM_RECOVERY`          | `true`                                                            | Kunna farfaɗo da rafi da wuri ta atomatik don gudanarwar wakilin `/goal` da aka gano. Saita `false`/`0`/`off` don kashe zaɓin shiga na musamman ga manufa. Wannan zai iya ƘARA farfaɗowa ne kawai a saman tsohon saitin mai gudanarwa — ba ya taɓa soke fitowar da aka bayyana sarai ta saitunan `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`/DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_DROP_NONSTANDARD_EVENTS`       | `true`                                                            | Cire abubuwan SSE marasa daidaito na `codex.*` (misali `codex.rate_limits`) waɗanda ke karya `responses.stream()` na OpenAI SDK da 502. Yana kunne ta tsohuwa (#11014). Saita `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` don tura su gaba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Zaɓin shiga don jigilar Codex app-server. Madogarar WebSocket (`ws://`/`wss://`) ta sidecar na `codex app-server` na gida. Idan aka saita shi tare da token, ana bi da buƙatun Codex ta JSON-RPC zuwa sidecar maimakon HTTP Responses API. Haka kuma ana iya saita shi ga kowace haɗi ta `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerUrl`. `open-sse/executors/codex/appServerConfig.ts` ne ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Token na capability/bearer da aka saka kai tsaye, wanda ake gabatarwa ga app-server. Maye gurbin kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerToken`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN_FILE`       | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Hanyar fayil ɗin da ke ɗauke da token ɗin damar app-server (daga `codex app-server --ws-token-file`). Ana amfani da shi idan ba a saita `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN` ba. Sauyin saitin kowace haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerTokenFile`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_CWD`                 | `/tmp`                                                            | Kundin aiki da zagayen app-server ke gudana a ciki. Sauyin saitin kowace haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerCwd`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_APPROVAL`            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Manufofin amincewa da ake miƙawa zagayen app-server (misali `never`, `on-request`). Sauyin saitin kowace haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerApprovalPolicy`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_SANDBOX`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Manufofin sandbox da ake miƙawa zagayen app-server (misali `read-only`, `workspace-write`, `danger-full-access`). Idan ba a saita ba, mai aiwatarwa yana amfani da `workspace-write` a matsayin tsoho (an ƙarfafa tsaro; a baya `danger-full-access`). Sauyin saitin kowace haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerSandbox`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_AUTO_APPROVE`        | `false`                                                           | Amince ta atomatik da buƙatun amincewa na app-server kansa (aiwatar da umarni/fayil/izini a kan host). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — ana ƙin buƙatun ta atomatik; kiran kayan aikin harness bai shafu ba (suna bi ta hanyar wucewa ta daban ta `item/tool/call`). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`yes`. Sauyin saitin kowace haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerAutoApprove`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin karɓar headers na amsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_FETCH_START_TIMEOUT_CAP_MS`          | `110000`                                                          | Matsakaicin lokacin jiran headers na amsa a buƙatun streaming (ms). `0` yana kashe wannan iyaka; takamaiman `fetchStartTimeoutCapMs` na provider yana da fifiko.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000` (30s)                                                     | Matsakaicin lokacin jiran fara amsa (ms) ga kowace yunƙurin kai-tsaye ba tare da proxy ba. Idan lokaci ya ƙare, za a sake gwadawa sau ɗaya a sabon socket; saita `0` don kashe iyakar da kuma riƙe halin da ya gabata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`     | `300000` (minti 5)                                                | Mafi ƙarancin lokacin jiran fara amsa (ms) ga yunƙurin kai-tsaye ba tare da proxy ba waɗanda host ɗin da ake nufi na sirri/LAN/loopback ne (misali `localhost`, `host.docker.internal`, `192.168.x.x`), domin kada tsohon iyakar 30s da ke sama ya katse jinkirin farawar Ollama na asali. Yana aiki a matsayin mafi ƙarancin iyaka: ainihin lokacin jira shi ne mafi girma tsakanin wannan ƙimar da `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`; `0` ko ƙima marar inganci yana cire mafi ƙarancin iyakar gida. Wannan bai shafi hosts na nesa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_RESPONSE_RETRY_TIMEOUT_MS`    | `600000` (minti 10)                                               | Matsakaicin iyaka (ms) ga yunƙurin RETRY ta sabon socket bayan ƙarewar lokacin fara amsa na yunƙurin pooled da ke sama (#13703). Yana aiki ne kawai idan mai kira ya riga ya haɗa nasa siginar wa'adin ƙarewa (jerin fifikon connection/model/provider/`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` da aka warware); wannan siginar ce ainihin iyaka kuma ita ce ke fara aiki a hanyar da aka nufa, don haka wannan kariyar baya ce mai yalwa maimakon tsayayyen iyaka — ba tare da ita ba, sake gwadawar tana sake amfani da gajeren taga iri ɗaya na `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS` kamar yunƙurin pooled, kuma tana haifar da kuskuren 504 ga reasoning models masu lafiya amma masu jinkirin TTFB. Ba a taɓa yarda ta yi ƙasa da tsayayyen mafi ƙarancin iyakar da ke sama ba; idan mai kira bai samar da siginar wa'adin ƙarewa ko kaɗan ba, sake gwadawar tana barin tsayayyen mafi ƙarancin iyakar yadda yake. |
| `FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin karɓar cikakken jikin amsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `30000`                                                           | Lokacin ƙarewar kafa haɗin TCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                    | `4000`                                                            | Lokacin ƙarewar rashin aikin socket na keep-alive.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin ƙarewar proxy na TLS fingerprint (wreq-js).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS`                    | `10000`                                                           | Yana iyakance lokacin jira zuwa byte na farko musamman a jikin transport na TLS-fingerprint na wreq-js; `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS` kaɗai ba zai iya gano jikin da ya tsaya cik ba saboda yana kammala da zarar headers sun iso (#12656). Ƙarewar lokaci tana soke mai karatun wreq sannan ta koma ga dispatcher na kai-tsaye/proxy; `0` yana kashe watchdog.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `15000`                                                           | Na mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai, kuma kawai yayin da feature flag na `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_STALL_ROTATION` yake kunne (a kashe yake ta tsohuwa): yana iyakance jiran byte na farko na jikin amsar Responses da ake streaming bayan headers ɗinta (#13484). Stream na Responses yana farawa da `response.created`, don haka yin shiru bayan wannan taga yana nuna tsayawa cik: ana sanyaya asusun sannan a juya buƙatar zuwa asusu na gaba sau ɗaya; tsayawa cik na biyu yana sa ta gaza nan take. `0` yana kashe kariyar ko da flag ɗin yana kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS`            | `0` (= a kashe)                                                   | Na mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: yana iyakance jiran headers na amsa daga upstream a kiran Responses da ake streaming idan har yanzu akwai wani asusu — buƙatar da ke cikin layi tana daina jira ta wuce gaba maimakon riƙe dukkan tagar jiran headers. Kiran Responses da ake streaming kawai abin ya shafa (stream na Responses yana farawa da `response.created`, don haka yin shiru a nan yana nuna layin jira ne, ba samarwa ba); chat completions da kiraye-kirayen da ba na streaming ba ba su shafu, kuma tagar tana rage ainihin iyakar fara fetch ne kawai, ba ta taɓa tsawaita shi ba. Ƙimar da aka ba da shawara yayin kunnawa: `30000`. `0` yana kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS` | `2`                                                               | Mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: sau nawa a kowace buƙata iyakar jiran kanun da ke sama za ta iya matsawa zuwa asusu na gaba. Asusun ƙarshe da ya rage koyaushe yana riƙe cikakkiyar taga ta kanun.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_AND_RESUME`                      | `false`                                                           | Mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: ajiye buƙatar tare da siginar ci-gaba bayan maimaitattun 429 na wucin gadi (ko sabon alamar matsin-pool), sannan a sake aiwatar da zagaye guda mai iyaka na asusu har 3 a jere maimakon bazawa ga dukkan rukunin (#13924). A kashe ta tsohuwa: kowane 429 yana sauyawa zuwa asusu na gaba daidai kamar da.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_REPLAY_SERVE_SETASIDE`           | `false`                                                           | Mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: idan kowane asusun da ya shirya bayan lokacin hucewa a zagayen sake kunnawa na ajiye-da-ci-gaba an ware shi saboda ƙwaƙwalwar ƙin amincewar proxy, a yi amfani da su duk da haka maimakon kada a sake aiwatar da komai (#14851, ci-gaba ga #14750). A kashe ta tsohuwa: zagayen sake kunnawa ba shi da komai a wannan yanayin, kamar kafin #14750.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `STREAM_READINESS_STALL_RETRY`                  | `false`                                                           | Taɗi mai gudana kawai: idan jikin farko na upstream ya tsaya kafin samar da abin da za a iya amfani da shi, a yi ƙoƙari na biyu guda mai iyaka ta hanyar hanyar routing ɗin nan ɗin, da kasafin shirye-shirye iri ɗaya kuma ba tare da hukunta asusu ba. A kashe ta tsohuwa: tsayawar jiki na farko yana sa buƙatar ta gaza ba tare da sake gwadawa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_RESELECT`                        | `false`                                                           | Mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: bayan 429 daga mai samarwa da aka rarraba bisa egress a kan asusun da ba shi da proxy ƙarƙashin mahallin pool na yanayi, a nemi connection pool ya ba da wani memba don ƙoƙari na gaba maimakon sake gwada adireshin egress ɗin nan. Yana tsara fifiko ne, ba ya warewa: pool da ya ƙare yana ci gaba da halin yanzu. A kashe ta tsohuwa: kowane 429 yana sauyawa zuwa asusu na gaba daidai kamar da.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_STRAIN_MARKER_PATH`              | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: sauya path na alamar matsin-pool da ake karantawa kafin ajiye buƙata (`{since, reason, ttl_s}`, tsoho `/tmp/opencode-pool-strain.json`, #13924). Sabuwar alama tana sa a ajiye ba tare da sake ƙirgawa ba; idan babu ko ta tsufa, sai a koma ga ma’aunin burst.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

Relay da ya maƙale dole ne ya gaza kafin lokacin ƙarewar client/agent (~30s), domin masu kira su ga gazawa ta musamman ga relay maimakon lokacin ƙarewar upstream na gaba ɗaya. | `OMNIROUTE_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS`                    | `10`                                                              | Lokacin jira na gama-gari kafin sake gwadawa ga hanyoyin sake-gwadawa-sau-ɗaya na direct/relay/proxy a cikin `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). `0` = sake gwadawa nan take.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin rangwamen ƙayyadadden wa'adin JS da ake ɗorawa a kan lokacin ƙarewa na asali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — Perplexity yana yin bincike ko ta yaya, kuma jimlar tana bayyana a cikin amsoshi a matsayin sharhin-meta ga kwastomomin shirye-shirye. | `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin rangwamen ƙayyadadden wa'adin JS da ake ɗorawa a kan lokacin ƙarewa na asali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

| `OBSCURA_BIN`                                   | `auto-detect`                                                     | Hanyar zuwa binary na `obscura` da rumbun burauza da mai aiwatar da Cloudflare Playground suke amfani da shi a matsayin injin farko (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`); ana gano shi ta atomatik daga PATH na tsarin idan ba a saita ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OBSCURA_CDP_ENDPOINT`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Nuna zuwa Obscura da yake gudana tuni (`http://host:port`) maimakon ƙaddamar da wani; wannan module ɗin ba shi ne mamallakin wannan tsari ba (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OBSCURA_PORT`                                  | `random free port`                                                | Tashar jiragen ruwa da aka ayyana sarai don `obscura serve` da aka ƙaddamar; ana zaɓar wata tashar da babu mai amfani da ita ta atomatik idan ba a saita ba (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `WEB_COOKIE_USE_BROWSER`                        | `0`                                                               | Shigar da buƙatar hirar kuki-na-yanar-gizo cikin hanyar da ke amfani da burauza (`browserBackedChat.ts`); `1` don kunnawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL`                             | `https://www.kimi.ai`                                             | URL na tushe don mai aiwatar da Kimi Web (Connect-RPC na kimi.ai na ƙasa da ƙasa) (`kimi-web.ts`); sauya shi kawai don wuraren madubi/proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `KIMI_WEB_CHAT_URL`                             | `<KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL>/apiv2/kimi.gateway.chat.v1.ChatService/Chat` | Cikakken wurin ƙarshen hira don mai aiwatar da Kimi Web (`kimi-web.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | _(ana gano shi ta atomatik)_                                      | Hanyar zuwa executable na Chrome/Edge na tsarin don shiga ta burauza mai hulɗa ta Adobe Firefly (`adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`); tana maye gurbin ganowa ta atomatik bisa kowane OS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                      | _fitowar standalone ta .build/_                                   | Sauyin lokacin gini don kundin adireshin fitowar standalone da matakin haɗa-wuri bayan gini ke amfani da shi (`scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`); kayan aikin gini ne, ba na lokacin aiki ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

Ƙoƙarin manufa na combo suna gaji lokacin ƙarewar buƙatar upstream da aka tantance (`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`, ko
`REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` lokacin da shi ke samar da tsohuwar ƙimar fetch). Saita `targetTimeoutMs` a cikin combo,
tsofaffin ƙimomin combo, ko sauyin mai samarwa kawai don sa komawar combo ta fi sauri; ƙimomin da suka wuce
lokacin ƙarewar upstream na yanzu ana iyakance su zuwa lokacin ƙarewar upstream.

`comboTimeoutMs` wani kasafin lokacin bango ne daban na dukan combo a duk faɗin manufofin failover.
Bar shi ba a saita ba ko `0` don ci gaba da maimaitawa mara iyaka (dakatarwar makalewa ta minti 10 da aka hardcode a cikin
`comboPredicates.ts` har yanzu tana aiki). Ƙima mai kyau tana maye gurbin wannan
matakin kariya ga combo. Riƙe `comboTimeoutMs` ya fi `targetTimeoutMs` tsawo domin failover
ya ci gaba da samun lokaci bayan manufa ta farko mai jinkiri.

### Iyakokin Circuit Breaker

Daidaita circuit breaker a matakin mai samarwa. Tsofaffin ƙimomi suna nuna ƙimomin da aka faɗaɗa waɗanda ake amfani da su tun daga v3.6 don haɗin kai 500+.

| Sauƙaƙe                                                       | Tsoho     | Fayil ɗin Tushe                      | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_THRESHOLD`                   | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Iyakar gazawa a jere ga masu samar da OAuth kafin mai katsewar ya kunna.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_RESET_MS`                    | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Tagar sake saiti (ms) ga mai katsewar mai samar da OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_THRESHOLD`                 | `12`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Iyakar gazawa a jere ga masu samarwa masu amfani da API-key.                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_RESET_MS`                  | `30000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Tagar sake saiti (ms) ga mai katsewar mai samarwa mai amfani da API-key.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_THRESHOLD`                   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Iyakar gazawa a jere ga masu samarwa na gida (Ollama, LM Studio, ...).                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_RESET_MS`                    | `15000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Tagar sake saiti (ms) ga mai katsewar mai samarwa na gida.                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `10`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katsewa a matakin mai samarwa: yawan gazawa cikin tagar kafin gaba ɗaya mai samar da OAuth ya shiga lokacin hucewa.                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `900000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katsewa a matakin mai samarwa: taga mai motsi ta ƙidayar gazawa (ms) ga masu samar da OAuth.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katsewa a matakin mai samarwa: lokacin hucewa (ms) da zarar an kai iyakar mai samar da OAuth.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`      | `5`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai samar da OAuth yana shiga DEGRADED bayan gazawa wannan adadi.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`     | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Matsakaicin ninkin ƙara resetTimeout na mai samar da OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT`   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai samar da OAuth yana ƙara matakin jinkiri bayan wannan adadin zagayen buɗewa.                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`        | `15`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katsewa a matakin mai samarwa: yawan gazawa cikin tagar kafin gaba ɗaya mai samarwa mai amfani da API-key ya shiga lokacin hucewa.                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`        | `1800000` | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katsewa a matakin mai samarwa: taga mai motsi ta ƙidayar gazawa (ms) ga masu samarwa masu amfani da API-key.                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`              | `600000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katsewa a matakin mai samarwa: lokacin hucewa (ms) da zarar an kai iyakar mai samarwa mai amfani da API-key.                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`    | `7`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai samarwa mai amfani da API-key yana shiga DEGRADED bayan gazawa wannan adadi.                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`   | `4`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Matsakaicin ninkin ƙara resetTimeout na mai samarwa mai amfani da API-key.                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT` | `3`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai samarwa mai amfani da API-key yana ƙara matakin jinkiri bayan wannan adadin zagayen buɗewa.                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katsewa a matakin mai samarwa: yawan gazawa kafin gaba ɗaya mai samarwa na gida ya shiga lokacin hucewa.                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katsewa a matakin mai samarwa: taga mai motsi ta ƙidayar gazawa (ms) ga masu samarwa na gida.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katsewa a matakin mai samarwa: lokacin hucewa (ms) da zarar an kai iyakar mai samarwa na gida.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`                               | `60000`   | `open-sse/services/apiKeyRotator.ts` | Lokacin hucewa na kowace maɓalli (ms), wanda juyawar API-key mai maɓallai da yawa ke amfani da shi idan 429 daga upstream bai ƙunshi `Retry-After` ba (#14573). Ƙimomin da ba su fi sifili ba ko waɗanda ba lambobi ba za su koma ga tsohuwar ƙima. |
| `PIN_DROP_BACKOFF_LEVEL`                                      | `2`       | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`         | Zurfin jinkiri wanda a cikinsa ake ɗaukar mai samar da pin na context-cache a matsayin wanda rashin lafiyarsa ta dawwama, kuma a cire pin ɗin don sauyawa zuwa madadin.                                                                             |
| `PIN_DROP_GRACE_MS`                                           | `20000`   | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`         | Tagar hana yawan sauyawa (ms) da ke jure gajerun lokutan hucewa na wucin gadi kafin cire pin na context-cache.                                                                                                                                      |

### Yanayi

| Yanayi                                      | Saitawa                                                   |
| ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Samar da lamba mai ɗaukar dogon lokaci**  | `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS=900000` (minti 15)                    |
| **Iyakance jimillar tsawon rayuwar stream** | `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS=1260000` (minti 21)             |
| **Gaza da sauri don API na samarwa**        | `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS=10000`                       |
| **Samfuran tunani mai tsawo**               | `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS=300000` (minti 5 tsakanin chunks) |
