| `PROXY_FAST_FAIL_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `2000`                      | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                        | Wa'adin ƙarewar binciken lafiya na gazawa-da-wuri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_LATENCY_WINDOW_HOURS`                      | `3`                         | `src/lib/db/proxies.ts`                                                                         | Tsawon lokaci (awanni) don ƙididdige matsakaicin jinkirin proxy masu yiwuwa a dabarar rukunin da aka inganta don rage jinkiri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS`                       | `30000`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                        | TTL na ma'ajiyar sakamakon binciken lafiya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_UNHEALTHY_CACHE_TTL_MS`             | `2000`                      | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                        | TTL na ma'ajiya don binciken lafiyar proxy da ya gaza. Ajiye wannan ya fi `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS` gajarta domin a sake gwada ƙarewar lokacin proxy na wucin gadi cikin sauri a ƙarƙashin yawan aiki na lokaci guda, ba tare da kashe fasalin gazawa-da-wuri ga proxy da suka daina aiki gaba ɗaya ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_ENABLED`                            | `true`                      | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                              | Saita `false` don kashe mai tsara binciken lafiyar proxy da ke aiki a bayan fage, wanda ke bincikar proxy da aka yi wa rajista lokaci-lokaci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_INTERVAL_MS`                        | `600000`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                              | Tazarar zagayen mai tsara binciken lafiya a bayan fage a cikin ms (mafi ƙanƙanta `60000`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RELAY_INTERVAL_MS`                  | `21600000`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/relayCadence.ts`                                                           | Tazarar gwaji a cikin ms don proxies na edge-relay (`deno`/`vercel`/`cloudflare`), waɗanda gwajinsu ke tayar da serverless isolate mai kuɗin amfani (mafi ƙaranci `60000`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RECOVERY_INTERVAL_MS`               | `600000`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                              | Tazarar aikin dawo da lafiya na bango a cikin ms: yawan lokutan da za a sake gwada proxies da a baya ba su da lafiya domin proxy da ya farfaɗo ya koma juyawa ba tare da sake farawa ba. Ƙimomin da suke ƙasa da `60000` za su koma ga ƙimar tsoho.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL`                           | `https://httpbin.org/ip`    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                            | Manufar gwajin iya isa da scheduler da endpoint na `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` ke amfani da ita. Nuna shi zuwa URL na ciki/mai masaukin kansa domin kauce wa tsohon zaɓi na jama'a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_CONCURRENCY`                   | `10`                        | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                            | Yawan gwaje-gwajen da ake fara wa lokaci guda a kowane rukuni, wanda scheduler da endpoint na `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` suke amfani da shi tare. Ana sa mafi ƙarancinsa ya zama 1, kuma mafi yawansa 50.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_STAGGER_MS`                    | `100`                       | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                            | Jinkiri a cikin ms tsakanin fara gwaje-gwaje biyu a cikin rukuni. Idan babu shi, dukkan rukunin zai tashi a lokaci guda, kuma IP na fita da ake amfani da shi tare zai iya jawo takaita ƙimar buƙatu a wurin da ake gwadawa. Saita zuwa `0` domin kashe wannan tazara; mafi yawansa 5000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_USE_PROVIDER_TARGET`                | `true`                      | `src/lib/proxyHealth/providerProbeTarget.ts`                                                    | Saita "false" domin dakatar da gwada ainihin host na provider da aka sanya wa proxy (`GET /models`, babu API key), sannan a kullum a yi amfani da `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL` a maimakonsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_AUTO_DEACTIVATE`                    | `false`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/statusPolicy.ts`                                                           | Idan `false` ne (tsohon zaɓi), gwaje-gwajen iya isa masu sarrafa kansu (scheduler + maɓallin "Gwada Duka" na `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test`) **na karantawa kawai** ne kuma ba sa taɓa rubuta matsayin proxy — mai gudanarwa ne kaɗai yake saita mai aiki/mara aiki, don haka gwajin da ba ya da tabbas ba zai iya barin proxy da aka sanya a makale ba (#6246). Saita `true` domin dawo da tsohuwar ɗabi'ar gwadawa-da-saitawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `FLUSH_EMPTY_RETRY_ENABLED`                       | `false`                     | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                              | Tutocin fasalin shiga-da-yardar-mai-amfani (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); sauyin DB na dashboard yana da fifiko). `true` (ko `1`, `yes`) yana sake gwada zagayen streaming da aka fassara amma babu komai ta hanyar bayanan shaida ta yau da kullum (har zuwa sake-gwaji `STREAM_RECOVERY.EMPTY_TURN_RETRY_MAX`) maimakon nuna 200 mara komai ko 502 mai abun ciki mara komai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `EMPTY_STREAM_SIBLING_RECOVERY_ENABLED`           | `false`                     | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                              | Tutocin fasalin shiga-da-yardar-mai-amfani (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); sauyin DB na dashboard yana da fifiko). `true` (ko `1`, `yes`) yana kunna gwajin abun ciki na empty-stream na Claude/Codex kuma ya yi amfani da sake-gwaji mai iyaka da ake da shi a kan wata haɗi da ta cancanta bayan zagayen `empty_content`, ba tare da lokacin jira ko kulle model ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `STREAM_LATE_OUTPUT_PROBE_ENABLED`                | `false`                     | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                              | Tutocin fasalin shiga-da-yardar-mai-amfani (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); sauyin DB na dashboard yana da fifiko). `true` (ko `1`, `yes`) yana ci gaba da ɗaukar samfurin chunk ɗaya da aka tura cikin kowane 16 bayan 64 na farko, domin fitowar da ake iya gani daga baya har yanzu ta nuna cewa stream ɗin ya fitar da sakamako (5xx guda ɗaya bayan fitowa zai ci gaba da taƙaitu ga buƙatar maimakon kulle model kaɗai).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_POOL_EGRESS_OBSERVATION`                   | `false`                     | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                              | Tutar fasalin zaɓin shiga (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); saitin maye gurbi na DB na dashboard yana da fifiko). `true` (ko `1`, `yes`) yana nuna lura na fita daga pool na karantawa kawai a ƙarƙashin proxy pool a dashboard (adreshin IP na fita daban-daban, haɗe-haɗe, da wanda aka fi gani a bayan IP ɗaya cikin awanni 24 da suka gabata, daga log ɗin proxy). Ba a taɓa amfani da shi wajen routing ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_ENABLED`                   | `false`                     | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                              | Tutar fasalin zaɓin shiga (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); saitin maye gurbi na DB na dashboard yana da fifiko). `true` (ko `1`, `yes`) yana karɓar adiresoshin lura masu kwanan wata da operator ya tura ga kowane memban pool, sannan ya haɗa su da karatun journal domin nunawa da tsara pool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_WINDOW_HOURS`              | `24`                        | `src/lib/db/proxyOperatorEgress.ts`                                                             | Tagar lura cikin awanni don layukan fita da operator ya tura (ana karantawa a kowane karatu; ba a buƙatar sake farawa). An iyakance zuwa 1..168; ƙimar da ta fita daga iyaka, wadda ba lamba ba ce, ko marar komai tana komawa zuwa 24 tare da gargadi guda ɗaya a duk faɗin process, ba tare da taɓa jefa kuskure ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`                               | `false`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                              | Saita `true` domin ba scheduler damar cire proxies ta atomatik bayan maimaitattun gazawa a jere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`                         | `3`                         | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                              | Yawan gazawa a jere kafin scheduler ya cire proxy ta atomatik (lokacin da `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROXY_AUTO_DISABLE`                              | `false`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                              | Saita `true` domin ba scheduler damar kashe proxy a hankali (matsayi `dead`, ba za a taɓa share shi ba) bayan maimaitattun gazawa a jere, maimakon cire shi. Madadin `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE` ne wanda ba ya lalata bayanai: nan take proxy ɗin zai daina shiga warwarewar pool/rotation (tacewar alive-status da warwarewar scope-pool ke amfani da ita tuni tana ware shi), kuma za a sake kunna shi ta atomatik da zarar ya fara cin jarabawar probes. Yana amfani da iyakar `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER` iri ɗaya. Idan tutocin biyu `true` ne, `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE` yana da fifiko.                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_PASSIVE_SKIP`                       | `false`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                              | Zaɓin tsallakewa na passive: lokacin da yake `true`, scheduler yana tsallake live probe na endpoint wanda traffic na production na baya-bayan nan ya riga ya tabbatar da ingancinsa (aƙalla nasara ɗaya kuma babu gazawar proxy da aka jingina masa a cikin tagar, an lura da provider ɗaya tak). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa: ana yi wa kowane proxy probe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_PASSIVE_WINDOW_MS`                         | `300000`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/passiveVerdict.ts`                                                         | Tagar traffic na production cikin ms da tsallakewar passive ke dubawa (an iyakance zuwa `60000..599999`). Ta fi tazarar sweep ta mintuna 10 gajarta domin gazawar baya-bayan nan ta yi rinjaye maimakon ta narke cikin sauran bayanai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTROL_PLANE_PROXY_DIRECT_FALLBACK`   | `false`                     | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                                | Ba wa hanyoyin OAuth da tabbatar da provider damar ƙetare pinned proxy su haɗu kai tsaye lokacin da pre-checks na isa ga proxy suka gaza. Tsarin fifiko mai aiki shi ne saitin maye gurbi na Feature Flags DB > env var > tsohon saiti.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_WAIT_MS`                          | `30000` (30s)               | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts`                                                                | Tsohon kasafin **jiran-layi**: tsawon lokacin da buƙata za ta iya jiran gurbin mai bayarwa kuma ta zauna a layi kafin a ƙi ta, ba tare da ta taɓa isa ga upstream ba. Ana soke ma'aunin lokacin da zarar aikin ya fara gudana — ana iyakance lokacin gudana daban ta `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS`. Env yana saita tsohuwar ƙima kawai: `resilienceSettings.requestQueue.maxWaitMs` da aka adana, sannan `rateLimitOverrides.maxWaitMs` na kowace haɗi, kowannensu yana rinjayar ta.                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS`                | `600000` (minti 10)         | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                         | Iyakar tsawon lokacin da buƙatar da aka karɓa za ta iya ci gaba da gudana kafin ajiyar iyakar ƙimar ta ƙare — an ware shi daga kasafin jiran-layi domin jinkirin fara fetch a kan gateways marasa incremental kada ya haifar da ƙarewar lokaci (#12027).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_QUEUE_DEPTH`                      | `0` (a kashe)               | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                         | Iyakar karɓar buƙatu cikin layi: a ƙi tare da 429 `queue_full` da zarar buƙatu masu wannan adadi sun riga sun kasance a layi. `0` = babu iyaka (tsohuwar ƙima).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `RATE_LIMIT_AUTO_ENABLE`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_           | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                         | Tilasta kunna/kashe kariyar iyakar ƙima ta atomatik ba tare da la'akari da saitin Dashboard da aka adana ba (`resilienceSettings.requestQueue.autoEnableApiKeyProviders`). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on` don tilasta kunnawa, `false`/`0`/`off` don tilasta kashewa. Idan yana aiki (tsohuwar ƙima), ana kare haɗin API-key masu aiki da iyakar ƙima ta atomatik. Saita `rateLimitProtection: false` ga kowace haɗi yana kashe kariya bayyananniya amma ba ya cire haɗin daga wannan kariyar atomatik; saita wannan env var zuwa `false` ita ce hanyar gama-gari ta gudanar da haɗin API-key ba tare da layukan iyakar ƙima ba.                                                           |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`                       | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                                  | Bin diddigin lokacin hucewar mai bayarwa/haɗi na gama-gari tsakanin buƙatu, wanda sai an zaɓi kunnawa. A KASHE yake ta tsohuwa (yana cin karo da Lokacin Hucewar Haɗi / Mai Katse Da'irar Mai Bayarwa). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on` don kunnawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MIN_MS`                        | `5000`                      | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                                  | Mafi ƙarancin lokacin hucewa (ms) kafin a sake gwada mai bayarwa/haɗin da ya gaza. Ana ƙara shi a kan sikelin exponential bisa gazawar da suka biyo juna. Ana amfani da shi kawai idan `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MAX_MS`                        | `300000` (minti 5)          | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                                  | Matsakaicin iyakar lokacin hucewa (ms) kafin a sake gwada mai bayarwa/haɗin da ya gaza ko da menene. Ana amfani da shi kawai idan `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`                         | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (shuka) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (dabaru)       | **Mene ne:** dawo da rafukan upstream da aka yanke cikin gaskiya ga mai amfani (port ɗin free-claude-code). Yana riƙe taga SSE ta farko har zuwa `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS` (ms 750), domin yankewar _kafin-aika_ — wadda ke faruwa kafin ko byte ɗaya ya isa ga abokin ciniki — a sake buɗe ta kuma a sake gwadawa ba tare da an gani ba. **Lokacin kunnawa:** upstream masu tangal-tangal/waɗanda suke yawan yankewa da byte 0 a farkon rafi; bar shi A KASHE idan ba za ka iya ɗaukar ƙarin har ms 750 ga lokacin-samun-token-na-farko a kowane rafi ba. Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on`. Yana shuka saitin Juriya da aka adana; saitin Dashboard yana rinjaya da zarar an saita shi. |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_MIDSTREAM_ENABLED`               | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (ƙimar farko) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (dabaru) | **Mene ne:** ci gaba daga tsakiyar rafi (Mataki na 4.4) — bayan yankewa ta _post-commit_ (bytes sun riga sun isa ga abokin ciniki), a sake aika buƙata tare da rubutun da bai cika ba a matsayin cikon farko na mataimaki, sannan a haɗa ƙarshen da ya ɓace. Don rafukan rubutu-tsantsa masu dacewa da OpenAI kawai; ba ya taɓa aiki yayin da ake gudanar da kiran kayan aiki. **Lokacin kunnawa:** ga dogayen samar da amsa da ake yankewa a tsakiyar amsa kuma ka yarda ƙarshen da aka dawo da shi ya iso gaba ɗaya lokaci guda maimakon token-bayan-token. Mai zaman kansa ne daga `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED` (bayanan haɗari sun bambanta). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on`.             |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_ENABLED`              | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` → `open-sse/services/throughputWatchdog.ts`                    | Mai sa-ido na fitarwa mai amfani a rafi mai aiki, wanda sai an zaɓi kunnawa. Yana gano rafukan da ke ci gaba da aika gutsattsari amma suke ƙasa da ƙimar fitarwar mataimaki da aka saita; bugun-zuciya, abubuwan amfani, delta marasa komai, da matakan kayan aiki/tunani ba sa ɓoye kansu a matsayin ci gaba. Ya bambanta da iyakokin lokacin zaman banza da na wa'adin ƙarshe mai tsauri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WARMUP_MS`            | `30000`                     | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                      | Lokacin rangwame kafin tantance yawan fitarwa, an iyakance shi zuwa ms 0–600000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WINDOW_MS`            | `30000`                     | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                      | Tagar fitarwa mai amfani mai motsi, an iyakance ta zuwa ms 1000–600000; ana buƙatar cikakkiyar taga guda kafin katsewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_BYTES_PER_SECOND` | `4`                         | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                      | Mafi ƙarancin ƙimar bytes na fitarwar mataimaki ta UTF-8 a kowace daƙiƙa (kimantawar token mai taka-tsantsan), an iyakance zuwa 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_USEFUL_BYTES`     | `1`                         | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                      | Mafi ƙarancin samfurin fitarwa mai amfani wanda ba sifili ba kuma ake ɗauka a matsayin abin aunawa, an iyakance zuwa bytes 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS`                          | `3000`                      | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                   | Tazarar jinkirtawa (ms) tsakanin duba lafiyar token na masu samarwa yayin farawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MIN_MS`                       | `500`                       | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                   | Mafi ƙarancin karkacewar lokaci ta bazuwar (ms) da ake ƙarawa a kan `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` tsakanin duba lafiyar token na masu samarwa, don hana cunkoson buƙatu lokaci guda (Batu #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MAX_MS`                       | `5000`                      | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                   | Mafi girman karkacewar lokaci ta bazuwar (ms) da ake ƙarawa a kan `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` tsakanin duba lafiyar token na masu samarwa, don hana cunkoson buƙatu lokaci guda (Batu #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `HEALTHCHECK_BATCH_SIZE`                          | `20`                        | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                   | Girman rukuni na dubawa masu gudana lokaci guda don cikakken duba lafiyar token yayin farawa; ƙimomi masu girma suna duba ƙarin haɗe-haɗe a lokaci guda, ƙananan ƙimomi kuma suna rage nauyin cunkoso (Batu #7875, komawar matsala ta #7719).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `REQUEST_RETRY`                                   | `2`                         | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                        | Adadin sake-gwada ta atomatik kan martanin lokacin sanyaya na takamaiman model kafin mayar da kuskure ga abokin ciniki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `MAX_RETRY_INTERVAL_SEC`                          | `30`                        | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                        | Matsakaicin tazarar backoff (dakiku) tsakanin sake gwaje-gwajen cooldown. Wannan ƙimar ce iyaka ko da kuwa menene `Retry-After` daga upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `HEADROOM_URL`                                    | `http://localhost:8787`     | `src/lib/headroom/detect.ts`                                                                    | URL na proxy mai adana token na Headroom. Tsarin rayuwar dashboard (`api/headroom/*`) yana ƙaddamar da CLI na `headroom-ai` na cikin gida a kan loopback ta tsohuwa; a sauya wannan kawai don nuna wani proxy na Docker sidecar na waje.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

### Konstantocin daidaita dawo da stream

Ana daidaita halayyar riƙe bayanai ta dawo da stream ta hanyar konstantoci da aka ƙayyade kai tsaye a cikin
`open-sse/config/constants.ts` (`STREAM_RECOVERY`), waɗanda aka nuna a nan don tunani —
canza su na buƙatar gyaran lamba, ba env var ba:

- `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS = 750` — tsawon lokacin da ake riƙe tagar SSE ta farko
  domin a iya sake gwadawa idan an yanke ta da wuri kafin a tura ko da byte ɗaya zuwa ga abokin ciniki.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.BUFFER_MAX_BYTES = 65536` — ƙaƙƙarfan iyaka ga tagar da aka riƙe; a tura
  (flush + passthrough) da zarar bytes masu yawa haka sun taru, ba tare da la’akari da timer ba.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.EARLY_RETRY_MAX = 4` — matsakaicin adadin sake buɗe stream na upstream
  a ɓoye yayin da har yanzu ba a tura abin da aka riƙe ba.

> **Iyakar ƙimar sliding-window ta kowane provider (babu env var):** _fallback_ na iyakar ƙimar
> sliding-window ta kowane provider da aka ɗauko daga FCC yana cikin lambar
> (`open-sse/services/providerDefaultRateLimit.ts`, an haɗa shi ta
> `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`) amma ana fitar da shi da **empty default map**
> kuma **ba shi da env var na operator** a halin yanzu — ana kunna shi ne kawai ta hanyar test hook /
> gyaran lamba. Da gangan ba a lissafo shi a teburin da ke sama ba. Limiter na relay na kowane-`(token, IP)`
> wanda _ke_ da maɓallin daidaitawa shi ne `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE` (§3 Network & Ports).

> [!CAUTION]

| Variable                              | Tsoho                        | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                                                       | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CURSOR_DEBUG`                        | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Saita `1` don kunna cikakkun logs na Cursor executor (SSE chunks da aka decode, da sauransu).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Alias mai dacewa da tsofaffin nau'ikan `CURSOR_DEBUG`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CURSOR_DUMP_FILE`                    | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Path na fayil na zaɓi wanda ke karɓar ɗanyen Cursor chunks da aka decode yayin da `CURSOR_DEBUG=1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `300000`                     | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Lokacin jira kafin stream marar aiki ya ƙare (ms) ga Cursor executor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CURSOR_KV_GRACE_MS`                  | `2000`                       | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ƙarin lokacin jira (ms) bayan composer kv_after_text soft terminator yayin da bytes suka rage a buffer — yana ba trailing exec_mcp tool call lokaci don kammala frame ɗinsa.                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CURSOR_TOOL_DIRECTIVE`               | a kunne (`!== "0"`)          | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Tool-commit directive da ke sa composer-2.5 ya yi tool calls cikin aminci. Saita `0` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYSTEM_INSTRUCTION_APPEND` | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/translator/request/claude-to-openai.ts`, `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts` | Rubutun system prompt da operator ya ayyana wanda ake liƙawa ga system message BAYAN translation (post-translation injection), yana isa ga hanyoyin codex/Responses da `/v1/messages`. Haka kuma ana amfani da shi a matsayin directive prefix da ake cirewa daga echoed system preamble blocks. Bar shi ba a saita ba don kashewa. |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRIP_SYSTEM_PREAMBLE`     | `0` (a kashe)                | `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts`, `open-sse/utils/directivePreambleStripper.ts`     | Saita zuwa `1` don cire echoed system-prompt preamble blocks daga farkon openai→claude stream. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — heuristics ɗin sun dace da tsarin rubutun turanci kuma suna canza response payload, saboda haka reply da ya fara da irin wannan sashe bisa halacci zai rasa shi.                                           |

Ba masu amfani damar kai rahoton matsaloli kai tsaye daga Dashboard.

| Sauyi                 | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                               | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                           |
| --------------------- | ----------------- | --------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_REPO`  | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`       | Ma'ajiyar lamba a tsarin `owner/repo`.                                                                                                                                           |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`       | GitHub Personal Access Token mai izinin `issues:write`.                                                                                                                          |
| `GITHUB_TOKEN`        | _(ba a saita ba)_ | tantance matsaloli / mataimakan wakilin cloud | Alamar samun damar GitHub ta gama-gari da ake amfani da ita a matsayin madadin `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN`, kuma mataimakan wakilin cloud a `src/lib/cloudAgent/*` suna amfani da ita. |

## Yanayin Turawa

Don jagorar SRE ta backend na relay (halayen ts/bifrost/auto, wurin sanya 9router idan aka kwatanta da CLIProxyAPI, da dabarar komawa madadin yayin yawan zirga-zirga), duba [Dabarar Backend na Relay](/docs/reference/RELAY_BACKEND_STRATEGY.md).

### Mafi Ƙarancin Saitin Ci-gaban Gida

```bash
JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)
API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
INITIAL_PASSWORD=dev123
PORT=20128
NODE_ENV=development
```

### Docker na Yanayin Samarwa

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
API_KEY_SECRET=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
INITIAL_PASSWORD=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
DATA_DIR=/data
PORT=20128
API_PORT=20129
NODE_ENV=production
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=8192
CORS_ORIGIN=https://your-frontend.example.com
```

### Muhalli Marar Haɗin Intanet / CI

```bash
JWT_SECRET=test-jwt-secret-for-ci
API_KEY_SECRET=test-api-key-secret-for-ci
INITIAL_PASSWORD=testpass
NODE_ENV=production
OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES=true
APP_LOG_TO_FILE=false
```

### VPS tare da Reverse Proxy (nginx + Cloudflare)

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
API_KEY_SECRET=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
PORT=20128
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:20128
CORS_ORIGIN=https://omniroute.example.com
ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true
CLI_COMPAT_ALL=1
```

## 24. Skills Sandbox (v3.8.0+)

Iyakoki da saitunan tsaro da ake amfani da su lokacin da tsarin Skills (`src/lib/skills/`) ke aiwatar da ayyukan atomatik da masu amfani suka ayyana a cikin muhalli mai keɓewa.

| Sauyi                             | Tsoho                                          | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                    | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| --------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `10000` (10 s)                                 | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Iyakar lokacin agogo ga kowace aiwatarwar lambar skill a cikin sandbox. Iyaka ce tabbatacciya; ana kashe duk abin da ya wuce ta.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `SKILLS_EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_MS`     | _(yana komawa ga `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`)_ | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Iyakar lokacin tsara aiwatar da skill a babban mataki. Saita shi sama da `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` don ba da damar jerin ayyuka masu matakai da yawa.                                                                                                                                |
| `SKILLS_MAX_FILE_BYTES`           | `1048576` (1 MB)                               | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Matsakaicin bytes da skill zai iya karantawa daga kowane fayil guda ɗaya a cikin sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SKILLS_MAX_HTTP_RESPONSE_BYTES`  | `256000` (250 KB)                              | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Matsakaicin bytes da ake ɗauka daga kowace amsar HTTP guda ɗaya a cikin skill.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SKILLS_MAX_SANDBOX_OUTPUT_CHARS` | `100000`                                       | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Tabbatacciyar iyaka ga haruffan stdout/stderr da ake dawo da su daga kiran sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED`  | `false`                                        | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Saita `1`/`true` don ba da damar sadarwar fita daga cikin sandbox. Ta tsohuwa tana kasancewa **keɓantacciya** saboda tsaro.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SKILLS_ALLOWED_SANDBOX_IMAGES`   | _(babu komai)_                                 | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Jerin container images da aka ware da waƙafi waɗanda aka amince da su don aiwatarwa a sandbox. Idan babu komai, ana amfani da tsohon ginannen zaɓi kawai.                                                                                                                              |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_DOCKER_IMAGE`     | _(tsohon ginannen zaɓi)_                       | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Container image da ake amfani da shi lokacin ƙirƙirar sandbox mai dogaro da Docker. Sauya shi don ɗaure amfani da keɓaɓɓen base image mai ƙarfafa tsaro.                                                                                                                               |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME`          | `auto`                                         | `src/lib/skills/sandbox.ts`, `src/lib/skills/containerProvider.ts` | Container runtime don keɓe skills: `auto` \| `docker` \| `apple` \| `wsl` \| `orbstack` \| `podman`. `auto` yana zaɓar runtime mafi dacewa da aka shigar bisa OS na host (Apple Container/OrbStack a macOS, WSL Container a Windows, Podman a Linux), sannan ya koma Docker idan babu. |

> [!CAUTION]
> Kunna `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED=true` yana buɗe hanyar fitar bayanai daga kowace lambar skill. Haɗa shi da `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED=true` da tsauraran manufofin `CORS_ORIGIN`/proxy a turawar da ake rabawa.

---

## 25. Ƙayyadaddun Amfani na Masu Bayarwa, Tunnels, Ajiyar Bayanai & Sauran Abubuwan Lokacin Gudanarwa

Wuraren haɗin API na ƙayyadaddun amfani na masu bayarwa, tunnels na hanyar sadarwa (Tailscale, Ngrok, wakilin debug na MITM), rukunin egress na 1Proxy, ajiyar bayanan database da ƙananan sauye-sauyen kowane fasali da matakin executor ko scripts ke amfani da su.

| Ma’auni                                        | Tsoho                                                                       | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_URL`                                    | `redis://localhost:6379`                                                    | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | Kirtanin haɗin Redis don bangaren baya na mai iyakance yawan buƙatu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_HOST`                     | _(uwar-garken samarwa)_                                                     | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Sauya uwar-garken da ake amfani da ita wajen ɗauko ƙididdigar coding-plan na Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_QUOTA_URL`                | an samo daga uwar-garken                                                    | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Sauya cikakken URL na ƙididdiga na Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_COOKIE`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Cookie na zaman console don mashigar ƙididdigar Token Plan ta sirri ta Qwen Cloud / Model Studio (mabuɗin API na inference ba zai iya karanta shi ba). Kwafi dukkan taken buƙatar `Cookie` — yana ɗauke da `login_qwencloud_ticket` — daga kowane kiran `api.json` zuwa `cs-data.qwencloud.com` a home.qwencloud.com › Billing › Subscription (F12 › Network). Mai muhimmanci ne kuma ya keɓanta ga zaman; an fi son filin Dashboard na `qwenCloudCookie` na kowace haɗi.                                                                           |

Mai muhimmanci ne; idan ba a saita shi ba, mai ɗaukowa zai samo shi daga HTML na dashboard ta amfani da cookie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_HOST`                         | `https://cs-data.qwencloud.com`                                             | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Sauya uwar-garken mashiga don mai ɗauko ƙididdigar Token Plan ta sirri (misali, `bailian-singapore-cs.alibabacloud.com` don console na Model Studio).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

| `QDRANT_API_KEY`                               | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | _(bayanan martabar cluster na zaɓi)_                                      | API key na zaɓi don Qdrant Cloud ko instance na on-prem mai tantancewa.
