| `QDRANT_COLLECTION`                            | `omniroute-memory`                                                          | _(bayanan martabar cluster na zaɓi)_                                      | Sunan collection don embeddings na ƙwaƙwalwar tattaunawar OmniRoute. Ana ƙirƙirar shi a farkon aiki tare da dimensions na `QDRANT_VECTOR_SIZE`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `QDRANT_EMBEDDING_MODEL`                       | `text-embedding-3-small`                                                    | _(bayanan martabar cluster na zaɓi)_                                      | Sunan embedding model na tsohuwa da aka rubuta a cikin metadata na collection na Qdrant. Ainihin embeddings ana samar da su ne ta duk wani provider da field ɗin `embeddingModel` a cikin saitunan OmniRoute yake nunawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `QDRANT_VECTOR_SIZE`                           | `1536`                                                                      | _(bayanan gungu na zaɓi)_                                                 | Girman vector na embedding. Dole ne ya dace da model ɗin da kake yin embedding da shi (text-embedding-3-small → 1536; ada-002 → 1536; nomic-embed-text → 768).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `QDRANT_HNSW_EF_CONSTRUCT`                     | `128`                                                                       | _(bayanan gungu na zaɓi)_                                                 | Daidaiton lokacin gina index na HNSW. Mafi girma = ginawa a hankali, bincike cikin sauri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_ENABLED`                   | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Babban maɓallin kunna juyawar asusu da mai gudanarwa zai iya saita wa. Idan `false`, babu ɗaya daga cikin nau'ukan `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_*` na ƙasa da zai kunna komawa wani asusu (kasancewar babban maɓallin a kashe kuma yana hana nau'ukan 429/500/502 da aka kunna ta tsohuwa). Wannan yana bai wa manhajar gaba mai sa ido (misali, manhajar tebur ta VibeProxy) damar daidaita ƙa'idojin juyawarta da injin komawa wani asusu na backend.                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_RATE_LIMIT_RESET_SECONDS`  | `0`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Lokacin jira (dakikoki) da ake sanya wa asusun da aka iyakance yawan buƙatunsa idan upstream bai bayar da takamaiman alamar lokacin sake saiti ba. `0` = yi amfani da tsohon lokacin jira na injin maimakon ƙayyadadden sauyawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_DISABLE_TAG_WITHOUT_RESET` | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Kwaikwayon zaɓin front-end na "kar a yiwa alama a matsayin wanda aka iyakance yawan buƙatunsa ba tare da lokacin sake saiti ba".                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_429`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Kunna komawa madadi bisa kowane matsayi don kurakuran `429`. Idan `false` (kuma `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_ENABLED=true`), kuskuren `429` ba zai ƙara kunna juyawar asusu ba, maimakon haka za a mayar da shi ga client.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Adadin kurakuran `429` a cikin `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_WINDOW_SECONDS` da ake buƙata kafin a juya asusun. `1` (tsohon ƙima) yana juyawa nan take, yana kiyaye halayen da aka saba da su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Tagar lokaci mai motsi (dakikoki) wadda ake ƙirga kurakuran `429` a cikinta zuwa `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_500`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Kunna komawa madadi bisa kowane matsayi don kurakuran server na `5xx` (ban da `502`, wanda ke da nasa nau'in). Idan `false`, waɗannan kurakuran ba za su ƙara kunna juyawar asusu ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Adadin kurakuran `5xx` a cikin `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_WINDOW_SECONDS` da ake buƙata kafin a juya asusun. `1` (tsohon ƙima) yana juyawa nan take.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Tagar zamiya (daƙiƙoƙi) wadda a cikinta ake ƙirga kurakuran `5xx` domin kaiwa ga `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_502`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Kunna komawa madadin bisa kowane matsayi don kurakuran `502` (mummunar ƙofar sadarwa). Idan `false` ne, `502` ba za su ƙara jawo juya asusu ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Adadin kurakuran `502` a cikin `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS` da ake buƙata kafin a juya asusun. `1` (tsoho) yana juya shi nan take.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Tagar zamiya (daƙiƙoƙi) wadda a cikinta ake ƙirga kurakuran `502` domin kaiwa ga `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400`                      | `false`                                                                     | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Zaɓin shiga (a kashe yake ta tsohuwa): idan `true` ne, `400` na yau da kullum (mummunar buƙata) shi ma yana jawo juya asusu. Wannan ƙari ne kawai — ba ya taɓa hana halin injin da yake akwai inda `400` mai ɗauke da rubutun iyakar ƙimar amfani/ƙayyadadden kaso zai ci gaba da komawa madadin ba tare da la’akari da wannan tuta ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Adadin kurakuran `400` a cikin `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS` da ake buƙata kafin a juya asusun (ana duba wannan ne kawai idan `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Tagar zamiya (daƙiƙoƙi) wadda a cikinta ake ƙirga kurakuran `400` domin kaiwa ga `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

### Mai Tsara Dumama Claude

Dumama mai aiki bisa cron ga haɗin OAuth na Anthropic da aka amince da su, domin a buɗe tagar iyakar ƙimar amfani ta awa 5 ta hanyar buƙata mai sauƙi da aka tsara maimakon buƙata ta ainihi ta farko (#8848). Mai tsara aikin a kashe yake sai idan `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED` yana da ƙimar gaskiya **kuma** an sanya wa haɗin tuta a cikin `settings.claudeWarmup.connections`; jerin haɗi mara komai yana nufin ba za a dumama komai ba ko da an kunna canjin muhalli.

| Sauyi                          | Tsoho                       | Fayil ɗin Tushe              | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED`     | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Babban maɓallin kunna mai tsara warmup. Yana karɓar `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` (ba tare da la'akari da manyan ko ƙananan haruffa ba, kuma an cire sararin gefe). Duk wata ƙima dabam, ko rashin saita ta, yana barin mai tsara aikin a kashe. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CRON`        | `0 7 * * *`                 | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Bayanin cron mai filaye biyar don zagayen warmup, wanda ake tantancewa a `America/Los_Angeles` (yankin lokacin sake saiti na Anthropic) ba tare da la'akari da agogon masaukin ba.                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CONCURRENCY` | `3`                         | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Yawan haɗin da ake yi wa warmup a lokaci guda a kowane zagaye. Ana iyakance shi tsakanin `1`-`10`; ƙimar da ba lamba ba za ta koma `3`.                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_MODEL`       | `claude-3-5-haiku-20241022` | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Samfurin da ake amfani da shi don buƙatar warmup. Sauya shi kawai idan tsohon ba ya samuwa a tsarin ku; zaɓi samfurin mafi arha wanda har yanzu zai iya buɗe taga.                                                                       |

### Zaman VNC na Shiga ta Burauza da Sunan-Wakilin Data-Dir

Chromium+VNC da aka sanya cikin kwantena wanda ake amfani da shi don tattara bayanan shaidar shiga ta burauza ta hanyar hulɗa (`/api/vnc-session`), tare da tsohon sunan-wakilin `DATA_DIR`. Dukansu na zaɓi ne — tsoffin saitunan VNC suna amfani da hoton `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` da aka haɗa, kuma ana sauya su ne kawai don hoton kwantena na musamman, tashoshin sadarwa, ko daidaita zagayowar rayuwa.

| Sauyi                                 | Tsoho                                                                    | Fayil ɗin Tushe                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local`                                           | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Alamar hoton Docker don kwantenar shiga ta Chromium+VNC. Gina `docker/vnc-browser/chromium` ko nuna wannan zuwa wani hoto na musamman.                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                                                                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Fayil ɗin binary na tsarin gudanar da kwantena da ake amfani da shi don ƙaddamar da kwantenar VNC (misali, saita shi zuwa `podman`).                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar VNC/noVNC da aka buɗe a cikin kwantenar.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar Chrome DevTools Protocol a cikin kwantenar.                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                                                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Hanyar kundin bayanan martabar Chromium a cikin kwantenar.                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | `$HOME/.omniroute/browser-login-profiles`                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Kundin da ke kan masauki wanda ke riƙe da bayanan martabar shiga ta burauza da aka adana.                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000` (minti 10)                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Iyakar lokacin zaman banza (ms) kafin a cire zaman VNC da ba ya aiki.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000` (minti 30)                                                     | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin iyaka na dindindin (ms) ga tsawon rayuwar zaman VNC guda ɗaya.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin adadin zaman VNC da za su iya gudana a lokaci guda.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Iyakar lokacin jira (ms) har sai burauzar da ke cikin kwantena ta shirya don CDP.                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Iyakar lokaci (ms) don tattara zaman/cookies da aka kama bayan an kammala shiga.                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | `--remote-debugging-port=9222 --no-first-run --no-default-browser-check` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ƙarin tutocin layin umarni da ake miƙawa zuwa Chromium da ke cikin kwantenar.                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`                                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Keɓantacciyar hanyar sadarwar Docker da kwantenar shiga ta VNC ke shiga (#12571) maimakon gadar tsoho, ta yadda kwantenonin takwararta ba za su iya isa tashar gadar CDP ɗinta ba.                                         |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                        | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | **Tsohon suna-wakili** na `DATA_DIR`, wanda ake dubawa ne kawai bayan ba a saita `DATA_DIR` da `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR` duka ba. Yana gano ma’ajiyar zaman zaren yanar gizon Notion (`<dir>/notion-web-thread-sessions.json`). |

## 26. Gwaji & Tsarin E2E

Ana amfani da shi ta `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`, `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`,
`scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`, da `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`. A bar duk ƙimomin
da ke ƙasa ba tare da saita su ba a turawar samarwa.

| Maɓalli                                | Tsoho                             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| -------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_BOOTSTRAP_MODE`         | `auth`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Yanayin farawa na E2E (`auth`, `fresh`, `reuse`) don mai gudanar da Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_PASSWORD`               | yana komawa ga `INITIAL_PASSWORD` | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kalmar sirrin mai gudanarwa da ake saka cikin muhallin Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_LOCAL_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kashe binciken lafiyar cikin gida yayin gudanar da Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_TOKEN_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kashe madaukin binciken lafiyar token na OAuth yayin gwaje-gwaje.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_SKIP_PROVIDERS` | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`             | Masu samarwa da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda aka cire daga zagayen sabunta token na rigakafi (misali `codex,openai`). Madadin da aka keɓance maimakon kashe binciken lafiya gaba ɗaya — masu samarwa masu gajeren TTL suna ci gaba da sabuntawa yayin da masu samarwa na cascade ke kasancewa masu mayar da martani kawai. |
| `OMNIROUTE_HIDE_HEALTHCHECK_LOGS`      | `true`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Ɓoye hayaniyar binciken lafiya a stdout na Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BUILD`      | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Tsallake ginin samarwa na Next.js kafin Playwright ya fara (inganta CI).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_UNINSTALL_HOOK`        | `0`                               | `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`             | Tsallake hook ɗin cire OmniRoute (CI yana amfani da shi don kiyaye `node_modules` yadda yake).                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ECOSYSTEM_SERVER_WAIT_MS`             | `180000`                          | `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`     | Lokacin jira (ms) domin uwar garken ya zama lafiyayye kafin gudanar da gwaje-gwajen ecosystem/protocol.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_URL`                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/login`    | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | URL da tsarin gwajin smoke na Electron yake sa ran manhajar da aka tattara ta samar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `45000`                           | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Jimillar iyakar lokaci (ms) kafin tsarin gwajin smoke ya daina ƙoƙari.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_SETTLE_MS`             | `2000`                            | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Tsawon lokacin daidaitawa (ms) bayan shafin ya loda.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_APP_EXECUTABLE`        | _(ta atomatik)_                   | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Takamaiman hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin aiwatarwa na Electron da aka tattara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_DATA_DIR`              | _(tmpdir)_                        | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Kundin bayanai don gudanar da gwajin smoke na Electron.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_KEEP_DATA`             | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Saita zuwa `1` don adana kundin bayanan smoke bayan kammala gudanarwar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_STREAM_LOGS`           | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Saita zuwa `1` don yaɗa rajistan ayyukan Electron zuwa stdout yayin gudanarwar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_COLD_RESTART`          | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | #7592: sake ƙaddamarwa ta amfani da kundin bayanai iri ɗaya sannan a tabbatar cewa ƙaddamarwa ta biyu ta zaɓi direban SQLite na asali.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                        | _(binciken PATH)_                 | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`         | Sauya hanyar binary ta Devin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

### Tsarin fassarar takardu

Ana amfani da shi ta `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` (umarnin `npm run i18n:run`).
Dukkan sauye-sauye shida ba a saita su ta tsohuwa ba — saita su a cikin `.env` kawai a kan injunan
da ya kamata su iya gudanar da mai fassara takardun.

| Sauyi                                    | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                          | Bayani                                                                                |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_URL`          | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | URL na tushe mai dacewa da OpenAI don bangaren baya na fassara.                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_KEY`          | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Token na Bearer don bangaren baya na fassara (ba a taɓa rubuta shi a rajista ba).     |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_MODEL`            | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | ID na samfuri, misali `gpt-4o-mini` ko `cx/gpt-5.4-mini`.                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `60000`           | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Iyakar lokacin kowace buƙata a cikin millisekan.                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_REASONING_EFFORT` | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs` | Ikon tunani na zaɓi, ana aika shi yadda yake zuwa bangaren baya idan an saita shi.    |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_CONCURRENCY`      | `4`               | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Buƙatun fassara masu gudana a lokaci guda yayin aiki a kan fayiloli / yaruka da yawa. |

## 27. Ciyarwar Radar (Karɓar Baƙunci da Kai)

Ƙarin zaɓi ne da tutar fasali ta RADAR_ENABLED ke sarrafawa (a kashe take ta tsohuwa — tutar
fasali ce da ake kunnawa ko kashewa ta hanyar Settings/DB, ba env var ba; duba
[docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md#flag-radar_enabled-default-off)).
Sauye-sauye huɗu na farko da ke ƙasa zaɓaɓɓun sauye-sauyen ƙimomi ne don ciyarwar da aka karɓi
baƙuncinta da kai ko aka yi mata fork da kuma hanyoyin supporter-key. Na biyar, `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`,
mahaɗi ne na daban wanda ba shi da ƙimar tsohuwa zuwa keɓaɓɓen kwamitin ayyuka na mai shi.
Duba [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md) don cikakken bayanin
module da kuma
[jerin kunnawa daga farko har ƙarshe da saitin da ake jagoranta](../frameworks/RADAR.md#end-to-end-activation-and-guided-setup).

Ba a saita mai karanta sanarwar Home/Changelog na gama-gari ta hanyar environment
variable ba, kuma baya dogaro da tutar fasali ta RADAR_ENABLED. Yana karanta URL na `news.json`
na ma'ajiyar jama'a da aka ayyana a `src/shared/utils/releaseNotes.ts` ta hanyar
GET kawai; ID na sanarwar da aka yi watsi da su suna ci gaba da kasancewa a ma'ajiyar gida ta burauza.

| Sauyi                         | Ƙimar Tsohuwa                                | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                              | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `RADAR_FEED_URL`              | `https://radar.omniroute.online`             | `src/lib/radar/{sync,referralsSync,offersSync,intelSync}.ts` | URL na tushe da ke bai ɗaya ga catalog, referrals, supporter-offers, da ciyarwar Intel waɗanda aka sanya wa hannu daban-daban. Sauya shi don nuna sabis da aka karɓi baƙuncinsa da kai ko aka yi masa fork. |
| `RADAR_FEED_PUBKEY`           | _(maɓallin tsohuwa da aka kafa)_             | `src/lib/radar/pinnedKeys.ts`                                | Maɓallin jama'a na Ed25519 (base64-DER SPKI ko PEM) da ake amfani da shi don tabbatar da sa hannun ciyarwa daga ciyarwa ta musamman.                                                                        |
| `RADAR_CONTRIBUTOR_CLAIM_URL` | `https://radar.omniroute.online/auth/github` | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL da maɓallin dashboard na "Ni mai ba da gudummawa ne" yake buɗewa (hanyar neman supporter-key ta GitHub OAuth).                                                                                          |
| `RADAR_SUPPORTER_PLANS_URL`   | `https://radar.omniroute.online/planos`      | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL da maɓallin dashboard na "Tallafa wa aikin" yake buɗewa (shafin biyan kuɗi/tsare-tsare).                                                                                                                |
| `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                            | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | Keɓaɓɓen mahaɗin kwamitin ayyuka na mai shi kawai. Ana buƙatar HTTPS sai dai don HTTP loopback SSH forward; ƙimomin da ba a saita ba ko marasa inganci ba sa ƙirƙirar abin kewayawa.                        |

## Bincike: Sauye-sauyen da Aka Cire / Marasa Amfani

Sauye-sauye masu zuwa sun bayyana a sigogin baya na `.env.example` amma **ba su da nassoshin runtime** a cikin codebase na yanzu. An cire su:

| Maɓalli                                                                                                                                                                         | Dalili                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `STORAGE_DRIVER=sqlite`                                                                                                                                                         | Babu wani fayil ɗin tushe da yake karanta shi. SQLite ne kaɗai direban da ake tallafawa — babu buƙatar yin zaɓi.                                                                           |
| `INSTANCE_NAME=omniroute`                                                                                                                                                       | Yana cikin tsoffin takardu/samfuran env amma ba a amfani da shi lokacin aiki. Zai iya dawowa a wata fasalar amfani da instances da yawa a nan gaba.                                        |
| `SQLITE_MAX_SIZE_MB=2048`                                                                                                                                                       | Ba a ambace shi a lambar tushe ba. Ba a sanya iyakar wucin gadi ga girman ma'ajiyar bayanai ba.                                                                                            |
| `SQLITE_CLEAN_LEGACY_FILES=true`                                                                                                                                                | Ba a ambace shi a lambar tushe ba. Mai yiwuwa an cire aikin tsaftace tsoffin fayiloli.                                                                                                     |
| `CLI_ROO_BIN`                                                                                                                                                                   | Ba a yi masa rajista a cikin `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` ba.                                                                                                                       |
| `CLI_KIMI_CODING_BIN`                                                                                                                                                           | Ba a yi masa rajista a cikin `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` ba (Kimi Coding yana amfani da OAuth, ba binary na CLI ba).                                                               |
| `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` / `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                                                                                                                           | Ba a ambace su a ko'ina cikin lambar tushe ba.                                                                                                                                             |
| `CEREBRAS_API_KEY` / `COHERE_API_KEY` / `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` / `GROQ_API_KEY` / `MISTRAL_API_KEY` / `NEBIUS_API_KEY` / `PERPLEXITY_API_KEY` / `TOGETHER_API_KEY` / `XAI_API_KEY` | An cire su a v3.8.0. Tsarin aiki ya daina karanta waɗannan maɓallan env — bayanan tantancewa suna fitowa daga Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / ma'ajiyar bayanai mai ɓoyewa. |
| `CURSOR_PROTOBUF_DEBUG`                                                                                                                                                         | An cire shi a v3.8.0. Mai aiwatar da Cursor yana amfani da `CURSOR_DEBUG` / `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG` (duba §22).                                                                              |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIRO`                                                                                                                                                               | An cire shi a v3.8.0. Kiro yana cikin `CLI_COMPAT_OMITTED_PROVIDER_IDS` — maɓallin kunnawa/kashewar sa ba shi da wani tasiri.                                                              |
| `QIANFAN_API_KEY`                                                                                                                                                               | An cire shi tare da sauran samfuran maɓallan API na masu samarwa waɗanda ba a amfani da su a v3.8.0.                                                                                       |

### Gyaran Tsoffin Ƙimomi

| Maɓalli                   | Tsohuwar Ƙimar `.env.example` | Ainihin Tsohuwar Ƙimar Lamba | Gyara                                                                |
| ------------------------- | ----------------------------- | ---------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`  | `90`                          | `7`                          | ✅ An cire ƙimar mai ruɗarwa; an rubuta `7` a matsayin tsohuwar ƙima |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS` | `90`                          | `7`                          | ✅ An cire ƙimar mai ruɗarwa; an rubuta `7` a matsayin tsohuwar ƙima |

### Sake samar da saitin OpenCode (kayan aikin wucin gadi)

Ana amfani da shi ta `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` don sake samar da `opencode.json`
mai ingantattun ƙimomin `limit.context` da `limit.output` waɗanda aka samo daga instance na
OmniRoute da ke aiki. Ba a buƙatar ko ɗaya daga cikin waɗannan don aiki na yau da kullum — script ɗin
kayan aikin masu haɓakawa ne kawai.

| Sauƙaƙe            | Tsoho                    | Fayil ɗin Tushe                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------ | ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_URL`    | `http://localhost:20128` | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Asalin URL na samfurin OmniRoute da za a tambaya game da `/v1/models`.                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_KEY`    | _(ba a saita ba)_        | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Maɓallin API don tantancewa a ƙarshen OmniRoute na `/v1/models`. Yana komawa ga `OPENCODE_API_KEY` idan ba a saita shi ba.                                 |
| `OPENCODE_API_KEY` | _(ba a saita ba)_        | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Maɓallin API irin na OpenCode (`sk-...`) da ake rubutawa cikin `opencode.json` da aka sake samarwa. Yana komawa ga `OMNIROUTE_KEY` idan ba a saita shi ba. |

### Kayan gwajin compression na offline (kayan aikin ad-hoc)

Ana amfani da shi ta `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts`, wato CLI na kimanta compression na offline.
Ba a buƙatarsa don aiki na yau da kullum — kayan aikin masu haɓakawa ne kawai.

| Sauƙaƙe                      | Tsoho        | Fayil ɗin Tushe                     | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ---------------------------- | ------------ | ----------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVAL_CREDENTIALS` | `{}` (fanko) | `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts` | Shaidun JSON da mai gudanarwa ya bayar don mai samarwar da CLI na compression-eval na offline ke gwadawa (ana fassara su da `JSON.parse`). A bar shi ba a saita ba don gwajin busasshe. |

### Zaman Burauzar VNC

Ana amfani da su ta `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts` don saita zaman Chromium marasa nuni masu amfani da Docker ga masu samar da sarrafa burauza ta atomatik. Duk na zaɓi ne — an nuna tsoffin ƙimomi a ƙasa.

| Sauƙaƙe                               | Tsoho                          | Fayil ɗin Tushe                             | Bayani                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Hanyar zuwa binary na Docker da ake amfani da shi don ƙaddamar da kwantenonin VNC.                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Hoton Docker na kwantenan VNC Chromium.                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | _(tutocin ginannu)_            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ƙarin args na Chromium CLI da ake miƙawa burauzar da ke cikin kwantena.                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar VNC a cikin kwantena.                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar Chrome DevTools Protocol a cikin kwantena.                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Kundin bayanan martaba a cikin kwantena.                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Kundin ɓangaren host don bayanan martabar burauza masu ɗorewa.                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Wa'adin zaman rashin aiki (ms) kafin a girbe zaman VNC.                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin tsawon zama (ms).                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin zaman VNC da za su iya gudana a lokaci guda.                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Wa'adin jiran shirye-shiryen burauza (ms).                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Wa'adin girbi/tsabtacewa (ms).                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Keɓantacciyar hanyar sadarwar Docker da kwantenan ke shiga (#12571), ba tare da amfani da gada ta tsoho ba. |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | Kundin adana zaman zaren Notion na dindindin.                                                               |

### Tantancewar sabis na ciki

| Sauƙaƙe                                 | Tsoho | Bayani                                                                                                                                   |
| --------------------------------------- | ----- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN`      | –     | Token da aka saka kai tsaye don tantancewar sabis-zuwa-sabis na tsarin gudanarwa.                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN_FILE` | –     | Hanyar zuwa fayil mai ɗauke da token na sabis na ciki (an fi son wannan a cikin kwantena; yana rinjayar sauƙaƙen da aka saka kai tsaye). |

### Ƙididdigar mai samarwar OpenRouter

| Sauƙaƙe                             | Tsoho     | Bayani                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_ENABLED` | `true`    | Saita zuwa `false` don tsallake ɗauko ƙididdigar kowane mai samarwa ta OpenRouter don inganta kundin bayanai. |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_TTL_MS`  | `3600000` | TTL na ma'ajiyar wucin gadi (ms) don ƙididdigar masu samarwar OpenRouter da aka ɗauko.                        |

### Haɗin Redis da aka saka ciki

| Sauƙaƙe                     | Tsoho       | Bayani                                                                                |
| --------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_BIND_HOST`           | `127.0.0.1` | Adireshin ɗaurewa na sabis ɗin Redis da aka haɗa a ciki.                              |
| `REDIS_PORT`                | `6379`      | Tashar jiragen ruwa ta sabis ɗin Redis da aka haɗa a ciki.                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_BIND_HOST` | –           | Sauyin da aka keɓance ga OmniRoute don adireshin ɗaurewa na Redis da aka haɗa a ciki. |

## 24. Ƙarin abubuwan sakin v3.8.50

An gabatar da waɗannan saitunan bayan hoton-yanayin kwangilar mahalli na baya.

| Canji                                       | Tsoho                      | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_QUEUE_MS`         | `2000`                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Matsakaicin lokacin jira don samun gurbin karɓar tattaunawa mai nauyi kafin a bayar da `503` da za a iya sake gwadawa; ɗan gajeren jira mai iyaka yana jera cunkoson wakilai a maimakon bayar da `503` nan take. `0` yana dawo da ƙin amincewa nan take.                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_MAX_QUEUED_BYTES` | `4194304` (4 MB)           | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Kasafin bytes da aka jera don jiran karɓawa: yana iyakance jimillar bytes na jikin saƙo da aka adana a buffer a faɗin dukkan process domin jiran kada ya ƙara girman heap (#4380). Jiran da ya wuce kasafin yana karɓar `503` da za a iya sake gwadawa nan take.                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_TTL_MS`             | `60000` (60 s)             | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Tsohon zaɓi marar wani tasiri tun daga #10110: an cire layukan karɓawa na kowane session domin amfani da kasafi guda ɗaya na dukkan process. Ana karɓarsa don dacewar tsarawa; ana yin watsi da shi.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_MAX_SESSIONS`       | `64`                       | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Tsohon zaɓi marar wani tasiri tun daga #10110: an cire layukan karɓawa na kowane session domin amfani da kasafi guda ɗaya na dukkan process. Ana karɓarsa don dacewar tsarawa; ana yin watsi da shi.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_LANES`              | `0` (a kashe)              | `open-sse/services/admission/runtime.ts`                                                 | Layukan karɓawa na kama-da-wane masu daidaitawa a runtime (#9654): babban maɓalli don ƙofar daidaitawa ta kowane tenant (tsari na 2). Ya bambanta da tsofaffin canje-canjen layin kowace haɗi da ke sama (TTL_MS / MAX_SESSIONS, marasa tasiri tun daga #10110). Alamar fasalin dashboard mai suna iri ɗaya; env var yana rinjayar sauyin dashboard; yana buƙatar sake kunnawa. |
| `OMNIROUTE_RUNNOW_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                    | `src/app/api/jobs/[id]/run-now/route.ts`                                                 | Yana iyakance tsawon lokacin da kiran run-now zai jira job da ke gudana kafin fara gudun da aka jera.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`             | a kunne                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Yana sabunta yanayin IMS da haɗarin browser ta hanyar session na Chrome CDP na takamaiman account; saita `0` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`                | a kunne                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Yana adana session na Adobe da aka gyara a ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR`; saita `0` don yanayin da ke cikin memory kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`           | `12000`                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Mafi ƙarancin tazara tsakanin aika buƙatun generate na Adobe Firefly.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`          | `15000`                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Ƙarin lokacin hutu bayan kowace aikawa ta Adobe ta uku da ta yi nasara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`             | `0`                        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`                                          | Yanayin true-headless na gyara kurakurai kawai; Adobe colligo yawanci yana ƙin session na haɗarin da aka samar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CHROME_PATH`                               | ganowa ta atomatik         | `open-sse/executors/cloudflare-playground.ts`, `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts` | Cikakkiyar hanyar Chrome executable ta zaɓi da executors masu amfani da browser ke amfani da ita idan ganowa ta atomatik bisa platform ba ta wadatar ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`                        | _(ba a saita ba)_          | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Token na BotFather wanda ke kunna webhook mai shigowa kuma yake sanya hannu kan `initData` na Mini App.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_          | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Shared secret da aka yi rajista ta hanyar `setWebhook` kuma ake tabbatar da shi da header na `X-Telegram-Bot-Api-Secret-Token` a kowane isarwar webhook. Ana buƙatarsa don hanyar webhook; idan ba a saita shi ba, ana ƙin isarwar webhook da 503.                                                                                                                              |
| `TELEGRAM_DEFAULT_MODEL`                    | `auto/chat`                | `src/lib/telegram/chatProxy.ts`                                                          | Model da ake amfani da shi don amsoshin tattaunawar Telegram.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `TELEGRAM_BOT_API_BASE`                     | `https://api.telegram.org` | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Sauyin tushe URL na Bot API don proxies ko sabobin Bot API da aka ɗauki nauyinsu da kai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `60000`                    | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Iyakar lokaci a milliseconds don kiran Bot API masu fita.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_OPTIONAL_PACK_TAR`               | `1` (a kunne)              | `scripts/build/optionalPackStaging.mjs`                                                  | Saita `0` don tsallake samar da tarballs na `.tar.gz` yayin shirya optional ML/browser packs don standalone tree na Electron (har yanzu ana samar da kundin adireshin pack da `optional-packs.index.json`). Desktop release workflow ne ke amfani da shi don rage girman artifact da ake ɗorawa.                                                                                |
| `GROK_SUBSCRIPTION_IMAGES_ENABLED`          | `false`                    | `open-sse/config/imageRegistry.ts`                                                       | Yana kunna rajistar hanyoyin hotunan xAI subscription; a kashe yake ta tsohuwa domin hanyar hoton API-key ta ci gaba ba tare da canji ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`             | `60000`                    | `open-sse/services/apiKeyRotator.ts`                                                     | Lokacin cooldown na kowane key a milliseconds idan amsar `429` ba ta da alamar `Retry-After` mai amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_ESTIMATOR_CALIBRATION`           | a kunne                    | `open-sse/services/estimatorCalibration.ts`                                              | Saita zuwa `off`, `0`, ko `false` don kashe rikodi da amfani da calibration na estimator a cikin memory.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NEXT_MANUAL_SIG_HANDLE`                    | _(na ciki)_                | `scripts/dev/standalone-server-ws.mjs`                                                   | Sauyin signal-handler na standalone-server na ciki; yawanci launcher ne ke saita shi kuma yake share shi maimakon operators su tsara shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

### ChatGPT Web (Codex)

Saitunan asali na gama-gari don yanayin gudanar da burauza da tunnel ɗin kayan aiki mai fita. Ƙimomin haɗin da aka saita a dashboard suna da fifiko.

| Maɓalli                                 | Tsoho                          | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                                 | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CHROME_PATH`         | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_         | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Takamaiman hanyar Chrome/Chromium don shigarwar npm, systemd da PM2.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CHROME_PATH`                           | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_         | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Madadin gama-gari don takamaiman hanyar Chrome/Chromium.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CDP_URL`             | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Maƙurar CDP ta ciki; Docker yana amfani da sidecar a tashar `9223`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_USE_CDP_PROFILE`     | _(ba a saita ba — a kashe)_    | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/persistent-cdp-context.ts`                   | Saita zuwa `1` (tare da saita maƙurar CDP) don sake amfani da shafin ChatGPT na yanzu da ke cikin bayanin martabar burauza da ake sarrafawa daga waje kuma aka shiga cikinsa, maimakon keɓantaccen mahalli a kowane zagaye; haka kuma yana iyakance shafukan ChatGPT masu aiki lokaci guda zuwa 1. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa. Duba [bayanan martabar CDP mai ɗorewa](../guides/CHATGPT-WEB-PERSISTENT-CDP.md). |
| `CDP_PROXY_TOKEN`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `docker/chatgpt-web-codex-browser/cdp-proxy.mjs`                                | Sirrin da ake rabawa wanda ake buƙata a cikin header na `X-Omni-Cdp-Token` don kowace buƙatar HTTP ta wakilin CDP da haɓakawa zuwa WebSocket. Idan ba a saita shi ba, wakilin zai loda ko samar da token ta amfani da `CDP_PROXY_TOKEN_FILE`; idan babu token mai aiki, zai ƙi kowace buƙata. Samar da shi da `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                        |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_TUNNEL_ID`           | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | ID na ramin OpenAI na gama-gari don zagayen kayan aikin Codex na gida.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_RUNTIME_KEY`         | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Maɓallin lokacin gudanarwa na rami na gama-gari; kada a taɓa saka shi cikin rajistan ayyuka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CONNECTOR_NAME`      | `OmniRoute Codex v2`           | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Ainihin sunan sabon mahaɗin musamman na ChatGPT da aka ƙirƙira don gadar MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_HOME`                | `<DATA_DIR>/chatgpt-web-codex` | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                                   | Kundin adireshi na musamman don yanayin burauza, dillali da rami.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BROWSER_DIAGNOSTICS` | `0`                            | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/adapters/chatgpt-web/browser-diagnostics.ts` | Idan an saita zuwa `1`, yana ɗaukar hotunan binciken matsalar burauza a kowane wurin dubawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_LAUNCHER`            | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                                   | Cikakkiyar hanya ta zaɓi zuwa binary na mai ƙaddamarwa mai ɗorewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`                 | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_         | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                                   | Cikakkiyar hanya ta zaɓi zuwa binary na lokacin gudanarwar Bun.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CODEX_WEB_GPT_BUN`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                                   | Tsohon madadi na `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`; sabbin shigarwa suna amfani da sunan da aka ƙayyade.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

---

## Gadar OmniConductor

Mai karɓar SSE mai dogon rai wanda ke kwaikwayon ayyukan cibiyar OmniConductor zuwa cikin A2A TaskManager na gida (`src/lib/conductor/`). Na zaɓi ne — gadar tana farawa ne kawai idan an saita `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`. Na ɓangaren sabar kawai: dole ne token ɗin cibiyar kada ya taɓa isa burauza.

| Sauyi                          | Tsoho                   | Fayil ɗin Asali                  | Bayani                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------- | -------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`            | _(babu)_                | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | URL na asali na cibiyar OmniConductor (misali `http://127.0.0.1:7910`). Idan ba a saita ba = an kashe gadar.                        |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN`          | _(babu)_                | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Shaidar cibiyar don rafin SSE — ƙirƙiri takwarar nau'in `spokesperson` a cibiyar (`POST /v1/peers`, mai gudanarwa).                 |
| `CONDUCTOR_ORCHESTRATOR_TOKEN` | _(babu)_                | `src/lib/conductor/hubProxy.ts`  | Shaida don wakilta ayyuka masu shigowa daga A2A→cibiya (`POST /v1/tasks`); idan ba a saita ba, ana amfani da `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN`. |
| `CONDUCTOR_SPOKESPERSON_URL`   | `http://127.0.0.1:7920` | `src/lib/conductor/faroProxy.ts` | URL na asali na sabis ɗin mai magana da yawun jama'a (Faro) da ke bayan wakilin tattaunawar dashboard (`/api/conductor/ask`).       |

### Tsarawa bisa la'akari da ƙayyadadden kaso

Ana amfani da shi a `open-sse/services/combo.ts` da `src/lib/quota/quotaScheduler.ts` don binciken kasafin token kafin buƙata. Na zaɓi ne — halayen turawa na asali ba ya canzawa idan ba a saita ba.

| Sauyi                              | Tsoho     | Fayil ɗin Asali                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ---------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_AWARE_ROUTING`    | `0`       | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                | Idan `1`, a tsallake haɗin da kasafin token ɗinsa na kowane taga (`rateLimitOverrides.tpm`, teburin `provider_quota_state`) ba zai iya ɗaukar kiyasin kuɗin buƙatar ba kafin aikawa. A ci gaba idan babu kasafin da aka saita.                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_ESTIMATOR_CALIBRATION`  | _(kunne)_ | `open-sse/services/estimatorCalibration.ts` | Daidaitawar mai ƙididdigar token na kariyar mahalli ta hanyar martani (#14931): EMA na `usage.prompt_tokens` / kiyasi ga kowane (mai bayarwa, samfuri, akwai kayan aiki), an iyakance shi zuwa 0.15–2.0, kuma a cikin ƙwaƙwalwa kawai. `off`, `0` ko `false` suna kashe rikodi da amfani. |
| `GROK_SUBSCRIPTION_IMAGES_ENABLED` | `false`   | `open-sse/config/imageRegistry.ts`          | Yana rijistar hanyoyin hoto na `xai-oauth` (`xao`) da `grok-cli`. Tutar fasali ta dashboard mai suna iri ɗaya; ƙimar maye gurbi ta DB tana rinjayar wannan ƙimar.                                                                                                                         |
